\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}:=\sum_{l=1}^{2}\left(a_{i,l}Z_{-1,l}+p_{i,l}Z_{2,l}\right)
represents the linear part,
\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}
denotes the higher order terms.

For any given
\displaystyle i\neq j
, we now focus on the terms involving
\displaystyle P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)}}
and
\displaystyle P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{\mu_{j},\xi^{(j)}}
in (
3.17
). For convenience, we denote
\displaystyle P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)}}
and
\displaystyle P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{\mu_{j},\xi^{(j)}}
as
\displaystyle P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{i}
and
\displaystyle P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{j}
, respectively.
There holds

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}v\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{i}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{j}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{j}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{i}\right)_{y}\right]+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}v\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{i}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(g^{(j)}\right)_{y}+\left(g^{(j)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{i}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}v\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{j}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(g^{(i)}\right)_{y}+\left(g^{(i)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{j}\right)_{y}\right]+2\varepsilon^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}v\cdot\left[\left(g^{(i)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(g^{(j)}\right)_{y}+\left(g^{(j)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(g^{(i)}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=2\int_{\mathcal{D}}v\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{i}+\varepsilon g^{(i)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{j}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{j}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{i}+\varepsilon g^{(i)}\right)_{y}\right].

Note that the boundary function
\displaystyle g^{(i)}(z,\omega^{(i)})=\left(g^{(i)}_{1}(z,\omega^{(i)}),g^{(i)}_{2}(z,\omega^{(i)}),g^{(i)}_{3}(z,\omega^{(i)})\right)
is defined as follows

g^{(i)}_{1}(z,\omega^{(i)})=2h_{1}^{(1)}(z,\omega^{(i)}),\quad g^{(i)}_{2}(z,\omega^{(i)})=2h_{2}^{(1)}(z,\omega^{(i)}),\quad g^{(i)}_{3}(z,\omega^{(i)})=-2\varepsilon h_{3}^{(1)}(z,\omega^{(i)}),

where
\displaystyle h_{1}^{(1)}(z,\omega^{(i)}),~h_{2}^{(1)}(z,\omega^{(i)}),~h_{3}^{(1)}(z,\omega^{(i)})
are the functions given in (
2.5
).

Let
\displaystyle\gamma=\frac{d}{2}
and divide the integral domain into three regions
\displaystyle B_{i}:=B_{\gamma}(\xi^{(i)})
,
\displaystyle B_{j}:=B_{\gamma}(\xi^{(j)})
and
\displaystyle\mathcal{D}\setminus\left(B_{i}\cup B_{j}\right)
. From the definition of
\displaystyle Z
, see (
3.1
), it is clear that
\displaystyle B_{i}
and
\displaystyle B_{j}
are disjoint. Using (
2.9
) and Hölder’s inequality, along with the fact that

\left\|\nabla\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{i}+\varepsilon g^{(i)}\right)\right\|_{L^{p}(B_{i})}=O\left(\mu_{i}^{\frac{2-p}{p}}\right),\quad\left\|\nabla\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{j}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}(B_{i})}=O\left(\mu_{j}^{2}\right),

for
\displaystyle p=\frac{4}{4-\alpha}
, where
\displaystyle\alpha
is the constant in (
2.7
), it is straightforward to verify that

\displaystyle\left|\int_{B_{i}}v\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{i}+\varepsilon g^{(i)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{j}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{j}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{i}+\varepsilon g^{(i)}\right)_{y}\right]\right|

\displaystyle=O\left(\mu_{j}^{2}\right)\left\|\nabla\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{i}+\varepsilon g^{(i)}\right)\right\|_{L^{p}(B_{i})}\|v\|_{L^{q}(B_{i})}

\displaystyle=O\left(\mu_{i}^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\mu_{j}^{2}\right)\|v\|_{H_{0}^{1}(\mathcal{D})},

where
\displaystyle\frac{1}{p}+\frac{1}{q}=1
. Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\left|\int_{B_{j}}v\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{i}+\varepsilon g^{(i)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{j}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{j}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{i}+\varepsilon g^{(i)}\right)_{y}\right]\right|

\displaystyle=O\left(\mu_{i}^{2}\mu_{j}^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\right)\|v\|_{H_{0}^{1}(\mathcal{D})}.

And since
\displaystyle\left\|\nabla\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{j}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{D}\backslash\left(B_{i}\cup B_{j}\right))}=O\left(\mu_{j}^{2}+(\mu_{j}^{2}-\varepsilon)d^{-3}\right)
, there holds

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathcal{D}\setminus\left(B_{i}\cup B_{j}\right)}v\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{i}+\varepsilon g^{(i)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{j}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{j}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{i}+\varepsilon g^{(i)}\right)_{y}\right]\right|

\displaystyle=O\left(\left(\mu_{i}^{2}+(\mu_{i}^{2}-\varepsilon)d^{-3}\right)\left(\mu_{j}^{2}+(\mu_{j}^{2}-\varepsilon)d^{-3}\right)\right)\|v\|_{H_{0}^{1}(\mathcal{D})}.

Thus, we conclude that

(3.18)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathcal{D}}v\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{i}+\varepsilon g^{(i)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{j}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{j}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{i}+\varepsilon g^{(i)}\right)_{y}\right]\right|

\displaystyle=O\left(\mu_{i}^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\mu_{j}^{2}+\mu_{i}^{2}\mu_{j}^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\right)\|v\|_{H_{0}^{1}(\mathcal{D})}.

In (
3.17
), for given
\displaystyle i\neq j
, we also need to consider terms of the following form.

(3.19)

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}v\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}+P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)_{y}+\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}+P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}+P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}\right)\cdot\left[v_{x}\wedge\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)_{y}+\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)_{x}\wedge v_{y}\right].

Since

(3.20)

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}+P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}\right)\right\|_{L^{p}(B_{i})}=O\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{i,l}\mu_{i}d^{-2}+p_{i,l}\mu_{i}^{4}d^{-5}\right),\quad\left\|\nabla\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}(B_{i})}=O(\mu_{j}^{2}),

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}+P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}(B_{j})}=O\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{i,l}\mu_{i}+p_{i,l}\mu_{i}^{4}\right),

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)\right\|_{L^{p}(B_{j})}=O\left(\mu_{j}^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}+\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{j,l}\mu_{j}d^{-2}+p_{j,l}\mu_{j}^{4}d^{-5}\right),

following a similar approach as in the estimate of (
3.18
), we can estimate (
3.19
) as

(3.21)

\displaystyle O\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{i,l}\mu_{i}\mu_{j}^{2}d^{-2}+p_{i,l}\mu_{i}^{4}\mu_{j}^{2}d^{-5}+\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{i,l}\mu_{i}\mu_{j}^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}+p_{i,l}\mu_{i}^{4}\mu_{j}^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\right).

For the remaining terms in (
3.15
) and (
3.16
), we have

(3.22)

\displaystyle\left|2\int_{\mathcal{D}}v\cdot\left[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\varphi_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\varphi_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)_{y}\right]\right.

\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}v\cdot\left[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\varphi_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)_{y}+\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\varphi_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\left.+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}v\cdot\left[\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(g^{(i)}\right)_{y}+\left(g^{(i)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}\right)_{y}\right]+2\varepsilon^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}v\cdot\left[\left(g^{(i)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(g^{(i)}\right)_{y}\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\left|2\int_{\mathcal{D}}v\cdot\left[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\varphi_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}-\varepsilon g^{(i)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\varphi_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}-\varepsilon g^{(i)}\right)_{y}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}v\cdot\left[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\varphi_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}-\varepsilon g^{(i)}\right)_{y}+\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\varphi_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}-\varepsilon g^{(i)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)_{y}\right]\right|.

Since

(3.23)

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\varphi_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}-\varepsilon g^{(i)}\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{D})}=O\left((\mu_{i}^{2}-\varepsilon)d^{-3}+\sum_{l=1}^{2}\left(|a_{i,l}|\mu_{i}d^{-2}+|p_{i,l}|\mu_{i}^{4}d^{-5}\right)\right),

\displaystyle\|\nabla\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)\|_{L^{p}(B_{i})}=O\left(\mu_{i}^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}+\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{i,l}\mu_{i}^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}+p_{i,l}\mu_{i}^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\right),

we can estimate (
3.22
) as

(3.24)

\displaystyle O\left(\mu_{i}^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\left((\mu_{i}^{2}-\varepsilon)d^{-3}+\sum_{l=1}^{2}\left(|a_{i,l}|\mu_{i}d^{-2}+|p_{i,l}|\mu_{i}^{4}d^{-5}\right)\right)\right)\|v\|_{H_{0}^{1}(\mathcal{D})}.

Combining (
3.18
), (
3.21
), (
3.24
), we obtain the conclusion.
∎

Lemma 3.5
.

Let
\displaystyle Z
be as in (
3.1
). Then for
\displaystyle z_{\mathcal{A}}\in Z
,
\displaystyle l=1,2
, we have the following estimates

\displaystyle\left\|I_{\varepsilon}^{\prime\prime}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\left[\frac{\partial z_{\mathcal{A}}}{\partial\xi_{l}^{(i)}}\right]\right\|=O\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mu_{i}^{3-\frac{\alpha}{2}}d^{-4}\right),\qquad\left\|I_{\varepsilon}^{\prime\prime}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\left[\frac{\partial z_{\mathcal{A}}}{\partial\mathcal{Q}_{i}}\right]\right\|=O\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mu_{i}^{3-\frac{\alpha}{2}}d^{-3}\right),

\displaystyle\left\|I_{\varepsilon}^{\prime\prime}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\left[\frac{\partial z_{\mathcal{A}}}{\partial\mu_{i}}\right]\right\|=O\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mu_{i}^{2-\frac{\alpha}{2}}d^{-3}\right),\qquad\left\|I_{\varepsilon}^{\prime\prime}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\left[\frac{\partial z_{\mathcal{A}}}{\partial a_{i,l}}\right]\right\|=O\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mu_{i}^{2-\frac{\alpha}{2}}d^{-2}\right),

\displaystyle\left\|I_{\varepsilon}^{\prime\prime}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\left[\frac{\partial z_{\mathcal{A}}}{\partial p_{i,l}}\right]\right\|=O\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mu_{i}^{5-\frac{\alpha}{2}}d^{-5}\right).

Proof.

The functional
\displaystyle I_{\varepsilon}^{\prime\prime}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)
is defined as follows,

I_{\varepsilon}^{\prime\prime}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)[v,\tilde{v}]=\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla v\cdot\nabla\tilde{v}+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\tilde{v}\cdot\left(\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge v_{y}+v_{x}\wedge\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}\right)+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}\tilde{v}\cdot(g_{x}\wedge v_{y}+v_{x}\wedge g_{y}),\quad\forall\ v,\tilde{v}\in H_{0}^{1}(\mathcal{D},\mathbb{R}^{3}).

Now, we choose
\displaystyle\tilde{v}=\frac{\partial z_{\mathcal{A}}}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}
and let
\displaystyle v\in H_{0}^{1}(\mathcal{D},\mathbb{R}^{3})
be an arbitrary test function. Clearly, we have

\frac{\partial z_{\mathcal{A}}}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}=\frac{\partial\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}-\frac{\partial\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\varphi_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}},

where
\displaystyle\varphi_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}
is defined in (
2.3
) corresponding to
\displaystyle\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}
. We have

(3.25)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla v\cdot\nabla\frac{\partial z_{\mathcal{A}}}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\frac{\partial z_{\mathcal{A}}}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\cdot\left(\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge v_{y}+v_{x}\wedge\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}\right)+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}\frac{\partial z_{\mathcal{A}}}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\cdot(g_{x}\wedge v_{y}+v_{x}\wedge g_{y})

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla v\cdot\nabla\left(\frac{\partial\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right)

\displaystyle+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\frac{\partial\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\cdot\left[\Big(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\Big)_{x}\wedge v_{y}+v_{x}\wedge\Big(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\Big)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+2\sum_{j\neq i}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\frac{\partial\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\cdot\left[\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)_{x}\wedge v_{y}+v_{x}\wedge\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla v\cdot\nabla\frac{\partial\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\varphi_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\frac{\partial\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\varphi_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\cdot\left[\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}+\varepsilon g\right)_{x}\wedge v_{y}+v_{x}\wedge\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}+\varepsilon g\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\frac{\partial\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\cdot\left[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\varphi_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}-\varepsilon g^{(i)}\right)_{x}\wedge v_{y}+v_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\varphi_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}-\varepsilon g^{(i)}\right)_{y}\right].

Since
\displaystyle\Delta(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}})=2(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}})_{x}\wedge(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}})_{y},

integrating by parts, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla v\cdot\nabla\left(\frac{\partial\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right)

\displaystyle+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\frac{\partial\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\cdot\left[\Big(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\Big)_{x}\wedge v_{y}+v_{x}\wedge\Big(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\Big)_{y}\right]=0.

For the third term in (
3.25
), let
\displaystyle B_{i}
and
\displaystyle B_{j}
denote the balls of radius
\displaystyle\frac{d}{2}
centered at
\displaystyle\xi^{(i)}
and
\displaystyle\xi^{(j)}
, respectively. By (
2.12
), we obtain

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Bigg|2\sum_{j\neq i}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\frac{\partial\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\cdot\left[\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)_{x}\wedge v_{y}+v_{x}\wedge\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)_{y}\right]\Bigg|

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\Bigg|2\sum_{j\neq i}\left(\int_{B_{i}}+\int_{B_{j}}+\int_{\mathcal{D}\backslash\left(B_{i}\cup B_{j}\right)}\right)\frac{\partial\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\cdot\left[\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)_{x}\wedge v_{y}+v_{x}\wedge\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)_{y}\right]\Bigg|

\displaystyle\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{j\neq i}\left\|\frac{\partial\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right\|_{L^{2}(B_{i})}\left\|\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)_{x}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(B_{i})}\|v\|_{H_{0}^{1}(\mathcal{D})}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j\neq i}\left\|\frac{\partial\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(B_{j})}\left\|\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)_{x}\right\|_{L^{2}(B_{j})}\|v\|_{H_{0}^{1}(\mathcal{D})}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j\neq i}\left\|\frac{\partial\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{D}\backslash\left(B_{i}\cup B_{j}\right))}\left\|\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}+\varepsilon g^{(j)}\right)_{x}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{D}\backslash\left(B_{i}\cup B_{j}\right))}\|v\|_{H_{0}^{1}(\mathcal{D})}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=O\left(\sum_{j\neq i}\mu_{j}^{2}+\mu_{i}^{2}+\mu_{i}^{2}\mu_{j}^{2}\right)\|v\|_{H_{0}^{1}(\mathcal{D})}.

We proceed to estimate the terms in (
3.25
) involving
\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{i}\varphi_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}
. Since

\Delta(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\varphi_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}})=0\quad\text{in}~\mathcal{D},

we apply integration by parts, which gives

\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla v\cdot\nabla\frac{\partial(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\varphi_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}})}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}=0.

Furthermore, by (
3.20
) and

\left\|\nabla\frac{\partial(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\varphi_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}})}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{D})}=O(\mu_{i}^{2}d^{-4}),

we get

\displaystyle\left|2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\frac{\partial(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\varphi_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}})}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\cdot\left[\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}+\varepsilon g\right)_{x}\wedge v_{y}+v_{x}\wedge\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}+\varepsilon g\right)_{y}\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\left|2\int_{\mathcal{D}}v\cdot\left[\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}+\varepsilon g\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\frac{\partial(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\varphi_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}})}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right)_{y}+\left(\frac{\partial(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\varphi_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}})}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}+\varepsilon g\right)_{y}\right]\right|

\displaystyle=O\left(\sum_{j\neq i}\mu_{i}^{3-\frac{\alpha}{2}}d^{-4}+\mu_{i}^{2}\mu_{j}^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}}+\mu_{i}^{2}d^{-4}\mu_{j}^{2}\right)\|v\|_{H_{0}^{1}(\mathcal{D})}.

Additionally, through (
3.23
) and the boundedness of
\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{D})}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left|2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\frac{\partial\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\cdot\left[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\varphi_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}-\varepsilon g^{(i)}\right)_{x}\wedge v_{y}+v_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{i}\varphi_{\mu_{i},\xi^{(i)},a_{i},p_{i}}-\varepsilon g^{(i)}\right)_{y}\right]\right|

\displaystyle=O\left((\mu_{i}^{2}-\varepsilon)d^{-3}+\sum_{l=1}^{2}\left(|a_{i,l}|\mu_{i}d^{-2}+|p_{i,l}|\mu_{i}^{4}d^{-5}\right)\right)\|v\|_{H_{0}^{1}(\mathcal{D})}.

Combine the above estimates, and recall that for all
\displaystyle i=1,\dots,k
,
\displaystyle\frac{\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\mu_{i}},\ \frac{\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}d^{2}}{|a_{i}|},\ \frac{\varepsilon^{-1}d^{5}}{|p_{i}|}\in\left[\bar{C}^{-1},\bar{C}\right]
, we conclude that

\left\|I_{\varepsilon}^{\prime\prime}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\left[\frac{\partial z_{\mathcal{A}}}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right]\right\|=O\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mu_{i}^{3-\frac{\alpha}{2}}d^{-4}\right).

From similar arguments, we can obtain the results of this lemma.
∎

Proposition 3.6
.

Let
\displaystyle w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)
be as in Proposition
3.3
, then

(3.26)

\left|I_{\varepsilon}(z_{\mathcal{A}}+w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right))-I_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right|\lesssim e^{2}(\varepsilon),\quad\forall z_{\mathcal{A}}\in Z,

where
\displaystyle e(\varepsilon)
is given in Lemma
3.4
. Moreover, for any
\displaystyle z_{\mathcal{A}}\in Z
,
\displaystyle l=1,2
, there holds

(3.27)

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\partial I_{\varepsilon}(z_{\mathcal{A}}+w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right))}{\partial\xi_{l}^{(i)}}-\frac{\partial I_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{l}^{(i)}}\right|=O\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mu_{i}^{3-\frac{\alpha}{2}}d^{-4}\right)e(\varepsilon)+\frac{e^{2}(\varepsilon)}{\mu_{i}},

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\partial I_{\varepsilon}(z_{\mathcal{A}}+w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right))}{\partial\mathcal{Q}_{i}}-\frac{\partial I_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)}{\partial\mathcal{Q}_{i}}\right|=O\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mu_{i}^{3-\frac{\alpha}{2}}d^{-3}\right)e(\varepsilon)+e^{2}(\varepsilon),

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\partial I_{\varepsilon}(z_{\mathcal{A}}+w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right))}{\partial\mu_{i}}-\frac{\partial I_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)}{\partial\mu_{i}}\right|=O\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mu_{i}^{2-\frac{\alpha}{2}}d^{-3}\right)e(\varepsilon)+\frac{e^{2}(\varepsilon)}{\mu_{i}},

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\partial I_{\varepsilon}(z_{\mathcal{A}}+w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right))}{\partial a_{i,l}}-\frac{\partial I_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)}{\partial a_{i,l}}\right|=O\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mu_{i}^{2-\frac{\alpha}{2}}d^{-2}\right)e(\varepsilon)+e^{2}(\varepsilon),

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\partial I_{\varepsilon}(z_{\mathcal{A}}+w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right))}{\partial p_{i,l}}-\frac{\partial I_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)}{\partial p_{i,l}}\right|=O\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mu_{i}^{5-\frac{\alpha}{2}}d^{-5}\right)e(\varepsilon)+e^{2}(\varepsilon).

Proof.

From the mean value theorem, we have

(3.28)

\displaystyle I_{\varepsilon}(z_{\mathcal{A}}+w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right))-I_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{1}I^{\prime}_{\varepsilon}(z_{\mathcal{A}}+sw_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right))[w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)]ds

\displaystyle=I^{\prime}_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)[w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)]+\int_{0}^{1}\left(I^{\prime}_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}+sw_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right)-I^{\prime}_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right)[w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)]ds.

Since the functional
\displaystyle I^{\prime\prime}_{\varepsilon}
is locally bounded, we have

\displaystyle\left|I^{\prime}_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}+sw_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right)-I^{\prime}_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\int_{0}^{1}I^{\prime\prime}_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}+tsw_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right)[w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)]dt\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{t,s\in[0,1]}\|I^{\prime\prime}_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}+tsw_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right)\|\|w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\|.

In the sequel, we abbreviate
\displaystyle\|\cdot\|_{H_{0}^{1}(\mathcal{D})}
as
\displaystyle\|\cdot\|
. By Proposition
3.3
and Lemma
3.4
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left|I_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}+w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right)-I_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right|\lesssim\|I^{\prime}_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\|\|w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\|+\|w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\|^{2}\lesssim e^{2}(\varepsilon).

Thus (
3.26
) holds.

Next, we prove (
3.27
). Differentiating the equation
\displaystyle F_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}},w_{\varepsilon},\beta\right)
with respect to
\displaystyle\xi_{1}^{(i)}
, which is defined in (
3.10
), we obtain

0=\frac{\partial F_{\varepsilon}}{\partial z_{\mathcal{A}}}\left[\frac{\partial z_{\mathcal{A}}}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right]+\frac{\partial F_{\varepsilon}}{\partial(w_{\varepsilon},\beta)}\frac{\partial(w_{\varepsilon},\beta)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}=I^{\prime\prime}_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}+w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right)\left[\frac{\partial z_{\mathcal{A}}}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right]+\frac{\partial F_{\varepsilon}}{\partial(w_{\varepsilon},\beta)}\frac{\partial(w_{\varepsilon},\beta)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}.

From Lemma
3.2
,
\displaystyle\frac{\partial F_{\varepsilon}}{\partial(w_{\varepsilon},\beta)}
is uniformly invertible. Combine the fact that
\displaystyle I^{\prime\prime}_{\varepsilon}
is locally Lipschitz, we have

\frac{\partial(w_{\varepsilon},\beta)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}=-\left(\frac{\partial F_{\varepsilon}}{\partial(w_{\varepsilon},\beta)}\right)^{-1}\left[I^{\prime\prime}_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}+w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right)\left[\frac{\partial z_{\mathcal{A}}}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right]\right],

and then

(3.29)

\left\|\frac{\partial(w_{\varepsilon},\beta)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right\|\lesssim\left\|I^{\prime\prime}_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}+w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right)\left[\frac{\partial z_{\mathcal{A}}}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right]\right\|\lesssim\left(\left\|I^{\prime\prime}_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\left[\frac{\partial z_{\mathcal{A}}}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right]\right\|+\left\|w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial z_{\mathcal{A}}}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right\|\right).

By (
3.28
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{\partial I_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}+w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}-\frac{\partial I_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}

\displaystyle=I^{\prime\prime}_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\left[\frac{\partial z_{\mathcal{A}}}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}},w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right]+I^{\prime}_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\left[\frac{\partial w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right]

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\left(I^{\prime\prime}_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}+sw_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right)-I^{\prime\prime}_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right)\left[\frac{\partial z_{\mathcal{A}}}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}},w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right]ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}s\left(I^{\prime\prime}_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}+sw_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right)-I^{\prime\prime}_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right)\left[\frac{\partial w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}},w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right]ds+\int_{0}^{1}sI^{\prime\prime}_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\left[\frac{\partial w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}},w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right]ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{1}\left(I^{\prime}_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}+sw_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right)-I^{\prime}_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right)\left[\frac{\partial w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right]ds.

Thus

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\partial I_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}+w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}-\frac{\partial I_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|I^{\prime\prime}_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\left[\frac{\partial z_{\mathcal{A}}}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right]\right\|\|w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\|+\left\|I^{\prime}_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right\|

\displaystyle+\|w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\|\left\|\frac{\partial w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right\|+\|w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\|^{2}\left(\left\|\frac{\partial z_{\mathcal{A}}}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right\|+\left\|\frac{\partial w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right\|\right).

From Lemma
3.4
, (
3.29
), Lemma
3.5
, Proposition
3.3
and the fact that
\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial z_{\mathcal{A}}}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right\|=O\left(\frac{1}{\mu_{i}}\right)
, we obtain

\left|\frac{\partial I_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}+w_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}-\frac{\partial I_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)}{\partial\xi_{1}^{(i)}}\right|\lesssim\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mu_{i}^{3-\frac{\alpha}{2}}d^{-4}\right)e(\varepsilon)+\frac{e^{2}(\varepsilon)}{\mu_{i}}.

The remaining parts of (
3.27
) can be proved in a similar way.
∎

4.
Energy expansion for one bubble

In this section, we evaluate the expansion of
\displaystyle I_{\varepsilon}\left(z_{\mathcal{A}}\right)
for sufficiently small
\displaystyle\varepsilon
and
\displaystyle z_{\mathcal{A}}\in Z
in the case where
\displaystyle k=1
. To simplify the notation, we denote
\displaystyle\mu_{1},\ \xi^{(1)},\ \mathcal{Q}_{1},\ a_{1,1},\ a_{1,2},\ p_{1,1},\ p_{1,2}
as

\displaystyle\mu,\ \xi,\ \mathcal{Q},\ a_{1},\ a_{2},\ p_{1},\ p_{2}
.

For a given boundary condition
\displaystyle\tilde{g}
and its harmonic extension
\displaystyle g
on
\displaystyle\mathcal{D}
, we aim to calculate the expansion of the functional
\displaystyle I_{\varepsilon}\left(P\mathcal{Q}\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\right)
as a function of
\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}\in SO(3)
,
\displaystyle\mu
,
\displaystyle\xi
,
\displaystyle a_{l}
,
\displaystyle p_{l}
,
\displaystyle l=1,2
and
\displaystyle\varepsilon
. For a fixed point
\displaystyle\omega\in\mathcal{D}
to be chosen, we define the boundary function
\displaystyle g(z,\omega)=\left(g_{1}(z,\omega),g_{2}(z,\omega),g_{3}(z,\omega)\right)
as follows

g_{1}(z,\omega)=2h_{1}^{(1)}(z,\omega),\quad g_{2}(z,\omega)=2h_{2}^{(1)}(z,\omega),\quad g_{3}(z,\omega)=-2\varepsilon h_{3}^{(1)}(z,\omega),

where
\displaystyle\left(h_{1}^{(1)}(z,\omega),h_{2}^{(1)}(z,\omega),h_{3}^{(1)}(z,\omega)\right)
are the functions given in (
2.5
). The main result of this section is the following proposition.

Proposition 4.1
.

Let
\displaystyle\mu,\ \xi,\ \mathcal{Q},\ a_{1},\ a_{2},\ p_{1},\ p_{2}
be such that
\displaystyle P\mathcal{Q}\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\in Z
, and
\displaystyle d=d_{\varepsilon}=1-|\xi|^{2}
.
Then there holds

I_{\varepsilon}\left(P\mathcal{Q}\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\right)=\frac{8}{3}\pi+F_{\mathcal{D},g}(\mu,\xi,\mathcal{Q},a,p)+\tilde{e}^{(1)}(\varepsilon,\mu,a,p)+\tilde{e}^{(2)}(\varepsilon,\mu,a,p),

where

\displaystyle\displaystyle F_{\mathcal{D},g}(\mu,\xi,\mathcal{Q},a,p)

\displaystyle\displaystyle:=8\pi\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\xi)-4\pi\varepsilon\mu^{2}\left[\frac{\partial^{2}\left(\mathcal{Q}^{-1}g\right)_{1}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\omega)+\frac{\partial^{2}\left(\mathcal{Q}^{-1}g\right)_{2}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\omega)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-16\pi a_{1}^{2}\mu^{2}\frac{\partial h_{1}^{(-1,1)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)-16\pi a_{2}^{2}\mu^{2}\frac{\partial h_{2}^{(-1,2)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{4\pi}{3}p_{1}^{2}\mu^{8}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,1)}_{1}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{4\pi}{3}p_{2}^{2}\mu^{8}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,2)}_{2}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-32\pi p_{1}a_{1}\mu^{5}\frac{\partial h_{1}^{(2,1)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)-32\pi p_{1}a_{2}\mu^{5}\frac{\partial h_{2}^{(2,1)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)-32\pi p_{2}a_{1}\mu^{5}\frac{\partial h_{1}^{(2,2)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)-32\pi p_{2}a_{2}\mu^{5}\frac{\partial h_{2}^{(2,2)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi),

\displaystyle\tilde{e}^{(1)}(\mu,a,p):=

\displaystyle O\left(\mu^{8}d^{-10}+\varepsilon\mu^{4}d^{-6}+\varepsilon^{3}\mu^{2}|\log\mu|+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{2}+\varepsilon^{3}\mu^{2}d^{-2}\right)

\displaystyle+O\left(\left(a_{1}^{2}+a_{2}^{2}\right)\mu^{4}d^{-4}\right)+O\left((p_{1}^{2}+p_{2}^{2})\mu^{10}d^{-10}\right)+O\left((a_{1}+a_{2})(p_{1}+p_{2})\mu^{7}d^{-7}\right),

and

(4.1)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\tilde{e}^{(2)}(\varepsilon,\mu,a,p)

\displaystyle\displaystyle:=O\left(\left(p_{1}+p_{2}\right)(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\left(\mu^{4}d^{-6}+(\mu^{8}+\varepsilon\mu^{6}+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{4})d^{-12}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle~\quad+O\left((a_{1}+a_{2})(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\left(\mu d^{-3}+\left(\mu^{3}\left(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon\right)+\varepsilon^{2}\mu\right)d^{-9}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle~\quad+O\left(p_{1}p_{2}\mu^{10}d^{-10}+\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}p_{k}p_{l}(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\left(\left((\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)\right)\left(\mu^{6}d^{-12}+\mu^{2}d^{-6}\right)+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{6}d^{-14}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle~\quad+O\left(a_{1}a_{2}\mu^{4}d^{-4}+\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{k}a_{l}\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\left(\mu^{2}(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)d^{-8}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle~\quad+O\left(\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{k}p_{l}(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\left(\mu^{3}d^{-5}+(\mu^{5}(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)++\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{3})d^{-11}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle~\quad+O\left(\sum_{k,l,m=1}^{2}a_{k}a_{l}a_{m}\left(\mu^{5}d^{-7}+\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)d^{-7}\right)+a_{k}a_{l}p_{m}\left(\mu^{2}d^{-2}+\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)d^{-7}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle~\quad+O\left(\sum_{k,l,m=1}^{2}a_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\left(\mu^{5}d^{-5}+\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)d^{-7}\right)+p_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\left(\mu^{6}d^{-6}+\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)d^{-7}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle~\quad+O\left(\left(a_{1}+a_{2}+p_{1}+p_{2}\right)^{4}\left(\mu d^{-1}+(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\mu^{2}d^{-6}\right)\right)

(4.2)

\displaystyle\displaystyle~\quad+O\left(\left(a_{1}+a_{2}+p_{1}+p_{2}\right)^{3}\left(a_{1}+a_{2}\right)\mu^{4}d^{-6}\right)+O\left(\left(a_{1}+a_{2}+p_{1}+p_{2}\right)^{5}\right).

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{\partial I_{\varepsilon}\left(P\mathcal{Q}\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\right)}{\partial\mu}=\frac{\partial F_{\mathcal{D},g}}{\partial\mu}+\frac{1}{\mu}\tilde{e}^{(1)}(\mu,a,p)+\frac{1}{\mu}\tilde{e}^{(2)}(\varepsilon,\mu,a,p),

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{\partial I_{\varepsilon}\left(P\mathcal{Q}\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\right)}{\partial\xi_{l}}=\frac{\partial F_{\mathcal{D},g}}{\partial\xi_{l}}+\frac{{\partial}\tilde{e}^{(1)}(\mu,a,p)}{{\partial}\xi_{l}}+\frac{{\partial}\tilde{e}^{(2)}(\varepsilon,\mu,a,p)}{{\partial}\xi_{l}},\quad l=1,2,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{\partial I_{\varepsilon}\left(P\mathcal{Q}\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\right)}{\partial\mathcal{Q}}=\frac{\partial F_{\mathcal{D},g}}{\partial\mathcal{Q}}+\tilde{e}^{(1)}(\mu,a,p)+\tilde{e}^{(2)}(\varepsilon,\mu,a,p),

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{\partial I_{\varepsilon}\left(P\mathcal{Q}\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\right)}{\partial a_{l}}=\frac{\partial F_{\mathcal{D},g}}{\partial a_{l}}+\frac{{\partial}\tilde{e}^{(1)}(\mu,a,p)}{{\partial}a_{l}}+\frac{{\partial}\tilde{e}^{(2)}(\varepsilon,\mu,a,p)}{{\partial}a_{l}},\quad l=1,2,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{\partial I_{\varepsilon}\left(P\mathcal{Q}\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\right)}{\partial p_{l}}=\frac{\partial F_{\mathcal{D},g}}{\partial p_{l}}+\frac{{\partial}\tilde{e}^{(1)}(\mu,a,p)}{{\partial}p_{l}}+\frac{{\partial}\tilde{e}^{(2)}(\varepsilon,\mu,a,p)}{{\partial}p_{l}},\quad l=1,2.

Proof.

Assume
\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}=Id
, and let
\displaystyle\varphi_{\mu,\xi,a,p}
be the solution of (
2.3
). Then
\displaystyle P\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}=\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}-\varphi_{\mu,\xi,a,p}
and we have

(4.3)

\displaystyle I_{\varepsilon}\left(P\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\right)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left|\nabla P\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\right|^{2}+\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\cdot\left[(P\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p})_{x}\wedge(P\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p})_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\right)\cdot\left[(P\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p})_{x}\wedge g_{y}+g_{x}\wedge(P\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p})_{y}\right]+2\varepsilon^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\right)\cdot(g_{x}\wedge g_{y}).

Write
\displaystyle\delta_{\mu,\xi,0,0}
as
\displaystyle\delta
, recall that we have the following expansion

\displaystyle\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}:=\delta+\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}},

where
\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}:=\sum_{l=1}^{2}\left(a_{l}Z_{-1,l}+p_{l}Z_{2,l}\right)
is the linear part,
\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}
denotes the higher order terms.

Now, we highlight some evident relations between the terms in (
4.3
) that arise through integration by parts. Firstly, for the following terms in (
4.3
), there holds

(4.4)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\nabla\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)+\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\delta\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\delta\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\Delta\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)+\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\left[(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}L_{\delta}\left[P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right]\cdot\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{1}\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}\right],

where
\displaystyle L_{\delta}[\phi]:=\Delta\phi-2\delta_{x}\wedge\phi_{y}-2\phi_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}
.

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\nabla\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)+\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\delta\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\delta\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\nabla\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}\right].

Secondly,

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla P\delta\cdot\nabla\left(P(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}})\right)+\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}))\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\delta\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\delta\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta\cdot\left[\left(P\delta\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}))\right)_{y}+\left(P(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}))\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\delta\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}})\right)\cdot(\delta_{x}\wedge\delta_{y})+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}})\right)\cdot\left[(P\delta)_{x}\wedge(P\delta)_{y})\right]

\displaystyle=-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}))\right)\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}+(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}-(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{1}\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge\left(P(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}})\right)_{y}+\left(P(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}})\right)_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}})\right)\cdot\left[(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}\right].

Moreover,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\nabla\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)+\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\delta\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\delta\right)_{y}\right]+\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\delta\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\delta\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}+\left(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\delta\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\delta\right)_{y}\right].

Integrating by parts again, we derive

(4.5)

\displaystyle\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\right)\cdot\left[(P\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p})_{x}\wedge g_{y}+g_{x}\wedge(P\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p})_{y}\right]=2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}g\cdot\left[\left(P\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\right)_{y}\right].

For the sake of clarity and brevity, we expand the terms in (
4.3
), but only list some typical terms here, and the remaining terms which are of higher order will be estimated in Appendix
A
. Using the relations derived in (
4.4
)-(
4.5
), we have

(4.6)

\displaystyle I_{\varepsilon}\left(P\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}|\nabla P\delta|^{2}+\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta\cdot\left[(P\delta)_{x}\wedge(P\delta)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}L_{\delta}\left[P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right]\cdot\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{1}\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\nabla\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{1}\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge\left(P(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}})\right)_{y}+\left(P(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}})\right)_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}})\right)\cdot\left[(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}\right]

\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}+\left(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\delta\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\delta\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\left(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\left(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\left(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}g\cdot\left[\left(P\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\right)_{y}\right]+2\varepsilon^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\right)\cdot(g_{x}\wedge g_{y})

\displaystyle:=I^{(1)}_{\varepsilon}\left(P\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\right)+\mathcal{R}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon},

where

(4.7)

\displaystyle I^{(1)}_{\varepsilon}\left(P\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}|\nabla P\delta|^{2}+\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta\cdot\left[(P\delta)_{x}\wedge(P\delta)_{y}\right]+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}g\cdot\left[(P\delta)_{x}\wedge(P\delta)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle-\sum_{l=1}^{2}\frac{a_{l}^{2}}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}L_{\delta}[PZ_{-1,l}]\cdot(PZ_{-1,l})-\sum_{l=1}^{2}\frac{p_{l}^{2}}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}L_{\delta}[PZ_{2,l}]\cdot(PZ_{2,l})

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathcal{D}}L_{\delta}\left[\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}PZ_{2,l}\right]\cdot\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{l}PZ_{-1,l}\right).

And
\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}
is given in Appendix
A
, see (
A.1
).

We claim that when the boundary function
\displaystyle g(z,\omega)=\left(2h_{1}^{(1)}(z,\omega),2h_{2}^{(1)}(z,\omega),-2\varepsilon h_{3}^{(1)}(z,\omega)\right)
, the terms in

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}
become higher order after certain cancellations, which will be discussed in Appendix
A
.

4.1.
Estimates of the terms in (
4.7
).

In this subsection, we estimate the terms in (
4.7
).

\displaystyle\bullet
Estimates of the terms containing only
\displaystyle\mu
in (
4.7
).

Integrating by parts, we can write

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}|\nabla P\delta|^{2}=-\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta\cdot(\delta_{x}\wedge\delta_{y})=\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\varphi_{1}-\delta)\cdot(\delta_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}).

First, we calculate
\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\varphi_{1}-\delta)_{1}\cdot(\delta_{x}\wedge\delta_{y})_{1}
. By (
B.2
) and (
2.6
), we deduce

(4.8)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\varphi_{1}-\delta)_{1}\cdot(\delta_{x}\wedge\delta_{y})_{1}=\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left[\frac{2\left[\left(\frac{x-\xi_{1}}{\mu}\right)^{2}-\left(\frac{y-\xi_{2}}{\mu}\right)^{2}\right]}{1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}}-2\mu^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}(z,\xi)+O(\mu^{5})\right]\frac{32\left[\left(\frac{x-\xi_{1}}{\mu}\right)^{4}-\left(\frac{y-\xi_{2}}{\mu}\right)^{4}\right]}{\mu^{2}\left[1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]^{3}}.

Using a change of variable, we obtain

(4.9)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mu^{2}}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\frac{\left[\left(\frac{x-\xi_{1}}{\mu}\right)^{2}-\left(\frac{y-\xi_{2}}{\mu}\right)^{2}\right]\left[\left(\frac{x-\xi_{1}}{\mu}\right)^{4}-\left(\frac{y-\xi_{2}}{\mu}\right)^{4}\right]}{\left[1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]^{4}}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{(x^{2}-y^{2})(x^{4}-y^{4})}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{4}}+O(\mu^{8})=\frac{\pi}{24}+O(\mu^{8}).

From the smoothness of
\displaystyle h_{1}^{(1)}
and
\displaystyle\frac{{\partial}^{3}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x^{l}{\partial}y^{3-l}}(\xi,\xi)=O\left(d^{-5}\right)
for
\displaystyle l=0,1,2,3
, where
\displaystyle d=d_{\varepsilon}=1-|\xi|^{2}
, we obatin

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left[h_{1}^{(1)}(z,\xi)-h_{1}^{(1)}(\xi,\xi)-(z-\xi)\cdot\nabla h_{1}^{(1)}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{1}{2}(z-\xi)\cdot\nabla^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}(\xi,\xi)\cdot(z-\xi)^{\mathrm{T}}\right]\frac{\left[\left(\frac{x-\xi_{1}}{\mu}\right)^{4}-\left(\frac{y-\xi_{2}}{\mu}\right)^{4}\right]}{\mu^{2}\left[1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]^{3}}\right|

\displaystyle=O\left(\int_{\mathcal{D}}\frac{\left|\left(\frac{x-\xi_{1}}{\mu}\right)^{4}-\left(\frac{y-\xi_{2}}{\mu}\right)^{4}\right|d^{-5}\left|z-\xi\right|^{3}}{\left[1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]^{3}}\right),

where

(z-\xi)\cdot\nabla^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}(\xi,\xi)\cdot(z-\xi)^{\mathrm{T}}=(x-\xi_{1})^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+2(x-\xi_{1})(y-\xi_{2})\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\xi)+(y-\xi_{2})^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi).

As a consequence, we deduce

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}}h_{1}^{(1)}(z,\xi)\frac{\left(\frac{x-\xi_{1}}{\mu}\right)^{4}-\left(\frac{y-\xi_{2}}{\mu}\right)^{4}}{\left[1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]^{3}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{D}}h_{1}^{(1)}(\xi,\xi)\frac{\left(\frac{x-\xi_{1}}{\mu}\right)^{4}-\left(\frac{y-\xi_{2}}{\mu}\right)^{4}}{\left[1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]^{3}}+\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left((z-\xi)\cdot\nabla h_{1}^{(1)}(\xi,\xi)\right)\frac{\left(\frac{x-\xi_{1}}{\mu}\right)^{4}-\left(\frac{y-\xi_{2}}{\mu}\right)^{4}}{\left[1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]^{3}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left[(z-\xi)\cdot\nabla^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}(\xi,\xi)\cdot(z-\xi)^{\mathrm{T}}\right]\frac{\left(\frac{x-\xi_{1}}{\mu}\right)^{4}-\left(\frac{y-\xi_{2}}{\mu}\right)^{4}}{\left[1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]^{3}}+O\left(\int_{\mathcal{D}}\frac{\left[\left(\frac{x-\xi_{1}}{\mu}\right)^{4}-\left(\frac{y-\xi_{2}}{\mu}\right)^{4}\right]\frac{\left|z-\xi\right|^{3}}{\left(1-|\xi|^{2}\right)^{5}}}{\left[1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]^{3}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mu^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}(\xi,\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{x^{4}-y^{4}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}+\frac{1}{2}\mu^{4}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{x^{4}-y^{4}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}\left[x^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+y^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\mu^{5}d^{-5}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{(x^{4}-y^{4})|z|^{3}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}\right)+O\left(\mu^{8}d^{-5}\right)

(4.10)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\frac{\pi}{32}\mu^{4}\left[\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)\right]+O\left(\mu^{8}d^{-5}\right).

In (
4.9
) and (
4.10
), we use the fact that

(4.11)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{(x^{2}-y^{2})(x^{4}-y^{4})}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{4}}=\frac{\pi}{24},\quad\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{x^{4}-y^{4}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}=0,\quad\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{(x^{4}-y^{4})|z|^{3}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}=0,

(4.12)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{x^{2}(x^{4}-y^{4})}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{y^{2}(x^{4}-y^{4})}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}=\frac{\pi}{16}.

Combining (
4.8
), (
4.9
) and (
4.10
)
we obtain

(4.13)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\varphi_{1}-\delta)_{1}\cdot(\delta_{x}\wedge\delta_{y})_{1}=\frac{8\pi}{3}-2\pi\mu^{4}\left[\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)\right]+O\left(\mu^{8}d^{-5}\right).

Now we consider
\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\varphi_{1}-\delta)_{2}\cdot(\delta_{x}\wedge\delta_{y})_{2}
. From (
B.2
) and (
2.6
), we have

(4.14)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\varphi_{1}-\delta)_{2}\cdot(\delta_{x}\wedge\delta_{y})_{2}=\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left[\frac{4\frac{(x-\xi_{1})(y-\xi_{2})}{\mu^{2}}}{1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}}-2\mu^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}(z,\xi)+O(\mu^{5})\right]\frac{64\left(\frac{x-\xi_{1}}{\mu}\right)\left(\frac{y-\xi_{2}}{\mu}\right)\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}}{\mu^{2}\left[1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]^{3}}.

And

(4.15)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mu^{2}}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\frac{\frac{(x-\xi_{1})^{2}(y-\xi_{2})^{2}}{\mu^{4}}\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}}{\left[1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]^{4}}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{x^{2}y^{2}|z|^{2}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{4}}+O(\mu^{8})=\frac{\pi}{96}+O\left(\mu^{8}\right).

And similar as (
4.10
), we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}}h_{2}^{(1)}(z,\xi)\frac{\left(\frac{x-\xi_{1}}{\mu}\right)\left(\frac{y-\xi_{2}}{\mu}\right)\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}}{\left[1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]^{3}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{D}}h_{2}^{(1)}(\xi,\xi)\frac{\left(\frac{x-\xi_{1}}{\mu}\right)\left(\frac{y-\xi_{2}}{\mu}\right)\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}}{\left[1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]^{3}}+\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left[(z-\xi)\cdot\nabla h_{2}^{(1)}(\xi,\xi)\right]\frac{\left(\frac{x-\xi_{1}}{\mu}\right)\left(\frac{y-\xi_{2}}{\mu}\right)\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}}{\left[1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]^{3}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left[(z-\xi)\cdot\nabla^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}(\xi,\xi)\cdot(z-\xi)^{\mathrm{T}}\right]\frac{\left(\frac{x-\xi_{1}}{\mu}\right)\left(\frac{y-\xi_{2}}{\mu}\right)\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}}{\left[1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]^{3}}+O\left(\int_{\mathcal{D}}\frac{\left(\frac{x-\xi_{1}}{\mu}\right)\left(\frac{y-\xi_{2}}{\mu}\right)\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}d^{-5}|z-\xi|^{3}}{\left[1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]^{3}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mu^{4}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{x^{2}y^{2}|z|^{2}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\xi)+O\left(\frac{\mu^{5}}{\left(1-|\xi|^{2}\right)^{5}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{xy|z|^{5}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}\right)+O\left(\mu^{8}d^{-5}\right)

(4.16)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\frac{\pi}{32}\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\xi)+O\left(\mu^{8}d^{-5}\right).

In (
4.15
) and (
4.16
), we use the fact that

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{x^{2}y^{2}|z|^{2}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{4}}=\frac{\pi}{96},\quad\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{x^{2}y^{2}|z|^{2}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}=\frac{\pi}{32},\quad\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{xy|z|^{5}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}=0.

Combining (
4.14
), (
4.15
) and (
4.16
), we obtain

(4.17)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\varphi-\delta)_{2}\cdot(\delta_{x}\wedge\delta_{y})_{2}=\frac{8\pi}{3}-4\pi\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\xi)+O\left(\mu^{8}d^{-5}\right).

Similarly, for
\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\varphi_{1}-\delta)_{3}\cdot(\delta_{x}\wedge\delta_{y})_{3}
, we have

(4.18)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\varphi_{1}-\delta)_{3}\cdot(\delta_{x}\wedge\delta_{y})_{3}=\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\frac{2}{1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}}-2\mu^{4}h_{3}^{(1)}(z,\xi)+O(\mu^{5})\right)\frac{16\left[1-\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}}{\mu^{2}\left[1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]^{3}}.

Reasoning as (
4.9
) and (
4.10
), we have

(4.19)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mu^{2}}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\frac{\left[1-\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}}{\left[1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]^{4}}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{\left(1-|z|^{4}\right)\left|z\right|^{2}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{4}}+O\left(\mu^{8}\right)=\frac{\pi}{12}+O(\mu^{8}),

and

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}}h_{3}^{(1)}(z,\xi)\frac{\mu^{2}\left[1-\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}}{\left[1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]^{3}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{D}}h_{3}^{(1)}(\xi,\xi)\frac{\mu^{2}\left[1-\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}}{\left[1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]^{3}}+\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left[(z-\xi)\cdot\nabla h_{3}^{(1)}(\xi,\xi)\right]\frac{\mu^{2}\left[1-\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}}{\left[1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]^{3}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left[(z-\xi)\cdot\nabla^{2}h_{3}^{(1)}(\xi,\xi)\cdot(z-\xi)^{\mathrm{T}}\right]\frac{\mu^{2}\left[1-\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}}{\left[1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]^{3}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\int_{\mathcal{D}}\frac{\mu^{2}\left[1-\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]d^{-7}\left|z-\xi\right|^{3}}{\left[1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\xi}{\mu}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]^{3}}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mu^{4}h_{3}^{(1)}(\xi,\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{\left(1-|z|^{4}\right)\left|z\right|^{2}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}+\frac{1}{2}\mu^{6}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{\left(1-|z|^{4}\right)\left|z\right|^{2}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}\left[x^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{3}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+y^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{3}^{(1)}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\mu^{7}d^{-7}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{\left(1-|z|^{4}\right)\left|z\right|^{5}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}\right)+O\left(\mu^{8}d^{-7}\right)

(4.20)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-\frac{\pi^{2}}{32}\mu^{6}\left[\frac{\partial^{2}h_{3}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\partial^{2}h_{3}^{(1)}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)\right]+O\left(\mu^{7}d^{-7}\right).

In (
4.19
) and (
4.20
), we use the fact that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{\left(1-|z|^{4}\right)\left|z\right|^{2}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{4}}=\frac{\pi}{12},\quad\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{\left(1-|z|^{4}\right)\left|z\right|^{2}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}=0,\quad\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{\left(1-|z|^{4}\right)\left|z\right|^{5}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}=-\frac{9\pi^{2}}{16\sqrt{2}},

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{x^{2}\left(1-|z|^{4}\right)\left|z\right|^{2}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{y^{2}\left(1-|z|^{4}\right)\left|z\right|^{2}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}=-\frac{\pi^{2}}{16}.

From (
4.18
), (
4.19
) and (
4.20
), we obtain

(4.21)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\varphi_{1}-\delta)_{3}\cdot(\delta_{x}\wedge\delta_{y})_{3}

\displaystyle=\frac{8\pi}{3}-\pi^{2}\mu^{6}\left[\frac{\partial^{2}h_{3}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\partial^{2}h_{3}^{(1)}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)\right]+O(\mu^{7}d^{-7})=\frac{8\pi}{3}+O\left(\mu^{6}d^{-6}\right).

From (
4.13
), (
4.17
) and (
4.21
), it follows that

(4.22)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}|\nabla P\delta|^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\varphi_{1}-\delta)\cdot(\delta_{x}\wedge\delta_{y})

\displaystyle=8\pi-2\pi\mu^{4}\left[\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+2\frac{\partial^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)\right]+O\left(\mu^{6}d^{-6}\right)

\displaystyle=8\pi-8\pi\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\xi)+O\left(\mu^{6}d^{-6}\right),

where we use the fact that
\displaystyle\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)=2\frac{\partial^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\xi)
for
\displaystyle\xi\in\mathcal{D}
.

In fact, from the above computation, we can see that

(4.23)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}}\delta\cdot(\delta_{x}\wedge\delta_{y})=-8\pi+O\left(\mu^{8}\right),

and

(4.24)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{1}\cdot(\delta_{x}\wedge\delta_{y})=-8\pi\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\xi)+O\left(\mu^{6}d^{-6}\right).

We estimate the second integral in (
4.7
). It holds that

(4.25)

\displaystyle\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta\cdot\left[(P\delta)_{x}\wedge(P\delta)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta-\varphi_{1})\cdot(\delta_{x}\wedge\delta_{y})-\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta-\varphi_{1})\cdot[\delta_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}+(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}]+\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta-\varphi_{1})\cdot[(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}]

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta-\varphi_{1})\cdot(\delta_{x}\wedge\delta_{y})-\frac{4}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{1}\cdot(\delta_{x}\wedge\delta_{y})+\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{1}\cdot[\delta_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}+(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}]

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta-\varphi_{1})\cdot[(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}].

Since

|(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}|_{1}=O\left(\mu^{6}d^{-8}\right),~|(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}|_{2}=O\left(\mu^{6}d^{-8}\right),~|(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}|_{3}=O\left(\mu^{4}d^{-6}\right),

we obtain

(4.26)

\displaystyle\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta-\varphi_{1})\cdot[(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}]=O\left(\mu^{8}|\log\mu|d^{-8}+\mu^{6}d^{-6}+\mu^{8}d^{-10}\right).

Using (
B.1
), we deduce

\displaystyle\displaystyle\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}\right)_{1}[\delta_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}+(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}]_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-\frac{8}{3}\mu^{7}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left[h_{1}^{(1)}(\xi,\xi)+\mu y\frac{{\partial}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)\right]\frac{4y\left(-3x^{4}-2x^{2}y^{2}+y^{4}+1\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{2}}\left[\frac{{\partial}h_{3}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)+\mu y\frac{{\partial}^{2}h_{3}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-\frac{8}{3}\mu^{5}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left[h_{1}^{(1)}(\xi,\xi)+\mu x\frac{{\partial}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x}(\xi,\xi)\right]\frac{8x\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{2}}\left[\frac{{\partial}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)+\mu x\frac{{\partial}^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\frac{8}{3}\mu^{5}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left[h_{1}^{(1)}(\xi,\xi)+\mu y\frac{{\partial}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)\right]\left[\frac{{\partial}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x}(\xi,\xi)+\mu y\frac{{\partial}^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)\right]\frac{8y\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\frac{8}{3}\mu^{7}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left[h_{1}^{(1)}(\xi,\xi)+\mu x\frac{{\partial}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x}(\xi,\xi)\right]\left[\frac{{\partial}h_{3}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x}(\xi,\xi)+\mu x\frac{{\partial}^{2}h_{3}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)\right]\frac{4x\left(x^{4}-2x^{2}y^{2}-3y^{4}+1\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{2}}+O\left(\mu^{8}d^{-8}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=O\left(\mu^{6}d^{-6}\right).

For
\displaystyle l=2,3
,
\displaystyle\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}\right)_{l}[\delta_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}+(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}]_{l}
can be estimated similarly, we conclude that

(4.27)

\displaystyle\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{1}\cdot[\delta_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}+(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}]=O\left(\mu^{8}|\log\mu|d^{-8}+\mu^{6}d^{-6}\right).

Thus, combining (
4.23
), (
4.24
), (
4.25
), (
4.26
) and (
4.27
), we get

(4.28)

\displaystyle\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta\cdot\left[(P\delta)_{x}\wedge(P\delta)_{y}\right]=-\frac{16}{3}\pi+16\pi\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\xi)+O\left(\mu^{8}|\log\mu|d^{-8}+\mu^{6}d^{-6}+\mu^{8}d^{-10}\right).

Next, we compute the terms that contain the boundary function
\displaystyle g(z,\xi)
, i.e.
\displaystyle 2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}g\cdot\left[(P\delta)_{x}\wedge(P\delta)_{y}\right]
.
In Proposition
4.1
, the boundary function
\displaystyle g(z,\omega)=\left(g_{1}(z,\omega),g_{2}(z,\omega),g_{3}(z,\omega)\right)
is given by

g_{1}(z,\omega)=2h_{1}^{(1)}(z,\omega),\quad g_{2}(z,\omega)=2h_{2}^{(1)}(z,\omega),\quad g_{3}(z,\omega)=-2\varepsilon h_{3}^{(1)}(z,\omega),

and
\displaystyle\left|g(z,\omega)-g(z,\xi)\right|=O(d^{-3}|\xi-\omega|).

There holds

(4.29)

\displaystyle 2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}g\cdot\left[(P\delta)_{x}\wedge(P\delta)_{y}\right]=2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}g\cdot(\delta_{x}\wedge\delta_{y})-g\cdot[\delta_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}+(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}]+g\cdot[(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}].

Similar to the calculation of (
4.22
), using (
B.2
) and
\displaystyle\frac{\partial^{2}g_{1}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\omega)=-\frac{\partial^{2}g_{1}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\omega)
for
\displaystyle\xi\in\mathcal{D}
as well as the fact that
\displaystyle\frac{\partial^{2}g_{3}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\omega)+\frac{\partial^{2}g_{3}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\omega)=0
, we have

(4.30)

\displaystyle 2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}g\cdot(\delta_{x}\wedge\delta_{y})

\displaystyle=2\varepsilon\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left[\mu^{2}\frac{x^{2}}{2}\frac{\partial^{2}g_{1}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\omega)+\mu^{2}\frac{y^{2}}{2}\frac{\partial^{2}g_{1}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\omega)+O(\mu^{4})\right]\frac{-32(x^{4}-y^{4})}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}

\displaystyle+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left[\mu^{2}xy\frac{\partial^{2}g_{2}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\omega)+O(\mu^{4})\right]\frac{-64xy|z|^{2}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}+O\left(\varepsilon\mu^{4}d^{-6}\right)

\displaystyle=-4\pi\varepsilon\mu^{2}\left[\frac{\partial^{2}g_{1}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\omega)+\frac{\partial^{2}g_{2}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\omega)\right]+O\left(\varepsilon\mu^{4}d^{-6}\right).

For the second term in (
4.29
), similar to (
4.27
), we get

(4.31)

\displaystyle-2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}g\cdot[\delta_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}+(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}]=O\left(\varepsilon\mu^{4}d^{-3}+\varepsilon\mu^{6}|\log\mu|d^{-5}+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{4}|\log\mu|d^{-3}\right).

And analogously with (
4.26
), there holds

(4.32)

\displaystyle 2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}g\cdot[(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}]=O\left(\varepsilon\mu^{6}d^{-8}+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{4}d^{-6}\right),

(4.33)

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta-\varphi)\cdot(g_{x}\wedge g_{y})=O\left(\varepsilon^{3}\mu^{2}|\log\mu|+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{2}+\varepsilon^{3}\mu^{2}d^{-2}+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{4}d^{-4}\right).

\displaystyle\bullet
Estimates of the terms containing
\displaystyle a_{l}
in (
4.7
).

We start with the terms in (
4.7
) that contain
\displaystyle a_{l}^{2}
,
\displaystyle l=1,2
. Recall that
\displaystyle Z_{k,l}
,
\displaystyle k=-1,2
,
\displaystyle l=1,2
satisfy the linearized equation of (
1.4
) around
\displaystyle\mathcal{W}
defined by

\displaystyle\displaystyle L_{\mathcal{W}}[v]:=\Delta v-2\mathcal{W}_{x}\wedge v_{y}-2v_{x}\wedge\mathcal{W}_{y}=0

for
\displaystyle v\in\mathcal{X}
. Thus,

\displaystyle-\frac{a_{1}^{2}}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}L_{\delta}[PZ_{-1,1}]\cdot(PZ_{-1,1})

\displaystyle=-a_{1}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left[\delta_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{-1,1})_{y}+(\varphi_{-1,1})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]\cdot(PZ_{-1,1})

\displaystyle=-a_{1}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{-1,1}\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge(PZ_{-1,1})_{y}+(PZ_{-1,1})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right].

Using (
B.3
), we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle-a_{1}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{-1,1}\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge(Z_{-1,1})_{y}+(Z_{-1,1})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-2a_{1}^{2}\mu\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left[\mu x\frac{\partial h_{1}^{(-1,1)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)+O\left(\mu^{3}d^{-4}\right)\right]\frac{128x\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(x^{2}\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}-2\right)+3y^{2}\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{4}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-2a_{1}^{2}\mu\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left[\mu y\frac{\partial h_{2}^{(-1,1)}}{\partial y}(\xi,\xi)+O\left(\mu^{3}d^{-4}\right)\right]\frac{64y\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(3x^{6}+7x^{4}y^{2}+x^{2}\left(5y^{4}-9\right)+y^{6}+y^{2}\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{4}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(a_{1}^{2}\mu^{4}d^{-4}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-8\pi a_{1}^{2}\mu^{2}\left[\frac{\partial h_{1}^{(-1,1)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\partial h_{2}^{(-1,1)}}{\partial y}(\xi,\xi)\right]+O\left(a_{1}^{2}\mu^{4}d^{-4}\right)

(4.34)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-16\pi a_{1}^{2}\mu^{2}\frac{\partial h_{1}^{(-1,1)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)+O\left(a_{1}^{2}\mu^{4}d^{-4}\right).

And similar to (
4.27
), there holds

\displaystyle a_{1}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{-1,1}\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{-1,1})_{y}+(\varphi_{-1,1})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]=O\left(a_{1}^{2}\mu^{6}|\log\mu|d^{-6}+a_{1}^{2}\mu^{4}d^{-4}\right).

Therefore,

(4.35)

\displaystyle-\frac{a_{1}^{2}}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}L_{\delta}[PZ_{-1,1}]\cdot(PZ_{-1,1})=-16\pi a_{1}^{2}\mu^{2}\frac{\partial h_{1}^{(-1,1)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)+O\left(a_{1}^{2}\mu^{4}d^{-4}\right).

And similarly, by (
B.4
), one has

\displaystyle\displaystyle-\frac{a_{2}^{2}}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}L_{\delta}[PZ_{-1,2}]\cdot(PZ_{-1,2})

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-a_{2}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{-1,2}\cdot\left(\delta_{x}\wedge(Z_{-1,2})_{y}+(Z_{-1,2})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right)+O\left(a_{2}^{2}\mu^{6}|\log\mu|d^{-6}+a_{2}^{2}\mu^{4}d^{-4}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-2a_{2}^{2}\mu\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left[\mu y\frac{\partial h_{1}^{(-1,2)}}{\partial y}(\xi,\xi)+O\left(\mu^{3}d^{-4}\right)\right]\frac{-128y\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(\left(x^{4}-2\right)y^{2}+2x^{2}y^{4}+3x^{2}+y^{6}\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{4}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-2a_{2}^{2}\mu\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left[\mu x\frac{\partial h_{2}^{(-1,2)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)+O\left(\mu^{3}d^{-4}\right)\right]\frac{64x\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(x^{6}+5x^{4}y^{2}+x^{2}\left(7y^{4}+1\right)+3y^{2}\left(y^{4}-3\right)\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{4}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(a_{2}^{2}\mu^{4}d^{-4}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=8\pi a_{2}^{2}\mu^{2}\left[\frac{\partial h_{1}^{(-1,2)}}{\partial y}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{\partial h_{2}^{(-1,2)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)\right]+O\left(a_{2}^{2}\mu^{4}d^{-4}\right)

(4.36)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-16\pi a_{2}^{2}\mu^{2}\frac{\partial h_{2}^{(-1,2)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)+O\left(a_{2}^{2}\mu^{4}d^{-4}\right).

\displaystyle\bullet
Estimates of the terms containing
\displaystyle p_{l}
in (
4.7
).

Now, we consider the term of
\displaystyle p_{l}^{2}
,
\displaystyle l=1,2
.
Similar to the calculations of (
4.35
), one has

\displaystyle\displaystyle-\sum_{l=1}^{2}\frac{p_{l}^{2}}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}L_{\delta}[PZ_{2,l}]\cdot(PZ_{2,l})

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left[\delta_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{2,l})_{y}+(\varphi_{2,l})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]\cdot(PZ_{2,l})

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{2,l}\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge(PZ_{2,l})_{y}+(PZ_{2,l})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{2,l}\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge(Z_{2,l})_{y}+(Z_{2,l})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]+O\left((p_{1}^{2}+p_{2}^{2})\left(\mu^{12}|\log\mu|d^{-12}+\mu^{10}d^{-10}\right)\right).

Using (
B.11
), we obtain

(4.37)

\displaystyle-p_{1}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{2,1}\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge(Z_{2,1})_{y}+(Z_{2,1})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=-2p_{1}^{2}\mu^{4}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left[h_{1}^{(2,1)}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\mu^{2}}{2}x^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h^{(2,1)}_{1}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\mu^{2}}{2}y^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h^{(2,1)}_{1}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\mu^{4}}{24}x^{4}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,1)}_{1}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{\mu^{4}}{24}y^{4}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,1)}_{1}}{\partial y^{4}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\mu^{4}}{4}x^{2}y^{2}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,1)}_{1}}{\partial x^{2}\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)\right]\frac{32\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(5x^{4}-14x^{2}y^{2}+5y^{4}-1\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{4}}

\displaystyle-2p_{1}^{2}\mu^{4}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left[h_{2}^{(2,1)}(\xi,\xi)+\mu^{2}xy\frac{\partial^{2}h^{(2,1)}_{2}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\mu^{4}}{6}x^{3}y\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,1)}_{2}}{\partial x^{3}\partial y}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\mu^{4}}{6}xy^{3}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,1)}_{2}}{\partial x\partial y^{3}}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\times\frac{384xy\left(x^{4}-y^{4}\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{4}}

\displaystyle+2p_{1}^{2}\mu^{6}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left[h_{3}^{(2,1)}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\mu^{2}}{2}x^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h^{(2,1)}_{3}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\mu^{2}}{2}y^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h^{(2,1)}_{3}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\times\frac{64(x-y)(x+y)\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}-2\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{4}}+O\left(p_{1}^{2}\mu^{10}d^{-10}\right)

\displaystyle=-2p_{1}^{2}\mu^{4}\left[\frac{\pi^{2}}{4}\mu^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h^{(2,1)}_{1}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\pi^{2}}{4}\mu^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h^{(2,1)}_{1}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\pi}{6}\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,1)}_{1}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\pi}{6}\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,1)}_{1}}{\partial y^{4}}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle-2p_{1}^{2}\mu^{4}\left[\frac{\pi}{6}\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,1)}_{2}}{\partial x^{3}\partial y}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{\pi}{6}\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,1)}_{2}}{\partial x\partial y^{3}}(\xi,\xi)\right]+O\left(p_{1}^{2}\mu^{10}d^{-10}\right)

\displaystyle=-\frac{4\pi}{3}p_{1}^{2}\mu^{8}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,1)}_{1}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)+O\left(p_{1}^{2}\mu^{10}d^{-10}\right).

And by (
B.12
), we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle-p_{2}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{2,2}\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge(Z_{2,2})_{y}+(Z_{2,2})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-2p_{2}^{2}\mu^{4}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left[h_{1}^{(2,2)}(\xi,\xi)+\mu^{2}xy\frac{\partial^{2}h^{(2,2)}_{1}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\mu^{4}}{6}x^{3}y\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,2)}_{1}}{\partial x^{3}\partial y}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\mu^{4}}{6}xy^{3}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,2)}_{1}}{\partial x\partial y^{3}}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\frac{384xy\left(x^{4}-y^{4}\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{4}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-2p_{2}^{2}\mu^{4}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left[h_{2}^{(2,2)}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\mu^{2}}{2}x^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h^{(2,2)}_{2}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\mu^{2}}{2}y^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h^{(2,2)}_{2}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\mu^{4}}{24}x^{4}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,2)}_{2}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)\right.

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\left.+\frac{\mu^{4}}{24}y^{4}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,2)}_{2}}{\partial y^{4}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\mu^{4}}{4}x^{2}y^{2}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,2)}_{2}}{\partial x^{2}\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)\right]\frac{-32\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(x^{4}-22x^{2}y^{2}+y^{4}+1\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{4}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+2p_{2}^{2}\mu^{6}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left[h_{3}^{(2,2)}(\xi,\xi)+\mu^{2}xy\frac{\partial^{2}h^{(2,2)}_{3}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\xi)\right]\frac{128xy\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}-2\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{4}}+O\left(p_{2}^{2}\mu^{10}d^{-10}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-2p_{2}^{2}\mu^{4}\left[\frac{\pi}{6}\mu^{4}\left(\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,2)}_{1}}{\partial x^{3}\partial y}-\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,2)}_{1}}{\partial x\partial y^{3}}\right)(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-2p_{2}^{2}\mu^{4}\left[\frac{\pi^{2}}{4}\mu^{2}\left(\frac{\partial^{2}h^{(2,2)}_{2}}{\partial x^{2}}+\frac{\partial^{2}h^{(2,2)}_{2}}{\partial y^{2}}\right)(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\pi}{12}\mu^{4}\left(\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,2)}_{2}}{\partial x^{4}}+\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,2)}_{2}}{\partial y^{4}}\right)(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\pi}{2}\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,2)}_{2}}{\partial x^{2}\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(p_{2}^{2}\mu^{10}d^{-10}\right)

(4.38)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\frac{4\pi}{3}p_{2}^{2}\mu^{8}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,2)}_{2}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)+O\left(p_{2}^{2}\mu^{10}d^{-10}\right).

Thus

(4.39)

\displaystyle-\sum_{l=1}^{2}\frac{p_{l}^{2}}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}L_{\delta}[PZ_{2,l}]\cdot(PZ_{2,l})=-\frac{4\pi}{3}p_{1}^{2}\mu^{8}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,1)}_{1}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{4\pi}{3}p_{2}^{2}\mu^{8}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,2)}_{2}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)+O\left((p_{1}^{2}+p_{2}^{2})\mu^{10}d^{-10}\right).

Now, we estimate terms in (
4.7
) that involve
\displaystyle p_{l}
,
\displaystyle l=1,2
.
Following a similar approach as in (
4.35
) and (
4.36
), we have

(4.40)

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathcal{D}}L_{\delta}\left[\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}PZ_{2,l}\right]\cdot\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{l}PZ_{-1,l}\right)

\displaystyle=-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left[\delta_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}\varphi_{2,l}\right)_{y}+\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}\varphi_{2,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]\cdot\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{l}PZ_{-1,l}\right)

\displaystyle=-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}\varphi_{2,l}\right)\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{l}Z_{-1,l}\right)_{y}+\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{l}Z_{-1,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+O\left((a_{1}+a_{2})(p_{1}+p_{2})\left(\mu^{9}|\log\mu|d^{-9}+\mu^{7}d^{-7}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=-32\pi p_{1}a_{1}\mu^{5}\frac{\partial h_{1}^{(2,1)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)-32\pi p_{1}a_{2}\mu^{5}\frac{\partial h_{2}^{(2,1)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)-32\pi p_{2}a_{1}\mu^{5}\frac{\partial h_{1}^{(2,2)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)-32\pi p_{2}a_{2}\mu^{5}\frac{\partial h_{2}^{(2,2)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)

\displaystyle+O\left((a_{1}+a_{2})(p_{1}+p_{2})\left(\mu^{9}|\log\mu|d^{-9}+\mu^{7}d^{-7}\right)\right).

4.2.
Conclusion

In this subsection, we conclude the results calculated in Subsection
4.1
and Appendix
A
.
Indeed, we have

\displaystyle I^{(1)}_{\varepsilon}\left(P\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\right)

\displaystyle=(\ref{expansion-15})+(\ref{A30-delta3})+(\ref{A30-delta4})+(\ref{Jy18-delta4})+(\ref{Jan5-4})+(\ref{Jy18-delta5})+(\ref{A30-a1})+(\ref{M27-a2})+(\ref{A30-p3})+(\ref{Au11-1})

\displaystyle=\frac{8\pi}{3}+8\pi\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\xi)-4\pi\varepsilon\mu^{2}\left[\frac{\partial^{2}g_{1}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\omega)+\frac{\partial^{2}g_{2}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\omega)\right]

\displaystyle-16\pi a_{1}^{2}\mu^{2}\frac{\partial h_{1}^{(-1,1)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)-16\pi a_{2}^{2}\mu^{2}\frac{\partial h_{2}^{(-1,2)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)

\displaystyle-\frac{4\pi}{3}p_{1}^{2}\mu^{8}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,1)}_{1}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{4\pi}{3}p_{2}^{2}\mu^{8}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,2)}_{2}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)

\displaystyle-32\pi p_{1}a_{1}\mu^{5}\frac{\partial h_{1}^{(2,1)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)-32\pi p_{1}a_{2}\mu^{5}\frac{\partial h_{2}^{(2,1)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)
