\displaystyle-32\pi p_{2}a_{1}\mu^{5}\frac{\partial h_{1}^{(2,2)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)-32\pi p_{2}a_{2}\mu^{5}\frac{\partial h_{2}^{(2,2)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)+\tilde{e}^{(1)}(\mu,a,p),

where

\displaystyle\tilde{e}^{(1)}(\varepsilon,\mu,a,p)=

\displaystyle O\left(\mu^{8}|\log\mu|d^{-8}+\mu^{6}d^{-6}+\mu^{8}d^{-10}\right)+O\left(\varepsilon\mu^{4}d^{-6}+\varepsilon\mu^{4}d^{-3}+\varepsilon\mu^{6}|\log\mu|d^{-5}+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{4}|\log\mu|d^{-3}\right)

\displaystyle+O\left(\varepsilon\mu^{6}d^{-8}+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{4}d^{-6}\right)+O\left(\varepsilon^{3}\mu^{2}|\log\mu|+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{2}+\varepsilon^{3}\mu^{2}d^{-2}+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{4}d^{-4}\right)

\displaystyle+O\left(\left(a_{1}^{2}+a_{2}^{2}\right)\mu^{4}d^{-4}\right)+O\left((p_{1}^{2}+p_{2}^{2})\mu^{10}d^{-10}\right)

\displaystyle+O\left((a_{1}+a_{2})(p_{1}+p_{2})\left(\mu^{9}|\log\mu|d^{-9}+\mu^{7}d^{-7}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\left(\mu^{8}d^{-10}+\varepsilon\mu^{4}d^{-6}+\varepsilon^{3}\mu^{2}|\log\mu|+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{2}+\varepsilon^{3}\mu^{2}d^{-2}\right)

\displaystyle+O\left(\left(a_{1}^{2}+a_{2}^{2}\right)\mu^{4}d^{-4}\right)+O\left((p_{1}^{2}+p_{2}^{2})\mu^{10}d^{-10}\right)+O\left((a_{1}+a_{2})(p_{1}+p_{2})\mu^{7}d^{-7}\right).

Recall that

\displaystyle I_{\varepsilon}\left(P\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\right)=I^{(1)}_{\varepsilon}\left(P\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}\right)+\mathcal{R}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}
,
where
\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}
is estimated in Appendix
A
, see (
A.44
).
Thus we complete the proof of Proposition
4.1
in this case. For general rotation
\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}
, it is sufficient to consider the boundary datum
\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}^{-1}\tilde{g}
and to replace
\displaystyle g_{1}
,
\displaystyle g_{2}
,
\displaystyle g_{3}
by
\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{Q}^{-1}g\right)_{1}
,
\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{Q}^{-1}g\right)_{2}
and
\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{Q}^{-1}g\right)_{3}
, respectively.
∎

5.
Energy expansion for multi-bubbles

In this section, we consider the case of multiple bubbles, starting with the case of two bubbles. We define

z_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}:=P\mathcal{Q}_{1}\delta_{\mu_{1},\xi^{(1)},a_{1},p_{1}}\approx P\mathcal{Q}_{1}\delta_{\mu_{1},\xi^{(1)}}+P\mathcal{Q}_{1}\left(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)

and

z_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}:=P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{\mu_{2},\xi^{(2)},a_{2},p_{2}}\approx P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{\mu_{2},\xi^{(2)}}+P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\left(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right),

where

\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}=\sum_{l=1}^{2}\left(a_{1,l}Z_{-1,l}\left(\frac{z-\xi^{(1)}}{\mu_{1}}\right)+p_{1,l}Z_{2,l}\left(\frac{z-\xi^{(1)}}{\mu_{1}}\right)\right),

\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}=\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{2,l}\left(Z_{-1,l}\left(\frac{z-\xi^{(2)}}{\mu_{2}}\right)+p_{2,l}Z_{2,l}\left(\frac{z-\xi^{(2)}}{\mu_{2}}\right)\right),

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}
,
\displaystyle i=1,2
denotes the corresponding higher order terms, as discussed in Section
4
.
For simplicity, we denote
\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{1}\delta_{\mu_{1},\xi^{(1)}}
,
\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{\mu_{2},\xi^{(2)}}
,
\displaystyle\xi^{(1)}
and
\displaystyle\xi^{(2)}
as
\displaystyle\delta_{1}
,
\displaystyle\delta_{2}
,
\displaystyle\xi
and
\displaystyle\zeta
, respectively. We assume
\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{1}=Id
, namely, the first bubble is not rotated. For
\displaystyle\bar{C}>0,\ k=2
and

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}\in Z,

we aim to expand the functional
\displaystyle I_{\varepsilon}
on
\displaystyle Z
in terms of the parameters
\displaystyle\mu_{i}
,
\displaystyle\xi^{(i)}
,
\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{i}
,
\displaystyle a_{i,l}
and
\displaystyle p_{i,l}
for
\displaystyle i,l=1,2
. In the following, we define

(5.1)

\sigma:=\xi^{(2)}-\xi^{(1)}=\zeta-\xi=(\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2})

and write
\displaystyle P\delta_{1}=\delta_{1}-\varphi_{1}(z,\xi)
,
\displaystyle P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}=\mathcal{Q}_{2}\left(\delta_{2}-\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)\right)
. Recall that for
\displaystyle u\in H_{0}^{1}(\mathcal{D},\mathbb{R}^{3})
, the explicit form of the functional
\displaystyle I_{\varepsilon}(u)
is

(5.2)

I_{\varepsilon}(u)=\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}|\nabla u|^{2}+\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}u\cdot(u_{x}\wedge u_{y})+\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}u\cdot(u_{x}\wedge g_{y}+g_{x}\wedge u_{y})+2\varepsilon^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}u\cdot(g_{x}\wedge g_{y}).

For
\displaystyle u=\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}\in Z
, we provide the estimates for (
5.2
). By applying integration by parts, we decompose the functional as
\displaystyle I_{\varepsilon}(u)=I^{(1)}_{\varepsilon}(u)+I^{(2)}_{\varepsilon}(u),

where

\displaystyle I^{(1)}_{\varepsilon}(u)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left|\nabla z_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left|\nabla z_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right|^{2}+\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}z_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\cdot\left[\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{y}\right]+\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}z_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\cdot\left[\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}z_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\cdot\left[\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{x}\wedge g^{(1)}_{y}+g^{(1)}_{x}\wedge\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{y}\right]+\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}z_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\cdot\left[\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}+\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+2\varepsilon^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}z_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\cdot\left(g^{(1)}_{x}\wedge g^{(1)}_{y}\right)+2\varepsilon^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}z_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\cdot\left[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}\right],

(5.3)

\displaystyle I^{(2)}_{\varepsilon}(u)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla z_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\cdot\nabla z_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}z_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\cdot\left[\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right)_{y}\right]+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}z_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\cdot\left[\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}g^{(1)}\cdot\left[\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right)_{y}\right]+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)\cdot\left[\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}z_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\cdot\left[\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}+\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right)_{y}\right]+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}z_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\cdot\left[\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{x}\wedge g^{(1)}_{y}+g^{(1)}_{x}\wedge\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+2\varepsilon^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}g^{(1)}\cdot\left[\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}+\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+2\varepsilon^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\cdot\left[\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{x}\wedge g^{(1)}_{y}+g^{(1)}_{x}\wedge\left(z_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+2\varepsilon^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}z_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\cdot\left[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}\right]+2\varepsilon^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}z_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\cdot\left(g^{(1)}_{x}\wedge g^{(1)}_{y}\right).

The estimate of
\displaystyle I^{(1)}_{\varepsilon}(u)
is provided in Section
4
. As for
\displaystyle I^{(2)}_{\varepsilon}(u)
, we estimate it in Subsections
5.1
-
5.3
.

5.1.
Interaction between two bubbles
\displaystyle\delta_{1}
and
\displaystyle\delta_{2}

In this subsection, we address the terms in (
5.3
) that involve both
\displaystyle P\delta_{1}
and
\displaystyle P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}
, namely,

(5.4)

\displaystyle N(\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Id,\mathcal{Q}_{2})

\displaystyle:=\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla P\delta_{1}\cdot\nabla P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta_{1}\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{x}\wedge(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2})_{y}\right]+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\cdot\left[(P\delta_{1})_{x}\wedge(P\delta_{1})_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}g^{(1)}\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{y}\right]+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\delta_{1}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\delta_{1}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta_{1}\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}+\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\cdot\left[\left(P\delta_{1}\right)_{x}\wedge g^{(1)}_{y}+g^{(1)}_{x}\wedge\left(P\delta_{1}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+2\varepsilon^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}g^{(1)}\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}+\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+2\varepsilon^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\cdot\left[\left(P\delta_{1}\right)_{x}\wedge g^{(1)}_{y}+g^{(1)}_{x}\wedge\left(P\delta_{1}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+2\varepsilon^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta_{1}\cdot\left[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}\right]+2\varepsilon^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\cdot\left(g^{(1)}_{x}\wedge g^{(1)}_{y}\right).

Integrating by parts, expression (
5.4
) simplifies to

\displaystyle\displaystyle N(\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Id,\mathcal{Q}_{2})

\displaystyle\displaystyle=2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\cdot\left[(\delta_{1})_{x}\wedge(\delta_{1})_{y}\right]+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\cdot\left[(\delta_{1})_{x}\wedge(\delta_{1})_{y}\right]+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}g^{(1)}\cdot\left[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\delta_{1}+\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)\cdot\left[(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2})_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}+\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2})_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta_{1}\cdot\left[(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta))_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}-\left(\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varepsilon g^{(1)}\cdot\left[(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta))_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}-\left(\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}+\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)\cdot\left[(\delta_{1})_{x}\wedge\left(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi)-\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{y}+\left(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi)-\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{x}\wedge(\delta_{1})_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\cdot\left[(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi))_{x}\wedge\left(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi)-\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{y}-\left(\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi)-\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\cdot\left[(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi))_{x}\wedge\left(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi)-\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{y}-\left(\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi)\right)_{y}\right].

Lemma 5.1
.

There holds

\displaystyle N(\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Id,\mathcal{Q}_{2})=F_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Id,\mathcal{Q}_{2})+\mathcal{E}\left(\mu_{1},\mu_{2}\right),

where

\displaystyle\displaystyle F_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Id,\mathcal{Q}_{2}):=

\displaystyle\displaystyle~2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\cdot\left[(\delta_{1})_{x}\wedge(\delta_{1})_{y}\right]+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\cdot\left[(\delta_{1})_{x}\wedge(\delta_{1})_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}g^{(1)}\cdot\left[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\displaystyle~q_{11}\left[\frac{-48\pi\left(\sigma_{1}^{4}-6\sigma_{2}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}+\sigma_{2}^{4}\right)}{|\sigma|^{8}}\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}-4\pi\varepsilon\mu_{2}^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}g_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\zeta,\omega^{(1)})\right]

(5.5)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+q_{12}\left[\frac{-192\pi\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}(\sigma_{1}^{2}-\sigma_{2}^{2})\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}}{|\sigma|^{8}}-4\pi\varepsilon\mu_{2}^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}g^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial x{\partial}y}(\zeta,\omega^{(1)})\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle+q_{21}\left[\frac{-192\pi\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}(\sigma_{1}^{2}-\sigma_{2}^{2})\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}}{|\sigma|^{8}}-4\pi\varepsilon\mu_{2}^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}g^{(1)}_{1}}{\partial x^{2}}(\zeta,\omega^{(1)})\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle+q_{22}\left[\frac{48\pi\left(\sigma_{1}^{4}-6\sigma_{2}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}+\sigma_{2}^{4}\right)}{|\sigma|^{8}}\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}-4\pi\varepsilon\mu_{2}^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}g^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial x\partial y}(\zeta,\omega^{(1)})\right],

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{E}\left(\mu_{1},\mu_{2}\right):=

\displaystyle\displaystyle~O\left(\varepsilon^{3}d^{-3}+\varepsilon^{2}\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-6}+\varepsilon^{2}\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-6}+\varepsilon(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)\right).

Proof.

Let us now compute the term
\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\cdot\left[(\delta_{1})_{x}\wedge(\delta_{1})_{y}\right]
. Up to an error of order
\displaystyle\varepsilon
, we have

P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}=\begin{pmatrix}q_{11}\quad q_{12}\quad q_{13}\\
q_{21}\quad q_{22}\quad q_{23}\\
q_{31}\quad q_{32}\quad q_{33}\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}\frac{2\left[\left(\frac{x-\zeta_{1}}{\mu_{2}}\right)^{2}-\left(\frac{y-\zeta_{2}}{\mu_{2}}\right)^{2}\right]}{1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\zeta}{\mu_{2}}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}}-2\mu_{2}^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}(z,\zeta)\\
\frac{4\frac{(x-\zeta_{1})(y-\zeta_{2})}{\mu_{2}^{2}}}{1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\zeta}{\mu_{2}}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}}-2\mu_{2}^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}(z,\zeta)\\
\frac{-2}{1+\left(\left|\frac{z-\zeta}{\mu_{2}}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}}+2\mu_{2}^{4}h_{3}^{(1)}(z,\zeta)\end{pmatrix},

where
\displaystyle q_{ij},\ i,j=1,2,3
are the entries of the matrix
\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{2}
.

Since

(5.6)

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\cdot\left[(\delta_{1})_{x}\wedge(\delta_{1})_{y}\right]=2\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}q_{ij}\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta_{2}-\varphi_{2})_{j}\cdot\left[(\delta_{1})_{x}\wedge(\delta_{1})_{y}\right]_{i},

the terms in (
5.6
) involving the coefficients
\displaystyle q_{ij}
are given by
\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta_{2}-\varphi_{2})_{j}\cdot\left[(\delta_{1})_{x}\wedge(\delta_{1})_{y}\right]_{i}
,
\displaystyle i,j=1,2,3
, respectively. Recall that in the definition of
\displaystyle Z
, we assume that
\displaystyle\text{dist}~(\xi^{(i)},\xi^{(j)})\geq\bar{C}^{-1}
for
\displaystyle i\neq j
and
\displaystyle\frac{\varepsilon}{\mu^{2}_{i}},\frac{\varepsilon}{\mu^{2}_{j}}\in[\bar{C}^{-1},\bar{C}]
.
Let
\displaystyle\gamma\leq\frac{\bar{C}^{-1}}{2}
be a fixed positive number, we may choose

\displaystyle\gamma=\frac{|\sigma|}{8}
, where
\displaystyle\sigma
is defined in (
5.1
). Then, we divide the integral domain into two regions,

\mathcal{D}_{1}:=B_{\gamma}(\zeta),\qquad\quad\mathcal{D}_{2}:=\mathcal{D}\backslash B_{\gamma}(\zeta),

where
\displaystyle B_{\gamma}(\zeta)
is the ball of radius
\displaystyle\gamma
centered at
\displaystyle\zeta
.

On the set
\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{1}=B_{\gamma}(\zeta)
, using (
2.9
) and recalling that for
\displaystyle i=1,2
,

d=d_{\varepsilon}=1-|\xi^{(i)}|^{2}=O\left(\mu_{i}^{\frac{2}{3}-\alpha}\right)=O\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\right),

we have

(5.7)

\displaystyle\left|2\int_{\mathcal{D}_{1}}(\delta_{2}-\varphi_{2})_{1}\left[(\delta_{1})_{x}\wedge(\delta_{1})_{y}\right]_{1}\right|\lesssim\mu_{1}^{6}\int_{\mathcal{D}_{1}}\left|(\delta_{2}-\varphi_{2})_{1}\right|\lesssim\mu_{1}^{6}\left(\mu_{2}^{2}|\log\mu_{2}|+\mu_{2}^{2}d^{-2}\right).

In
\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{2}
, since
\displaystyle z
is away from
\displaystyle\zeta
, for
\displaystyle\mu_{2}
sufficiently small,
\displaystyle\delta_{2}\left(\frac{z-\zeta}{\mu_{2}}\right)
has the following expansion

(5.8)

\displaystyle\delta_{2}\left(\frac{z-\zeta}{\mu_{2}}\right)=\left(\frac{2\mu_{2}^{2}\left[(x-\zeta_{1})^{2}-(y-\zeta_{2})^{2}\right]}{|z-\zeta|^{4}}+O\left(\mu_{2}^{6}\right),\frac{4\mu_{2}^{2}(x-\zeta_{1})(y-\zeta_{2})}{|z-\zeta|^{4}}+O\left(\mu_{2}^{6}\right),1-\frac{2\mu_{2}^{4}}{|z-\zeta|^{4}}+O\left(\mu_{2}^{8}\right)\right).

Then we deduce

\displaystyle\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}_{2}}(\delta_{2}-\varphi_{2})_{1}\left[(\delta_{1})_{x}\wedge(\delta_{1})_{y}\right]_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-128\int_{\frac{\mathcal{D}_{2}-\xi}{\mu_{1}}}\left[\mu_{2}^{2}\frac{(\mu_{1}x+\xi_{1}-\zeta_{1})^{2}-(\mu_{1}y+\xi_{2}-\zeta_{2})^{2}}{|\mu_{1}z+\xi-\zeta|^{4}}+O(\mu_{2}^{6})\right]\frac{x^{4}-y^{4}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+128\int_{\frac{\mathcal{D}_{2}-\xi}{\mu_{1}}}\left[\mu_{2}^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}(\mu_{1}z+\xi,\zeta)+O(\mu_{2}^{5})\right]\frac{(x^{4}-y^{4})}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}

(5.9)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-64\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}\int_{\frac{\mathcal{D}_{2}-\xi}{\mu_{1}}}\frac{6\left(\sigma_{1}^{4}-6\sigma_{2}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}+\sigma_{2}^{4}\right)}{|\sigma|^{8}}\frac{x^{2}(x^{4}-y^{4})}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}+\frac{-6\left(\sigma_{1}^{4}-6\sigma_{2}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}+\sigma_{2}^{4}\right)}{|\sigma|^{8}}\frac{y^{2}(x^{4}-y^{4})}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+64\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}\int_{\frac{\mathcal{D}_{2}-\xi}{\mu_{1}}}\frac{x^{2}(x^{4}-y^{4})}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\zeta)+\frac{y^{2}(x^{4}-y^{4})}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\zeta)+O\left(\mu_{1}^{4}\mu_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\frac{-48\pi\left(\sigma_{1}^{4}-6\sigma_{2}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}+\sigma_{2}^{4}\right)}{|\sigma|^{8}}\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}+4\pi\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}\left[\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\zeta)-\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\zeta)\right]+O\left(\mu_{1}^{4}\mu_{2}^{2}\right).

Hence, combining (
5.7
) and (
5.9
), and the fact that
\displaystyle\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\zeta)=-\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\zeta)
, we find

(5.10)

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta_{2}-\varphi_{2})_{1}\left[(\delta_{1})_{x}\wedge(\delta_{1})_{y}\right]_{1}=\frac{-48\pi\left(\sigma_{1}^{4}-6\sigma_{2}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}+\sigma_{2}^{4}\right)}{|\sigma|^{8}}\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}+8\pi\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\zeta)+O\left(\mu_{1}^{4}\mu_{2}^{2}\right).

Next, we present the details of the terms involving
\displaystyle q_{22}
, i.e., we need to compute
\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta_{2}-\varphi_{2})_{2}\left[(\delta_{1})_{x}\wedge(\delta_{1})_{y}\right]_{2}
. Similarly, in
\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{1}
, it can be estimated as
\displaystyle O\left(\mu_{1}^{6}\mu_{2}^{2}|\log\mu_{1}|+\mu_{1}^{6}\mu_{2}^{2}d^{-2}\right)
.
And in
\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{2}
, using (
5.8
), we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}_{2}}(\delta_{2}-\varphi_{2})_{2}\left[(\delta_{1})_{x}\wedge(\delta_{1})_{y}\right]_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-512\int_{\frac{\mathcal{D}_{2}-\xi}{\mu_{1}}}\left[\mu_{2}^{2}\frac{(\mu_{1}x+\xi_{1}-\zeta_{1})(\mu_{1}y+\xi_{2}-\zeta_{2})}{|\mu_{1}z+\xi-\zeta|^{4}}+O(\mu_{2}^{6})\right]\frac{xy|z|^{2}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+256\int_{\frac{\mathcal{D}_{2}-\xi}{\mu_{1}}}\frac{\left[\mu_{2}^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}(\mu_{1}z+\xi,\zeta)+O(\mu_{2}^{5})\right]xy|z|^{2}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-512\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}\int_{\frac{\mathcal{D}_{2}-\xi}{\mu_{1}}}\frac{-3\left(\sigma_{1}^{4}-6\sigma_{2}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}+\sigma_{2}^{4}\right)}{|\sigma|^{8}}\frac{x^{2}y^{2}|z|^{2}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}+256\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}\int_{\frac{\mathcal{D}_{2}-\xi}{\mu_{1}}}\frac{x^{2}y^{2}|z|^{2}}{\left(1+|z|^{4}\right)^{3}}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\zeta)+O\left(\mu_{1}^{4}\mu_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\frac{48\pi\left(\sigma_{1}^{4}-6\sigma_{2}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}+\sigma_{2}^{4}\right)}{|\sigma|^{8}}\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}+8\pi\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\zeta)+O\left(\mu_{1}^{4}\mu_{2}^{2}\right).

Thus,

(5.11)

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta_{2}-\varphi_{2})_{2}\left[(\delta_{1})_{x}\wedge(\delta_{1})_{y}\right]_{2}=

\displaystyle\frac{48\pi\left(\sigma_{1}^{4}-6\sigma_{2}^{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}+\sigma_{2}^{4}\right)}{|\sigma|^{8}}\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}+8\pi\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\zeta)+O\left(\mu_{1}^{4}\mu_{2}^{2}\right).

The terms involving the other coefficients of the matrix
\displaystyle\mathcal{Q}_{2}
can be handled similarly. We only present the results of these estimations without delving into the detailed calculations. It follows that

(5.12)

\displaystyle q_{12}:

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta_{2}-\varphi_{2})_{2}\left[(\delta_{1})_{x}\wedge(\delta_{1})_{y}\right]_{1}=\frac{-192\pi\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}(\sigma_{1}^{2}-\sigma_{2}^{2})\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}}{|\sigma|^{8}}+8\pi\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\zeta)+O\left(\mu_{1}^{4}\mu_{2}^{2}\right),

\displaystyle q_{13}:

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta_{2}-\varphi_{2})_{3}\left[(\delta_{1})_{x}\wedge(\delta_{1})_{y}\right]_{1}=O\left(\mu_{1}^{4}\mu_{2}^{2}\right),

\displaystyle q_{21}:

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta_{2}-\varphi_{2})_{1}\left[(\delta_{1})_{x}\wedge(\delta_{1})_{y}\right]_{2}=\frac{-192\pi\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}(\sigma_{1}^{2}-\sigma_{2}^{2})\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}}{|\sigma|^{8}}+8\pi\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\zeta)+O\left(\mu_{1}^{4}\mu_{2}^{2}\right),

\displaystyle q_{23}:

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta_{2}-\varphi_{2})_{3}\left[(\delta_{1})_{x}\wedge(\delta_{1})_{y}\right]_{2}=O\left(\mu_{1}^{4}\mu_{2}^{2}\right),

\displaystyle q_{31}:

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta_{2}-\varphi_{2})_{1}\left[(\delta_{1})_{x}\wedge(\delta_{1})_{y}\right]_{3}=O\left(\mu_{1}^{4}\mu_{2}^{2}|\log\mu_{2}|+\mu_{1}^{4}\mu_{2}^{2}d^{-2}\right),

\displaystyle q_{32}:

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta_{2}-\varphi_{2})_{2}\left[(\delta_{1})_{x}\wedge(\delta_{1})_{y}\right]_{3}=O\left(\mu_{1}^{4}\mu_{2}^{2}|\log\mu_{2}|+\mu_{1}^{4}\mu_{2}^{2}d^{-2}\right),

\displaystyle q_{33}:

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta_{2}-\varphi_{2})_{3}\left[(\delta_{1})_{x}\wedge(\delta_{1})_{y}\right]_{3}=O\left(\mu_{1}^{4}\mu_{2}^{2}+\mu_{1}^{4}\mu_{2}^{4}d^{-4}\right).

Similarly, one has

(5.13)

\displaystyle 2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)\cdot\left[\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=-4\pi\varepsilon\mu_{1}^{2}\left[q_{11}\frac{\partial^{2}g^{(2)}_{1}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\omega^{(2)})+q_{12}\frac{\partial^{2}g^{(2)}_{2}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\omega^{(2)})+q_{13}\frac{\partial^{2}g^{(2)}_{3}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\omega^{(2)})\right.

\displaystyle\left.+q_{21}\frac{\partial^{2}g^{(2)}_{1}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\omega^{(2)})+q_{22}\frac{\partial^{2}g^{(2)}_{2}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\omega^{(2)})+q_{23}\frac{\partial^{2}g^{(2)}_{3}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\omega^{(2)})\right]+O\left(\varepsilon\mu_{1}^{4}\right),

and

(5.14)

\displaystyle 2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}g^{(1)}\cdot\left[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=-4\pi\varepsilon\mu_{2}^{2}\left[q_{11}\frac{\partial^{2}g^{(1)}_{1}}{\partial x^{2}}(\zeta,\omega^{(1)})+q_{12}\frac{\partial^{2}g^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial x{\partial}y}(\zeta,\omega^{(1)})+q_{21}\frac{\partial^{2}g^{(1)}_{1}}{\partial x^{2}}(\zeta,\omega^{(1)})\right.

\displaystyle\left.+q_{22}\frac{\partial^{2}g^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial x\partial y}(\zeta,\omega^{(1)})+q_{31}\frac{\partial^{2}g^{(1)}_{3}}{\partial x^{2}}(\zeta,\omega^{(1)})+q_{32}\frac{\partial^{2}g^{(1)}_{3}}{\partial x\partial y}(\zeta,\omega^{(1)})\right]+O\left(\varepsilon\mu_{2}^{4}\right).

Now, we consider

(5.15)

\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\delta_{1}+\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)\cdot\left[(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2})_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}+\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2})_{y}\right].

Note that in
\displaystyle\mathcal{D}\backslash B_{\gamma}(\zeta)
, we have

(5.16)

\displaystyle\varphi_{1}(z,\xi)-\varepsilon g^{(1)}(z,\omega^{(1)})=O\left(\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-2}+\varepsilon d^{-3}|\xi-\omega^{(1)}|\right)=O\left(\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-2}\right),

where the second equality follows from the asymptotic relation
\displaystyle\varepsilon d^{-1}|\xi-\omega^{(1)}|=O\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)
. And in
\displaystyle B_{\gamma}(\zeta)
, one has

(5.17)

\displaystyle\left|\nabla\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}(z,\omega^{(2)})\right)\right|=O\left(\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-3}+\varepsilon d^{-4}|\zeta-\omega^{(2)}|\right)=O\left(\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-3}\right).

In the following, we will frequently refer to (
5.16
) and (
5.17
) to simplify our estimates without further explanation.
It is then straightforward to verify that

(5.18)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}\backslash B_{\gamma}(\zeta)}\left(P\delta_{1}+\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)\cdot\left[(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2})_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}+\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2})_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\left(\mu_{1}^{2}|\log\mu_{1}|+\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-2}\right)\mu_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\right).

Using (
B.1
), we have

(5.19)

\displaystyle\int_{B_{\gamma}(\zeta)}\left(P\delta_{1}+\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)\cdot\left[(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2})_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}+\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2})_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\left(\mu_{1}^{2}+\varepsilon\right)\mu_{2}^{2}|\log\mu_{2}|\left(\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-3}+\varepsilon d^{-4}|\zeta-\omega^{(2)}|\right)\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(\left(\mu_{1}^{2}+\varepsilon\right)\mu_{2}^{2}|\log\mu_{2}|\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-3}\right).

Moreover,

(5.20)

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta_{1}\cdot\left[(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta))_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}-\left(\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\left(\mu_{1}^{2}|\log\mu_{1}|+\mu_{1}^{2}d^{-2}\right)\mu_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)+\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-6}\right),

(5.21)

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varepsilon g^{(1)}\cdot\left[(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta))_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}-\left(\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\varepsilon\mu_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)+\varepsilon^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}+\varepsilon\mu_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-6}+\varepsilon^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}d^{-3}\right).

And similarly, one has

\displaystyle\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}+\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)\cdot\left[(\delta_{1})_{x}\wedge\left(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi)-\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{y}+\left(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi)-\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{x}\wedge(\delta_{1})_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\cdot\left[(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi))_{x}\wedge\left(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi)-\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{y}-\left(\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi)-\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\cdot\left[(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi))_{x}\wedge\left(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi)-\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{y}-\left(\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi)\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=O\left(\left(\mu_{2}^{2}|\log\mu_{2}|+\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-2}\right)\mu_{1}^{2}\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\right)+O\left(\left(\mu_{2}^{2}+\varepsilon\right)\mu_{1}^{2}|\log\mu_{1}|\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-3}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\left(\mu_{2}^{2}|\log\mu_{2}|+\mu_{2}^{2}d^{-2}\right)\mu_{1}^{2}\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)+\mu_{2}^{2}\mu_{1}^{2}\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-6}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\varepsilon\mu_{1}^{2}\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)+\varepsilon^{2}\mu_{1}^{2}+\varepsilon\mu_{1}^{2}\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-6}+\varepsilon^{2}\mu_{1}^{2}d^{-3}\right).

Finally, we use the connection between the function
\displaystyle h^{(1)}
and the boundary function
\displaystyle g
, i.e.

g^{(2)}(z,\omega^{(2)})=\left(2h_{1}^{(1)}(z,\omega^{(2)}),2h_{2}^{(1)}(z,\omega^{(2)}),-2\varepsilon h_{3}^{(1)}(z,\omega^{(2)})\right),

(5.22)

\displaystyle\left|h^{(1)}(\xi,\zeta)-h^{(1)}\left(\xi,\omega^{(2)}\right)\right|=O\left(|\zeta-\omega^{(2)}|\right)

to obtain

(5.23)

\displaystyle 8\pi\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}^{2}\left[q_{11}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\zeta)+q_{12}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\zeta)+q_{21}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\zeta)+q_{22}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\zeta)\right]

\displaystyle-4\pi\varepsilon\mu_{1}^{2}\left[q_{11}\frac{\partial^{2}g^{(2)}_{1}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\omega^{(2)})+q_{12}\frac{\partial^{2}g^{(2)}_{2}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\omega^{(2)})+q_{13}\frac{\partial^{2}g^{(2)}_{3}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\omega^{(2)})\right.

\displaystyle\left.+q_{21}\frac{\partial^{2}g^{(2)}_{1}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\omega^{(2)})+q_{22}\frac{\partial^{2}g^{(2)}_{2}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\omega^{(2)})+q_{23}\frac{\partial^{2}g^{(2)}_{3}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\omega^{(2)})\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\mu_{1}^{2}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)+\varepsilon\mu_{1}^{2}|\zeta-\omega^{(2)}|+\varepsilon^{2}\mu_{1}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(\mu_{1}^{2}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)+\varepsilon^{2}\mu_{1}^{2}\right).

Thus, combining the fact that

\displaystyle\mu_{i}=O\left(\sqrt{\varepsilon}\right),~i=1,2
,
Lemma
5.1
follows from (
5.10
)-(
5.23
).
∎

5.2.
Interaction with the linear and higher order expansions

Referring to the energy expansion of one bubble in Section
4
, along with the results from Lemma
5.1
, it is natural to consider the mixed terms of
\displaystyle\delta_{1},\ \delta_{2},\ Z_{-1,l},\ Z_{2,l}
for
\displaystyle l=1,2
in (
5.3
). For simplicity, we express these terms as

\mathcal{M}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}:=\mathcal{M}^{1}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}+\mathcal{M}^{2}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}+\mathcal{M}^{3}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}+\mathcal{M}^{4}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}+\mathcal{M}^{5}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}},

and by applying integration by parts, each
\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{i}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}},\ i=1,\dots,5
, takes the following form

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{1}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle:=\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\cdot\nabla P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\cdot\left[\left(P\delta_{1}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\delta_{1}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle~\quad+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle~\quad+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\cdot\left[\left(P\delta_{1}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\delta_{1}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle~\quad+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}+\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle~\quad+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\cdot\left[\left(g^{(1)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(g^{(1)}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle~\quad+2\varepsilon^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\cdot\left[\left(g^{(1)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(g^{(1)}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle~\quad+2\varepsilon^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\cdot\left[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle~=-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\cdot\left[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}+\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle~\quad+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\cdot\left[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}-\left(-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}\right]

(5.24)

\displaystyle\displaystyle~\quad+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}+\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\delta_{1}+\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\delta_{1}+\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{y}\right],

and similarly

(5.25)

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{2}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}

\displaystyle:=-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\cdot\left[\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi)-\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{y}+\left(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi)-\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\cdot\left[\left(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi)\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi)-\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{y}-\left(-\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi)-\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\delta_{1}+\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}+\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}+\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}\right].

And

(5.26)

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{3}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}:

\displaystyle=2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}+\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\delta_{1}+\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right)_{y}\right],

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{4}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}:=

\displaystyle\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\cdot\nabla P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\cdot\left[\left(P\delta_{1}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\delta_{1}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\cdot\left[\left(g^{(1)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(g^{(1)}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\cdot\left[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\cdot\left[\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}\left(a_{1,l}\varphi_{-1,l}\left(z,\xi\right)+p_{1,l}\varphi_{2,l}\left(z,\xi\right)\right)\right)_{y}\right.

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\left.+\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}\left(a_{1,l}\varphi_{-1,l}\left(z,\xi\right)+p_{1,l}\varphi_{2,l}\left(z,\xi\right)\right)\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\cdot\left[\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{y}\right]

(5.27)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}+\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}+\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}\right],

(5.28)

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{5}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}:

\displaystyle=~2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\right)_{y}\right]+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{y}\right].

Lemma 5.2
.

There holds

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{1}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}=F^{(1)}_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Id,\mathcal{Q}_{2},a_{1,l},p_{1,l})+\mathcal{E}^{(1)}\left(\varepsilon,a_{1,l},p_{1,l}\right),

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{2}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}=F^{(2)}_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Id,\mathcal{Q}_{2},a_{2,l},p_{2,l})+\mathcal{E}^{(2)}\left(\varepsilon,a_{2,l},p_{2,l}\right),

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{3}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}=\mathcal{E}^{(3)}\left(\varepsilon,a_{1,l},a_{2,l},p_{1,l},p_{2,l}\right),

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{4}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}=\mathcal{E}^{(4)}\left(\varepsilon,a_{1,l},a_{2,l},p_{1,l},p_{2,l}\right),

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{5}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}=\mathcal{E}^{(5)}\left(\varepsilon,a_{1,l},a_{2,l},p_{1,l},p_{2,l}\right),

where

(5.29)

\displaystyle F^{(1)}_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Id,\mathcal{Q}_{2},a_{1,l},p_{1,l}):

\displaystyle=a_{1,1}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}^{2}\left\{q_{11}\frac{-32\pi\sigma_{1}\left(\sigma_{1}^{2}-3\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}+q_{12}\frac{-32\pi\sigma_{2}\left(3\sigma_{1}^{2}-\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+q_{21}\frac{-32\pi\sigma_{2}\left(3\sigma_{1}^{2}-\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}+q_{22}\frac{32\pi\sigma_{1}\left(\sigma_{1}^{2}-3\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}\right\}

\displaystyle+a_{1,2}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}^{2}\left\{q_{11}\frac{32\pi\sigma_{2}\left(3\sigma_{1}^{2}-\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}+q_{12}\frac{-32\pi\sigma_{1}\left(\sigma_{1}^{2}-3\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+q_{21}\frac{-32\pi\sigma_{1}\left(\sigma_{1}^{2}-3\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}+q_{22}\frac{-32\pi\sigma_{2}\left(3\sigma_{1}^{2}-\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}\right\},

(5.30)

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{(1)}\left(\varepsilon,a_{1,l},p_{1,l}\right):

\displaystyle=O\left((a_{1,1}+a_{1,2})\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-6}+\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{2}}|\log\varepsilon|d^{-2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-5}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+O\left((p_{1,1}+p_{1,2})\left(\varepsilon^{3}+\varepsilon^{3}\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-6}+\varepsilon^{4}|\log\varepsilon|d^{-5}+\varepsilon^{3}\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-8}\right)\right),

(5.31)

\displaystyle F^{(2)}_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Id,\mathcal{Q}_{2},a_{2,l},p_{2,l}):=a_{2,1}\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}\left\{q_{11}\frac{32\pi\sigma_{1}\left(\sigma_{1}^{2}-3\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}+q_{12}\frac{32\pi\sigma_{2}\left(3\sigma_{1}^{2}-\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+q_{21}\frac{32\pi\sigma_{2}\left(3\sigma_{1}^{2}-\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}+q_{22}\frac{-32\pi\sigma_{1}\left(\sigma_{1}^{2}-3\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}\right\}

\displaystyle+a_{2,2}\mu_{1}^{2}\mu_{2}\left\{q_{11}\frac{-32\pi\sigma_{2}\left(3\sigma_{1}^{2}-\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}+q_{12}\frac{32\pi\sigma_{1}\left(\sigma_{1}^{2}-3\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+q_{21}\frac{32\pi\sigma_{1}\left(\sigma_{1}^{2}-3\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}+q_{22}\frac{32\pi\sigma_{2}\left(3\sigma_{1}^{2}-\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}\right\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{(2)}\left(\varepsilon,a_{2,l},p_{2,l}\right):=O\left((a_{2,1}+a_{2,2})\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-6}+\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{2}}|\log\varepsilon|d^{-2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-5}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+O\left((p_{2,1}+p_{2,2})\left(\varepsilon^{3}+\varepsilon^{3}\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-6}+\varepsilon^{4}|\log\varepsilon|d^{-5}+\varepsilon^{3}\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-8}\right)\right),

(5.32)

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{(3)}\left(\varepsilon,a_{1,l},a_{2,l},p_{1,l},p_{2,l}\right)

\displaystyle:=O\left(\left(\left(a_{1,1}+a_{1,2}\right)^{2}+\left(a_{2,1}+a_{2,2}\right)^{2}\right)\varepsilon^{2}d^{-4}\right)+O\left(\left(\left(p_{1,1}+p_{1,2}\right)^{2}+\left(p_{2,1}+p_{2,2}\right)^{2}\right)\left(\varepsilon^{2}+\varepsilon^{5}d^{-10}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+O\left(\left(a_{1,1}+a_{1,2}\right)\left(p_{1,1}+p_{1,2}\right)\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{2}}d^{-7}\right)\right)+O\left(\left(a_{2,1}+a_{2,2}\right)\left(p_{2,1}+p_{2,2}\right)\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{2}}d^{-7}\right)\right),

(5.33)

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{(4)}\left(\varepsilon,a_{1,l},a_{2,l},p_{1,l},p_{2,l}\right)

\displaystyle:=O\left(\sum_{i,k,l=1}^{2}a_{1,k}a_{2,l}\left(\varepsilon+\varepsilon^{2}|\log\varepsilon|d^{-2}+\varepsilon\left(\mu_{i}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-5}\right)+p_{1,k}p_{2,l}\left(\varepsilon^{5}|\log\varepsilon|d^{-5}+\varepsilon^{4}\left(\mu_{i}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-8}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+O\left(\sum_{i,k,l=1}^{2}\left(a_{1,k}p_{2,l}+p_{1,k}a_{2,l}\right)\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{2}}|\log\varepsilon|d^{-5}+\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{2}}\left(\mu_{i}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-8}\right)\right),

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{(5)}\left(\varepsilon,a_{1,l},a_{2,l},p_{1,l},p_{2,l}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle:=O\left(\sum_{j,k,l=1}^{2}\left(a_{1,j}a_{2,k}a_{2,l}+a_{2,j}a_{1,k}a_{1,l}\right)\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}|\log\varepsilon|+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}d^{-4}\right)+\left(a_{1,j}a_{2,k}p_{2,l}+a_{2,j}a_{1,k}p_{1,l}\right)\left(\varepsilon+\varepsilon^{3}d^{-7}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle~\quad+O\left(\sum_{j,k,l=1}^{2}\left(a_{1,j}p_{2,k}p_{2,l}+a_{2,j}p_{1,k}p_{1,l}\right)\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{9}{2}}d^{-10}\right)+\left(p_{1,j}a_{2,k}a_{2,l}+p_{2,j}a_{1,k}a_{1,l}\right)\left(\varepsilon^{3}|\log\varepsilon|+\varepsilon^{3}d^{-4}\right)\right)

(5.34)

\displaystyle\displaystyle~\quad+O\left(\sum_{j,k,l=1}^{2}\left(p_{1,j}a_{2,k}p_{2,l}++p_{2,j}a_{1,k}p_{1,l}\right)\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{9}{2}}d^{-7}\right)+\left(p_{1,j}p_{2,k}p_{2,l}+p_{2,j}p_{1,k}p_{1,l}\right)\left(\varepsilon^{3}+\varepsilon^{6}d^{-10}\right)\right).

Proof.

Firstly, we estimate
\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{1}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}
, i.e. (
5.24
). Similar to the calculation of (
5.5
),
in
\displaystyle\mathcal{D}\backslash B_{\gamma}(\xi)
, since
\displaystyle z
is away from
\displaystyle\xi
, for
\displaystyle\mu_{1}
sufficiently small, the following expansions hold

(5.35)

\displaystyle Z_{-1,1}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)=\left(\frac{-4\mu_{1}(x-\xi_{1})}{|z-\xi|^{2}}+O(\mu_{1}^{5}),~\frac{-4\mu_{1}(y-\xi_{2})}{|z-\xi|^{2}}+O(\mu_{1}^{5}),~\frac{8\mu_{1}^{3}(x-\xi_{1})}{|z-\xi|^{4}}+O(\mu_{1}^{7})\right),

(5.36)

\displaystyle Z_{-1,2}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)=\left(\frac{4\mu_{1}(y-\xi_{2})}{|z-\xi|^{2}}+O(\mu_{1}^{5}),~\frac{-4\mu_{1}(x-\xi_{1})}{|z-\xi|^{2}}+O(\mu_{1}^{5}),~\frac{8\mu_{1}^{3}(y-\xi_{2})}{|z-\xi|^{4}}+O(\mu_{1}^{7})\right),

(5.37)

\displaystyle Z_{2,1}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{-2\mu_{1}^{4}\left((x-\xi_{1})^{4}-6(x-\xi_{1})^{2}(y-\xi_{2})^{2}+(y-\xi_{2})^{4}\right)}{|z-\xi|^{8}}+O(\mu_{1}^{8})\\
\frac{-8\mu_{1}^{4}(x-\xi_{1})(y-\xi_{2})\left((x-\xi_{1})^{2}-(y-\xi_{2})^{2}\right)}{|z-\xi|^{8}}+O(\mu_{1}^{8})\\
\frac{4\mu_{1}^{6}\left((x-\xi_{1})^{2}-(y-\xi_{2})^{2}\right)}{|z-\xi|^{8}}+O(\mu_{1}^{10})\end{pmatrix},

(5.38)

\displaystyle Z_{2,2}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{-8\mu_{1}^{4}(x-\xi_{1})(y-\xi_{2})\left((x-\xi_{1})^{2}-(y-\xi_{2})^{2}\right)}{|z-\xi|^{8}}+O(\mu_{1}^{8})\\
\frac{2\mu_{1}^{4}\left((x-\xi_{1})^{4}-6(x-\xi_{1})^{2}(y-\xi_{2})^{2}+(y-\xi_{2})^{4}\right)}{|z-\xi|^{8}}+O(\mu_{1}^{8})\\
\frac{8\mu_{1}^{6}(x-\xi_{1})(y-\xi_{2})}{|z-\xi|^{8}}+O(\mu_{1}^{10})\end{pmatrix}.

Using (
5.17
), (
5.22
) and (
5.35
)-(
5.38
) , it is direct to check that

(5.39)

\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\cdot\left[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}+\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left((a_{1,1}+a_{1,2})\left(\mu_{1}d^{-1}\mu_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)+\mu_{1}\mu_{2}^{2}|\log\mu_{2}|\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-3}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+O\left((p_{1,1}+p_{1,2})\left(\left(\mu_{1}^{2}+\mu_{1}^{4}d^{-4}\right)\mu_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)+\mu_{1}^{4}\mu_{2}^{2}|\log\mu_{2}|\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-3}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=O\left((a_{1,1}+a_{1,2})\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}|\log\varepsilon|\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-3}+(p_{1,1}+p_{1,2})\varepsilon^{3}\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-4}\right),

and

(5.40)

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\cdot\left[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}-\left(-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\varphi_{1}(z,\zeta)-\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left((a_{1,1}+a_{1,2})\left(\mu_{1}d^{-1}\mu_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)+\mu_{1}\mu_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-6})+\mu_{1}\varepsilon\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-3}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+O\left((p_{1,1}+p_{1,2})\left(\left(\mu_{1}^{2}+\mu_{1}^{4}d^{-4}\right)\mu_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)+\mu_{1}^{4}\mu_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-6}+\mu_{1}^{4}\varepsilon\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-3}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=O\left((a_{1,1}+a_{1,2})\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-6}+(p_{1,1}+p_{1,2})\varepsilon^{3}\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-6}\right).

Now, we compute the last term in (
5.24
). There holds

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}+\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\delta_{1}+\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\delta_{1}+\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}+\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)\cdot\left[\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{y}+\left(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}+\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)\cdot\left[\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}\left(a_{1,l}\varphi_{-1,l}\left(z,\xi\right)+p_{1,l}\varphi_{2,l}\left(z,\xi\right)\right)\right)_{y}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}\left(a_{1,l}\varphi_{-1,l}\left(z,\xi\right)+p_{1,l}\varphi_{2,l}\left(z,\xi\right)\right)\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}+\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)\cdot\left[\left(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi)-\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi)-\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{y}\right].

Recall that

g^{(2)}(z,\omega^{(2)})=\left(2h_{1}^{(1)}(z,\omega^{(2)}),2h_{2}^{(1)}(z,\omega^{(2)}),-2\varepsilon h_{3}^{(1)}(z,\omega^{(2)})\right),

and since
\displaystyle\varepsilon d^{-1}|\zeta-\omega^{(2)}|=O\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)
, in
\displaystyle B_{\gamma}(\zeta)
, we get

\left|\varphi_{1}\left(z,\zeta\right)-\varepsilon g^{(2)}(z,\omega^{(2)})\right|=O\left((\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)d^{-2}+\varepsilon d^{-3}|\zeta-\omega^{(2)}|\right)=O\left((\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)d^{-2}\right).

Then

\displaystyle 2\int_{B_{\gamma}(\zeta)}\left(\delta_{2}-\varphi_{1}\left(z,\zeta\right)+\varepsilon g^{(2)}(z,\omega^{(2)})\right)_{1}\cdot\left[\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(Z_{-1,1}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)\right)_{y}+\left(Z_{-1,1}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{y}\right]_{1}

\displaystyle=O\left(\mu_{1}^{3}\mu_{2}^{2}|\log\mu_{2}|+\mu_{1}^{3}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)d^{-2}\right).

And using (
5.8
) and (
B.3
), similar to the calculation of (
5.5
), in
\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{2}=\mathcal{D}\backslash B_{\gamma}(\zeta)
, we obtain

\displaystyle\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}_{2}}\left(\delta_{2}-\varphi_{1}+\varepsilon g^{(2)}(z,\omega^{(2)})\right)_{1}\cdot\left[\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(Z_{-1,1}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)\right)_{y}+\left(Z_{-1,1}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{y}\right]_{1}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=4\int_{\frac{\mathcal{D}_{2}-\xi}{\mu_{1}}}\left[\mu_{2}^{2}\frac{(\mu_{1}x+\xi_{1}-\zeta_{1})^{2}-(\mu_{1}y+\xi_{2}-\zeta_{2})^{2}}{|\mu_{1}z+\xi-\zeta|^{2}}+O(\mu_{2}^{6})\right]\frac{128x\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(x^{2}\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}-2\right)+3y^{2}\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{4}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-4\int_{\frac{\mathcal{D}_{2}-\xi}{\mu_{1}}}\left[\mu_{2}^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}(\mu_{1}z+\xi,\zeta)-\varepsilon g^{(2)}(\mu_{1}z+\xi,\omega^{(2)})+O(\mu_{2}^{3}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon))\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\frac{128x\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(x^{2}\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}-2\right)+3y^{2}\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{4}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=4\mu_{1}\mu_{2}^{2}\int_{\frac{\mathcal{D}_{2}-\xi}{\mu_{1}}}\frac{-2\sigma_{1}\left(\sigma_{1}^{2}-3\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}\frac{128x^{2}\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(x^{2}\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}-2\right)+3y^{2}\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{4}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-4\mu_{1}\int_{\frac{\mathcal{D}_{2}-\xi}{\mu_{1}}}\frac{128x^{2}\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(x^{2}\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}-2\right)+3y^{2}\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{4}}\left[\mu_{2}^{2}\frac{\partial h_{1}}{\partial x}(\xi,\zeta)-\varepsilon\frac{\partial g_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\omega^{(2)})\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\mu_{1}^{3}\mu_{2}^{2}+\mu_{1}^{3}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\frac{-32\pi\sigma_{1}\left(\sigma_{1}^{2}-3\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}^{2}-16\pi\mu_{1}\left[\mu_{2}^{2}\frac{\partial h_{1}}{\partial x}(\xi,\zeta)-\varepsilon\frac{\partial g_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\omega^{(2)})\right]+O\left(\mu_{1}^{3}\mu_{2}^{2}+\mu_{1}^{3}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\frac{-32\pi\sigma_{1}\left(\sigma_{1}^{2}-3\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}^{2}+O\left(\mu_{1}^{3}\mu_{2}^{2}+\mu_{1}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)\right),

in the last equality, we use the fact that

\left|\mu_{2}^{2}\frac{\partial h_{1}}{\partial x}(\xi,\zeta)-\varepsilon\frac{\partial g_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\omega^{(2)})\right|=O\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon+\varepsilon|\zeta-\omega^{(2)}|\right)=O\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right).

Similarly, we can obtain

\displaystyle\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\delta_{2}-\varphi_{1}+\varepsilon g^{(2)}(z,\omega^{(2)})\right)_{1}\cdot\left[\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(Z_{-1,1}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)\right)_{y}+\left(Z_{-1,1}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{y}\right]_{2}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=4\int_{\frac{\mathcal{D}_{2}-\xi}{\mu_{1}}}\left[\mu_{2}^{2}\frac{(\mu_{1}x+\xi_{1}-\zeta_{1})^{2}-(\mu_{1}y+\xi_{2}-\zeta_{2})^{2}}{|\mu_{1}z+\xi-\zeta|^{2}}+O(\mu_{2}^{6})\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\times\frac{64y\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(3x^{6}+7x^{4}y^{2}+x^{2}\left(5y^{4}-9\right)+y^{6}+y^{2}\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{4}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-4\int_{\frac{\mathcal{D}_{2}-\xi}{\mu_{1}}}\left[\mu_{2}^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}(\mu_{1}z+\xi,\zeta)-\varepsilon g^{(2)}(\mu_{1}z+\xi,\omega^{(2)})+O(\mu_{2}^{3}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon))\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\frac{64y\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(3x^{6}+7x^{4}y^{2}+x^{2}\left(5y^{4}-9\right)+y^{6}+y^{2}\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{4}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\mu_{1}^{3}\mu_{2}^{2}|\log\mu_{2}|+\mu_{1}^{3}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)d^{-2}+\mu_{1}^{3}\varepsilon d^{-3}|\zeta-\omega^{(2)}|\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=4\mu_{1}\mu_{2}^{2}\int_{\frac{\mathcal{D}_{2}-\xi}{\mu_{1}}}\frac{-2\sigma_{2}\left(3\sigma_{1}^{2}-\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}\frac{64y^{2}\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(3x^{6}+7x^{4}y^{2}+x^{2}\left(5y^{4}-9\right)+y^{6}+y^{2}\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{4}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\mu_{1}^{3}\mu_{2}^{2}+\mu_{1}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)+\mu_{1}^{3}\mu_{2}^{2}|\log\mu_{2}|+\mu_{1}^{3}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)d^{-2}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\frac{-32\pi\sigma_{2}\left(3\sigma_{1}^{2}-\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}^{2}+O\left(\mu_{1}^{3}\mu_{2}^{2}+\mu_{1}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)+\mu_{1}^{3}\mu_{2}^{2}|\log\mu_{2}|+\mu_{1}^{3}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)d^{-2}\right).

And

\displaystyle\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\delta_{2}-\varphi_{1}+\varepsilon g^{(2)}(z,\omega^{(2)})\right)_{1}\cdot\left[\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(Z_{-1,1}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)\right)_{y}+\left(Z_{-1,1}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{y}\right]_{3}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=4\int_{\frac{\mathcal{D}_{2}-\xi}{\mu_{1}}}\left[\mu_{2}^{2}\frac{(\mu_{1}x+\xi_{1}-\zeta_{1})^{2}-(\mu_{1}y+\xi_{2}-\zeta_{2})^{2}}{|\mu_{1}z+\xi-\zeta|^{2}}+O(\mu_{2}^{6})\right]\frac{32x\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{4}-8\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+3\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{4}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-4\int_{\frac{\mathcal{D}_{2}-\xi}{\mu_{1}}}\left[\mu_{2}^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}(\mu_{1}z+\xi,\zeta)-\varepsilon g^{(2)}(\mu_{1}z+\xi,\omega^{(2)})+O(\mu_{2}^{3}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon))\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\frac{32x\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{4}-8\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+3\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{4}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\mu_{1}^{3}\mu_{2}^{2}|\log\mu_{2}|+\mu_{1}^{3}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)d^{-2}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=O\left(\mu_{1}^{3}|\log\mu_{1}|\mu_{2}^{2}+\mu_{1}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)+\mu_{1}^{3}\mu_{2}^{2}|\log\mu_{2}|+\mu_{1}^{3}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)d^{-2}\right).

Follow the same path, we conclude that

\displaystyle\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}+\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)\cdot\left[\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(a_{1,1}Z_{-1,1}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)\right)_{y}+\left(a_{1,1}Z_{-1,1}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=a_{1,1}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}^{2}\left\{q_{11}\frac{-32\pi\sigma_{1}\left(\sigma_{1}^{2}-3\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}+q_{12}\frac{-32\pi\sigma_{2}\left(3\sigma_{1}^{2}-\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}\right.

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.+q_{21}\frac{-32\pi\sigma_{2}\left(3\sigma_{1}^{2}-\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}+q_{22}\frac{32\pi\sigma_{1}\left(\sigma_{1}^{2}-3\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}\right\}

(5.41)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(a_{1,1}\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{2}}|\log\varepsilon|+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)d^{-2}\right)\right).

Using (
B.4
), (
B.11
) and (

), the remaining terms in

can be estimated in a similar manner. Indeed, reasoning as (

), (

) and (

), we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}+\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)\cdot\left[\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(a_{1,2}Z_{-1,2}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)\right)_{y}+\left(a_{1,2}Z_{-1,2}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=a_{1,2}\mu_{1}\mu_{2}^{2}\left\{q_{11}\frac{32\pi\sigma_{2}\left(3\sigma_{1}^{2}-\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}+q_{12}\frac{-32\pi\sigma_{1}\left(\sigma_{1}^{2}-3\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}\right.

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.+q_{21}\frac{-32\pi\sigma_{1}\left(\sigma_{1}^{2}-3\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}+q_{22}\frac{-32\pi\sigma_{2}\left(3\sigma_{1}^{2}-\sigma_{2}^{2}\right)}{|\sigma|^{6}}\right\}

(5.42)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(a_{1,2}\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{2}}|\log\varepsilon|+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)d^{-2}\right)\right),

(5.43)

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}+\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)\cdot\left[\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{1,l}Z_{2,l}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)\right)_{y}+\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{1,l}Z_{2,l}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left((p_{1,1}+p_{1,2})\left(\varepsilon^{3}+\varepsilon^{2}\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)+\varepsilon^{3}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)d^{-2}\right)\right).

And similar to the estimate of (
5.15
), using (
B.1
), we can get

(5.44)

\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}+\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)\cdot\left[\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}\left(a_{1,l}\varphi_{-1,l}\left(z,\xi\right)+p_{1,l}\varphi_{2,l}\left(z,\xi\right)\right)\right)_{y}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}\left(a_{1,l}\varphi_{-1,l}\left(z,\xi\right)+p_{1,l}\varphi_{2,l}\left(z,\xi\right)\right)\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left((a_{1,1}+a_{1,2})\left(\left(\mu_{2}^{2}|\log\mu_{2}|+\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-2}\right)\mu_{1}^{3}+\left(\mu_{2}^{2}+\varepsilon\right)\mu_{1}^{3}|\log\mu_{1}|d^{-2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+O\left((p_{1,1}+p_{1,2})\left(\left(\mu_{2}^{2}|\log\mu_{2}|+\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-2}\right)\mu_{1}^{6}+\left(\mu_{2}^{2}+\varepsilon\right)\mu_{1}^{6}|\log\mu_{1}|d^{-5}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=O\left((a_{1,1}+a_{1,2})\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{2}}|\log\varepsilon|d^{-2}\right)+(p_{1,1}+p_{1,2})\left(\varepsilon^{3}\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-2}+\varepsilon^{4}|\log\varepsilon|d^{-5}\right)\right).

Using (

), (

),(
B.8
), (

) and (

), it is direct to check that

(5.45)

\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}+\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)\cdot\left[\left(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi)-\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\varphi_{1}(z,\xi)-\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left((a_{1,1}+a_{1,2})\left(\left(\mu_{2}^{2}|\log\mu_{2}|+\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-2}\right)\mu_{1}\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)+\left(\mu_{2}^{2}+\varepsilon\right)\mu_{1}\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-5}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+O\left((p_{1,1}+p_{1,2})\left(\mu_{2}^{2}|\log\mu_{2}|+\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-2}\right)\mu_{1}^{4}\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\right)

\displaystyle+O\left((p_{1,1}+p_{1,2})\left(\mu_{2}^{2}+\varepsilon\right)\left(\mu_{1}^{2}\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-3}+\mu_{1}^{4}\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-8}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=O\left((a_{1,1}+a_{1,2})\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}|\log\varepsilon|+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)d^{-2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}d^{-5}\right)\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\right)

\displaystyle+O\left((p_{1,1}+p_{1,2})\left(\varepsilon^{3}|\log\varepsilon|+\varepsilon^{2}\left(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-2}+\varepsilon^{2}d^{-3}+\varepsilon^{3}d^{-8}\right)\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\right).

Thus

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{1}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}

\displaystyle=(\ref{Jan17-1})+(\ref{Jan17-2})+(\ref{Jan17-3})+(\ref{Jan17-4})+(\ref{Jan17-5})+(\ref{JR-2})+(\ref{JR-1-1})

\displaystyle=F^{(1)}_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Id,\mathcal{Q}_{2},a_{1,l},p_{1,l})+\mathcal{E}^{(1)}\left(\varepsilon,a_{1,l},p_{1,l}\right),

where
\displaystyle F^{(1)}_{\mathcal{D}}(\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Id,\mathcal{Q}_{2},a_{1,l},p_{1,l})
and
\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{(1)}\left(\varepsilon,a_{1,l},p_{1,l}\right)
are defined in (
5.29
) and (
5.30
).

Note that
\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{2}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}
, i.e. (
5.25
) can be addressed similarly, and the result stated in Lemma
5.2
holds, we omit the details here.

Now, we proceed to estimate the terms in
\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{3}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}
. For simplicity, we will only present the estimate for the first term in (
5.26
), as the second term follows a similar approach.
Using (
B.5
), (
B.6
), (
B.19
) and reasoning as (
5.45
), we deduce

(5.46)

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}+\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)\cdot\left[\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{1,l}PZ_{-1,l}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{1,l}PZ_{-1,l}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\left(a_{1,1}+a_{1,2}\right)^{2}\left(\varepsilon^{2}|\log\varepsilon|+\varepsilon(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)d^{-2}+\varepsilon^{2}d^{-4}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(\left(a_{1,1}+a_{1,2}\right)^{2}\varepsilon^{2}d^{-4}\right).

By (
B.13
)-(
B.18
) and (
B.24
), it is straightforward to deduce that

(5.47)

\displaystyle 2\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{1,l}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}+\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)\cdot\left[\left(PZ_{2,l}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)\right)_{x}\wedge\left(PZ_{2,l}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\left(p_{1,1}+p_{1,2}\right)^{2}\left(\varepsilon^{5}|\log\varepsilon|+\varepsilon^{4}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)d^{-2}+\varepsilon^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}d^{-5}+\varepsilon^{5}d^{-10}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(\left(p_{1,1}+p_{1,2}\right)^{2}\left(\varepsilon^{2}+\varepsilon^{5}d^{-10}\right)\right).

As for the mixed terms of
\displaystyle a_{1,k}
and
\displaystyle p_{1,l}
,
\displaystyle k,l=1,2
, by (
B.20
), (
B.21
), (
B.22
) and (
B.23
), there holds

(5.48)

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}+\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{2}g^{(2)}\right)\cdot\left[\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{1,l}PZ_{-1,l}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{1,l}PZ_{2,l}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)\right)_{y}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{1,l}PZ_{2,l}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{1,l}PZ_{-1,l}\left(\frac{z-\xi}{\mu_{1}}\right)\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\left(a_{1,1}+a_{1,2}\right)\left(p_{1,1}+p_{1,2}\right)\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{2}}|\log\varepsilon|+\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{2}}(\mu_{2}^{2}-\varepsilon)d^{-2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{2}}d^{-2}+\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{2}}d^{-7}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(\left(a_{1,1}+a_{1,2}\right)\left(p_{1,1}+p_{1,2}\right)\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{2}}d^{-7}\right)\right).

A parallel result applies to the second term in (
5.26
). Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{3}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}=\mathcal{E}^{(3)}\left(\varepsilon,a_{1,l},a_{2,l},p_{1,l},p_{2,l}\right),

where
\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{(3)}\left(\varepsilon,a_{1,l},a_{2,l},p_{1,l},p_{2,l}\right)
is as depicted in (
5.32
).

Now, we deal with the terms in
\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{4}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}
.
Using the expansion of
\displaystyle Z_{-1,l}
and
\displaystyle Z_{2,l}
,
\displaystyle l=1,2
as well as the corresponding derivatives provided in Appendix
B
, the first term in (
5.27
) can be estimated as

\displaystyle\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\cdot\left[\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}\left(a_{1,l}\varphi_{-1,l}\left(z,\xi\right)+p_{1,l}\varphi_{2,l}\left(z,\xi\right)\right)\right)_{y}\right.

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\left.+\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}\left(a_{1,l}\varphi_{-1,l}\left(z,\xi\right)+p_{1,l}\varphi_{2,l}\left(z,\xi\right)\right)\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\delta_{1}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=O\left(\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{2,k}a_{1,l}\left(\varepsilon^{2}d^{-1}+\varepsilon^{2}|\log\varepsilon|d^{-2}\right)\right)+O\left(\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{2,k}p_{1,l}\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{2}}d^{-1}+\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{2}}|\log\varepsilon|d^{-5}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}p_{2,k}a_{1,l}\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{2}}d^{-4}+\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{2}}|\log\varepsilon|d^{-2}\right)\right)+O\left(\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}p_{2,k}p_{1,l}\left(\varepsilon^{4}+\varepsilon^{5}d^{-4}+\varepsilon^{5}|\log\varepsilon|d^{-5}\right)\right).

And similar to (
5.45
), there holds

\displaystyle\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\cdot\left[\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g^{(1)}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=O\left(\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{2,k}a_{1,l}\left(\varepsilon\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-5}\right)+a_{2,k}p_{1,l}\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{2}}\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-8}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}p_{2,k}a_{1,l}\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)+\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{2}}\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-4}\right)+p_{2,k}p_{1,l}\left(\varepsilon^{4}\left(\mu_{1}^{2}-\varepsilon\right)d^{-8}\right)\right).

A similar estimate can be applied to the last term in (
5.27
), proceeding with the necessary adjustments. It is important to note that compared to the second term in
(
5.27
), where cancellations do not occur as described above, we need to estimate some additional terms taking the following form

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\cdot\left[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\sum_{l=1}^{2}\left(a_{2,l}Z_{-1,l}\left(\frac{z-\zeta}{\mu_{2}}\right)+p_{2,l}Z_{2,l}\left(\frac{z-\zeta}{\mu_{2}}\right)\right)\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{y}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\sum_{l=1}^{2}\left(a_{2,l}Z_{-1,l}\left(\frac{z-\zeta}{\mu_{2}}\right)+p_{2,l}Z_{2,l}\left(\frac{z-\zeta}{\mu_{2}}\right)\right)\right)_{y}\right].

Using (
5.35
)-(
5.38
), (
B.3
), (
B.4
), (
B.11
) and (
B.12
), similar to the calculation in (
4.34
), (
4.36
), (
4.37
) and (
4.38
), we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\cdot\left[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\sum_{l=1}^{2}\left(a_{2,l}Z_{-1,l}\left(\frac{z-\zeta}{\mu_{2}}\right)+p_{2,l}Z_{2,l}\left(\frac{z-\zeta}{\mu_{2}}\right)\right)\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{y}\right.

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\left.+\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\delta_{2}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{2}\sum_{l=1}^{2}\left(a_{2,l}Z_{-1,l}\left(\frac{z-\zeta}{\mu_{2}}\right)+p_{2,l}Z_{2,l}\left(\frac{z-\zeta}{\mu_{2}}\right)\right)\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=O\left(\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{1,k}a_{2,l}\left(\varepsilon+\varepsilon^{2}d^{-1}\right)\right)+O\left(\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}p_{1,k}a_{2,l}\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{2}}d^{-4}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{1,k}p_{2,l}\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{2}}d^{-1}\right)\right)+O\left(\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}p_{1,k}p_{2,l}\left(\varepsilon^{4}+\varepsilon^{5}d^{-4}\right)\right).

Thus we conclude that

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{4}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}=\mathcal{E}^{(4)}\left(\varepsilon,a_{1,l},a_{2,l},p_{1,l},p_{2,l}\right),

where
\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{(4)}\left(\varepsilon,a_{1,l},a_{2,l},p_{1,l},p_{2,l}\right)
appears in (
5.33
).

Next, we estimate the terms in
\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{5}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}
, i.e. (
5.28
).
And similar to the estimate of
\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{3}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}
, refer to (
5.46
), (
5.47
) and (
5.48
), we deduce

\displaystyle\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{Q}_{2}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{2}}\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=O\left(\left(a_{2,1}+a_{2,2}\right)\left(a_{1,1}+a_{1,2}\right)^{2}\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}|\log\varepsilon|+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}d^{-4}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\left(a_{2,1}+a_{2,2}\right)\left(a_{1,1}+a_{1,2}\right)\left(p_{1,1}+p_{1,2}\right)\left(\varepsilon+\varepsilon^{3}d^{-7}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\left(a_{2,1}+a_{2,2}\right)\left(p_{1,1}+p_{1,2}\right)^{2}\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{9}{2}}d^{-10}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\left(p_{2,1}+p_{2,2}\right)\left(a_{1,1}+a_{1,2}\right)^{2}\left(\varepsilon^{3}|\log\varepsilon|+\varepsilon^{3}d^{-4}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\left(p_{2,1}+p_{2,2}\right)\left(a_{1,1}+a_{1,2}\right)\left(p_{1,1}+p_{1,2}\right)\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{9}{2}}d^{-7}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\left(p_{2,1}+p_{2,2}\right)\left(p_{1,1}+p_{1,2}\right)^{2}\left(\varepsilon^{3}+\varepsilon^{6}d^{-10}\right)\right).

The first term in
\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{5}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}
can be estimated in a similar manner. Thus, we conclude that

\mathcal{M}^{5}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}=\mathcal{E}^{(5)}\left(\varepsilon,a_{1,l},a_{2,l},p_{1,l},p_{2,l}\right)

as stated in (
5.34
).

This completes the proof of Lemma
5.2
.
∎

Now we consider the interaction associated with the higher order expansion
\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}
in
\displaystyle z_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}
,
\displaystyle i=1,2
. Specifically, for
\displaystyle i,j=1,2
and
\displaystyle i\neq j
, we need to estimate terms of the following form

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{6}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}:

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{i}+P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}\right)\cdot\nabla P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}+\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}\cdot\nabla P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{i}+P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}+\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{i}g^{(i)}\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{j}+P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}\right)_{y}\right.

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\left.+\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{j}+P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{i}+P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}+\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{i}g^{(i)}\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{j}+P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}+P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{j}+P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}+P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\delta_{i}+P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{j}g^{(j)}\right)_{y}+\left(\mathcal{Q}_{j}g^{(j)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{j}+P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}+P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{j}g^{(j)}\right)_{y}\right.

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.+\left(\mathcal{Q}_{j}g^{(j)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\delta_{j}+P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}+P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+2\varepsilon^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\mathcal{Q}_{i}g^{(i)}\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{j}g^{(j)}\right)_{y}+\left(\mathcal{Q}_{j}g^{(j)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{Q}_{j}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{j}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+2\varepsilon^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\mathcal{Q}_{i}\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}\cdot\left[\left(\mathcal{Q}_{j}g^{(j)}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\mathcal{Q}_{j}g^{(j)}\right)_{y}\right].

Similar to the estimate of
\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{i}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}
,
\displaystyle i=1,\dots,5
, we conclude that

(5.49)

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{6}_{\delta_{1},\delta_{2},Z_{-1,l},Z_{2,l}}=\mathcal{E}^{(3)}\left(\varepsilon,a_{1,l},a_{2,l},p_{1,l},p_{2,l}\right)+\mathcal{E}^{(5)}\left(\varepsilon,a_{1,l},a_{2,l},p_{1,l},p_{2,l}\right).

5.3.
Expansion for
\displaystyle k
bubbles

Given two bubbles
