\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(4)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}:=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\nabla\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}\right]+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}\right].

In particular, we provide the cancellations associated with the terms involving
\displaystyle a
and
\displaystyle p
.

\displaystyle\bullet
Cancellations of the terms containing
\displaystyle p_{k}
or
\displaystyle p_{k}p_{l}
.

Firstly, we estimate the terms that contain
\displaystyle p_{1}
in
\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(1)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}
. We start with
\displaystyle-2\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge(PZ_{2,l})_{y}+(PZ_{2,l})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]
.
Using (
B.11
) and (
B.12
), we compute
\displaystyle-2\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge\left(Z_{2,l}\right)_{y}+\left(Z_{2,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right].

Similar to (
4.37
) and (
4.38
) we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle-2\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{1}\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge(Z_{2,l})_{y}+(Z_{2,l})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-4p_{1}\mu^{2}\left[\frac{\pi^{2}}{4}\mu^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\pi^{2}}{4}\mu^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\pi}{6}\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{4}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\pi}{6}\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{4}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial y^{4}}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-4p_{1}\mu^{2}\left[\frac{\pi}{6}\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{4}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{3}\partial y}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{\pi}{6}\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{4}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x\partial y^{3}}(\xi,\xi)\right]-4p_{2}\mu^{2}\left[\frac{\pi}{6}\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(1)}_{1}}{\partial x^{3}\partial y}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{\pi}{6}\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(1)}_{1}}{\partial x{\partial}y^{3}}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-4p_{2}\mu^{2}\left[\frac{\pi^{2}}{4}\mu^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\pi^{2}}{4}\mu^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-4p_{2}\mu^{2}\left[+\frac{\pi}{12}\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\pi}{12}\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial y^{4}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\pi}{2}\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial x^{2}\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)\right]+O\left((p_{1}+p_{2})\mu^{8}d^{-8}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-\frac{8\pi}{3}p_{1}\mu^{6}\frac{\partial^{4}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{8\pi}{3}p_{2}\mu^{6}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)+O\left((p_{1}+p_{2})\mu^{8}d^{-8}\right),

where in the last equality, we have used the fact that
\displaystyle\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)=0
. Recall that

g(z,\omega)=\left(g_{1}(z,\omega),g_{2}(z,\omega),g_{3}(z,\omega)\right)=\left(2h_{1}^{(1)}(z,\omega),2h_{2}^{(1)}(z,\omega),-2\varepsilon h_{3}^{(1)}(z,\omega)\right),

then we have

\displaystyle 2\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varepsilon g\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge(Z_{2,l})_{y}+(Z_{2,l})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{8\pi}{3}p_{1}\varepsilon\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{4}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\omega)+\frac{8\pi}{3}p_{2}\varepsilon\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\omega)+O\left((p_{1}+p_{2})\left(\varepsilon\mu^{6}+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{4}\right)d^{-8}\right).

Since

\left|\frac{\partial^{4}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\omega)-\frac{\partial^{4}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)\right|=O\left(d^{-7}|\xi-\omega|\right),\qquad\left|\frac{\partial^{4}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x^{4}}(\xi,\omega)-\frac{\partial^{4}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)\right|=O\left(d^{-7}|\xi-\omega|\right),

one has

\displaystyle-2\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge(PZ_{2,l})_{y}+(PZ_{2,l})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{8\pi}{3}p_{1}\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{4}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{8\pi}{3}p_{2}\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu^{4}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)

\displaystyle+O\left((p_{1}+p_{2})\left(\varepsilon\mu^{4}d^{-7}|\xi-\omega|+\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu^{4}\left(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon\right)d^{-8}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=O\left((p_{1}+p_{2})\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu^{4}d^{-6}\right).

Here we employ the derivatives of
\displaystyle h^{(1)}
established in Lemma
2.2
, and leverage the asymptotic relation
\displaystyle\varepsilon d^{-1}|\xi-\omega|=O\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)
, which persists throughout subsequent analysis. This allows us to bound the term
\displaystyle\varepsilon\mu^{4}d^{-7}|\xi-\omega|
as
\displaystyle O\left((\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\mu^{4}d^{-6}\right)
. Similar to (
4.27
), we have

\displaystyle 2\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge\left(\varphi_{2,l}\right)_{y}+\left(\varphi_{2,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left((p_{1}+p_{2})\left(\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu^{8}|\log\mu|d^{-10}+\varepsilon\mu^{8}d^{-11}|\log\mu||\xi-\omega|+\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu^{6}d^{-8}+\varepsilon\mu^{6}d^{-9}|\xi-\omega|\right)\right).

Thus we obtain

(A.6)

\displaystyle-2\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge(PZ_{2,l})_{y}+(PZ_{2,l})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]=O\left((p_{1}+p_{2})\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu^{4}d^{-6}\right).

For the remaining terms, we deduce

(A.7)

\displaystyle 2p_{1}\int_{\mathcal{D}}PZ_{2,1}\cdot\left[(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=-8p_{1}\mu^{8}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{-2(x^{4}-6x^{2}y^{2}+y^{4}-1)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{2}}\left[\frac{{\partial}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x}(\xi,\xi)\frac{{\partial}h_{3}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{{\partial}h_{3}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x}(\xi,\xi)\frac{{\partial}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)+O\left(\mu^{2}d^{-10}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+O\left(p_{1}\mu^{10}d^{-12}\right)

\displaystyle=-4\pi^{2}p_{1}\mu^{8}\left[\frac{{\partial}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x}(\xi,\xi)\frac{{\partial}h_{3}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{{\partial}h_{3}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x}(\xi,\xi)\frac{{\partial}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)\right]+O\left(p_{1}\mu^{10}d^{-12}\right).

Using (
B.13
), (
B.14
), (
B.15
) and (
B.16
), it is easy to check that

\displaystyle\displaystyle-2\varepsilon p_{1}\int_{\mathcal{D}}g\cdot\left[\left(PZ_{2,1}\right)_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}+(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge\left(PZ_{2,1}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=2\pi^{2}\varepsilon p_{1}\mu^{6}\left[\frac{\partial g_{2}}{\partial x}(\xi,\omega)\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{3}}{\partial y}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{3}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)\frac{\partial g_{2}}{\partial y}(\xi,\omega)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-2\pi^{2}\varepsilon p_{1}\mu^{4}\left[\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)\frac{\partial g_{3}}{\partial y}(\xi,\omega)-\frac{\partial g_{3}}{\partial x}(\xi,\omega)\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial y}(\xi,\xi)\right]+O\left(p_{1}\left(\varepsilon\mu^{8}d^{-12}+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{6}d^{-12}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=4\pi^{2}\varepsilon p_{1}\mu^{6}\left[\frac{{\partial}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x}(\xi,\xi)\frac{{\partial}h_{3}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{{\partial}h_{3}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x}(\xi,\xi)\frac{{\partial}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+4\pi^{2}\varepsilon^{2}p_{1}\mu^{4}\left[\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{3}}{\partial y}(\xi,\omega)-\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{3}}{\partial x}(\xi,\omega)\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial y}(\xi,\xi)\right]

(A.8)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\href

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(p_{1}\left(\varepsilon\mu^{6}d^{-9}|\xi-\omega|+\varepsilon\mu^{8}d^{-12}+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{6}d^{-12}\right)\right).

In the last equality, we use the following estimate

(A.9)

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\partial h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{\partial h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\omega)\right|=O\left(d^{-4}|\xi-\omega|\right),\quad\left|\frac{\partial h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial y}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{\partial h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial y}(\xi,\omega)\right|=O\left(d^{-4}|\xi-\omega|\right).

Similar to (
A.7
), using (
A.9
), we obtain

(A.10)

\displaystyle 2\varepsilon^{2}p_{1}\int_{\mathcal{D}}PZ_{2,1}\cdot\left(g_{x}\wedge g_{y}\right)

\displaystyle=\pi^{2}p_{1}\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{2}\left[\frac{{\partial}g_{2}}{{\partial}x}(\xi,\omega)\frac{{\partial}g_{3}}{{\partial}y}(\xi,\omega)-\frac{{\partial}g_{3}}{{\partial}x}(\xi,\omega)\frac{{\partial}g_{2}}{{\partial}y}(\xi,\omega)\right]

\displaystyle+O\left(p_{1}\left(\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{6}d^{-12}+\varepsilon^{3}\mu^{4}d^{-12}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=-4\pi^{2}\varepsilon^{3}p_{1}\mu^{2}\left[\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{3}}{\partial y}(\xi,\omega)-\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{3}}{\partial x}(\xi,\omega)\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial y}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle+O\left(p_{1}\varepsilon^{3}\mu^{2}d^{-9}|\xi-\omega|+p_{1}\left(\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{6}d^{-12}+\varepsilon^{3}\mu^{4}d^{-12}\right)\right).

Combining (
A.7
), (
A.8
), (
A.10
) and using the fact that
\displaystyle\varepsilon d^{-1}|\xi-\omega|=O\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)
, we obtain

\displaystyle\displaystyle 2p_{1}\int_{\mathcal{D}}PZ_{2,1}\cdot\left[(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}\right]-2\varepsilon p_{1}\int_{\mathcal{D}}g\cdot\left[\left(PZ_{2,1}\right)_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}+(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge\left(PZ_{2,1}\right)_{y}\right]+2\varepsilon^{2}p_{1}\int_{\mathcal{D}}PZ_{2,1}\cdot\left(g_{x}\wedge g_{y}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-4\pi^{2}p_{1}(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\mu^{6}\left[\frac{{\partial}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x}(\xi,\xi)\frac{{\partial}h_{3}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{{\partial}h_{3}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x}(\xi,\xi)\frac{{\partial}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+4\pi^{2}p_{1}(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{2}\left[\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{3}}{\partial y}(\xi,\omega)-\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{3}}{\partial x}(\xi,\omega)\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial y}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(p_{1}(\varepsilon\mu^{6}+\varepsilon^{3}\mu^{2})d^{-9}|\xi-\omega|+p_{1}(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\left(\mu^{8}+\varepsilon\mu^{6}+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{4}\right)d^{-12}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(p_{1}\left(\varepsilon\mu^{8}+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{6}+\varepsilon^{3}\mu^{4}\right)d^{-13}|\xi-\omega|\right)

(A.11)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=O\left(p_{1}(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\left((\mu^{6}+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{2})d^{-8}+\left(\mu^{8}+\varepsilon\mu^{6}+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{4}\right)d^{-12}\right)\right).

Similarly, for the corresponding terms that involve
\displaystyle p_{2}
, there holds

(A.12)

\displaystyle 2p_{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}PZ_{2,2}\cdot\left[(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}\right]-2\varepsilon p_{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}g\cdot\left[\left(PZ_{2,2}\right)_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}+(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge\left(PZ_{2,2}\right)_{y}\right]+2\varepsilon^{2}p_{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}PZ_{2,2}\cdot\left(g_{x}\wedge g_{y}\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(p_{2}(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\left((\mu^{6}+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{2})d^{-8}+\left(\mu^{8}+\varepsilon\mu^{6}+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{4}\right)d^{-12}\right)\right).

From (
A.6
), (
A.11
) and (
A.12
), we conclude that compared to the terms containing
\displaystyle p_{l}
,
\displaystyle l=1,2
in Subsection
4.1
, the corresponding terms in
\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(1)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}
are of higher order.

Secondly, we consider the terms containing
\displaystyle p_{k}p_{l}
in
\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(2)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}
. Using (
B.11
) and reasoning as (
4.39
) and (
4.37
), we can get

(A.13)

\displaystyle-p_{1}p_{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}L_{\delta}\left[PZ_{2,2}\right]\cdot\left(PZ_{2,1}\right)

\displaystyle=-2p_{1}p_{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{2,2}\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge(Z_{2,1})_{y}+(Z_{2,1})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]+O\left(p_{1}p_{2}\mu^{10}d^{-10}\right)

\displaystyle=-\frac{8\pi}{3}p_{1}p_{2}\mu^{8}\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,2)}_{1}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)+O\left(p_{1}p_{2}\mu^{10}d^{-10}\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(p_{1}p_{2}\mu^{10}d^{-10}\right),

where we use the fact that
\displaystyle\frac{\partial^{4}h^{(2,2)}_{1}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)=0
.
Next, we need to estimate the following terms

\displaystyle\displaystyle-2\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}p_{k}p_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[(PZ_{2,k})_{x}\wedge(PZ_{2,l})_{y}+\left(\delta_{x}\wedge\left(PZ^{2,2}_{k,l}\right)_{y}+\left(PZ^{2,2}_{k,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle+2\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}p_{k}p_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}PZ^{2,2}_{k,l}\cdot\left[(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}\right]

(A.14)

\displaystyle\displaystyle-2\varepsilon\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}p_{k}p_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}g\cdot\left[(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge\left(PZ^{2,2}_{k,l}\right)_{y}+\left(PZ^{2,2}_{k,l}\right)_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}\right]+2\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}p_{k}p_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}PZ^{2,2}_{k,l}\cdot\left[g_{x}\wedge g_{y}\right].

Recall that

Z^{2,2}_{k,l}:=\frac{{\partial}^{2}\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}}{{\partial}p_{k}{\partial}p_{l}}\Bigg|_{a=0,p=0}.

We begin by considering the terms containing
\displaystyle p_{1}^{2}
in (

).
Specifically, we have

(A.15)

\displaystyle-2p_{1}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[(PZ_{2,1})_{x}\wedge(PZ_{2,1})_{y}+\left(\delta_{x}\wedge(PZ^{2,2}_{1,1})_{y}+(PZ^{2,2}_{1,1})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=-2p_{1}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[(Z_{2,1})_{x}\wedge(Z_{2,1})_{y}+\left(\delta_{x}\wedge(Z^{2,2}_{1,1})_{y}+(Z^{2,2}_{1,1})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+2p_{1}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[(Z_{2,1})_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{2,1})_{y}+(\varphi_{2,1})_{x}\wedge(Z_{2,1})_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+O\left(p_{1}^{2}(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\left(\mu^{8}+\mu^{6}(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)\right)d^{-12}+p_{1}^{2}\varepsilon\mu^{8}d^{-13}|\xi-\omega|\right).

Using (
B.28
), we obtain

\displaystyle\displaystyle-2p_{1}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{1}\cdot\left[(Z_{2,1})_{x}\wedge(Z_{2,1})_{y}+\left(\delta_{x}\wedge(Z^{2,2}_{1,1})_{y}+(Z^{2,2}_{1,1})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-4p_{1}^{2}\mu^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left[h_{1}^{(1)}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\mu^{2}}{2}\left(x^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+y^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)\right)\right.

\displaystyle\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad+\frac{\mu^{4}}{24}\left(x^{4}\frac{{\partial}^{4}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)+6x^{2}y^{2}\frac{{\partial}^{4}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x^{2}{\partial}y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+y^{4}\frac{{\partial}^{4}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y^{4}}(\xi,\xi)\right)\right.

\displaystyle\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad+\frac{\mu^{6}}{720}\left(x^{6}\frac{{\partial}^{6}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x^{6}}(\xi,\xi)+15x^{4}y^{2}\frac{{\partial}^{6}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x^{4}{\partial}y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+15x^{2}y^{4}\frac{{\partial}^{6}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x^{2}{\partial}y^{4}}(\xi,\xi)+y^{6}\frac{{\partial}^{6}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y^{6}}(\xi,\xi)\right)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\frac{-96(x-y)(x+y)\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(5x^{4}-22x^{2}y^{2}+5y^{4}-3\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{5}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-4p_{1}^{2}\mu^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left[\mu^{2}xy\frac{\partial^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x\partial y}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\mu^{4}}{24}\left(4x^{3}y\frac{{\partial}^{4}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x^{3}{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)+4xy^{3}\frac{{\partial}^{4}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x{\partial}y^{3}}(\xi,\xi)\right)\right.

\displaystyle\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad+\frac{\mu^{6}}{720}\left(6x^{5}y\frac{{\partial}^{6}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x^{5}{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)+20x^{3}y^{3}\frac{{\partial}^{6}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x^{3}{\partial}y^{3}}(\xi,\xi)+6xy^{5}\frac{{\partial}^{6}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x{\partial}y^{5}}(\xi,\xi)\right)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\frac{-192xy\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(7x^{4}-18x^{2}y^{2}+7y^{4}-1\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{5}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-2p_{1}^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left[1-2\mu^{4}\left[h_{3}^{(1)}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\mu^{2}}{2}\left(x^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{3}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+y^{2}\frac{\partial^{2}h_{3}^{(1)}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)\right)\right.\right.

\displaystyle\displaystyle\left.\left.\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{\mu^{4}}{24}\left(x^{4}\frac{{\partial}^{4}h_{3}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)+6x^{2}y^{2}\frac{{\partial}^{4}h_{3}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x^{2}{\partial}y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+y^{4}\frac{{\partial}^{4}h_{3}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y^{4}}(\xi,\xi)\right)\right]\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\times\frac{-32\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(5x^{8}-4x^{6}y^{2}-x^{4}\left(18y^{4}+17\right)+x^{2}\left(38y^{2}-4y^{6}\right)+5y^{8}-17y^{4}+2\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{5}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(p_{1}^{2}\mu^{10}d^{-10}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\frac{\pi^{2}}{2}p_{1}^{2}\mu^{6}\left[\frac{\partial^{2}h_{3}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\partial^{2}h_{3}^{(1)}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)\right]+\frac{3\pi^{2}}{32}p_{1}^{2}\mu^{6}\left[\frac{\partial^{4}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{\partial^{4}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial y^{4}}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\pi^{2}}{16}p_{1}^{2}\mu^{6}\left[\frac{\partial^{4}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{3}{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\partial^{4}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x{\partial}y^{3}}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\pi}{30}p_{1}^{2}\mu^{8}\left[\frac{\partial^{6}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{6}}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{\partial^{6}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y^{6}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\partial^{6}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{5}{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\partial^{6}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x{\partial}y^{5}}(\xi,\xi)\right]+O\left(p_{1}^{2}\mu^{10}d^{-10}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\frac{\pi}{15}p_{1}^{2}\mu^{8}\left[\frac{\partial^{6}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{6}}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{\partial^{6}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y^{6}}(\xi,\xi)\right]+O\left(p_{1}^{2}\mu^{10}d^{-10}\right).

Since
\displaystyle g(z,\omega)=\left(2h_{1}^{(1)}(z,\omega),2h_{2}^{(1)}(z,\omega),-2\varepsilon h_{3}^{(1)}(z,\omega)\right)
, we deduce

(A.16)

\displaystyle-2p_{1}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[(Z_{2,1})_{x}\wedge(Z_{2,1})_{y}+\left(\delta_{x}\wedge(Z^{2,2}_{1,1})_{y}+(Z^{2,2}_{1,1})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi}{15}p_{1}^{2}(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\mu^{6}\left[\frac{\partial^{6}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{6}}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{\partial^{6}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y^{6}}(\xi,\xi)\right]+O\left(p_{1}^{2}\varepsilon\mu^{6}d^{-9}|\xi-\omega|+p_{1}^{2}(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\left(\mu^{8}+\mu^{6}\left(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon\right)\right)d^{-10}\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(p_{1}^{2}(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\mu^{6}d^{-8}\right).

For the second term in (
A.15
), similar to the calculation of (
A.8
), one has

\displaystyle\displaystyle 2p_{1}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[(Z_{2,1})_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{2,1})_{y}+(\varphi_{2,1})_{x}\wedge(Z_{2,1})_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-4\pi^{2}p_{1}^{2}\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu^{8}\left[\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)\frac{\partial h^{(2,1)}_{3}}{\partial y}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{\partial h^{(2,1)}_{3}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial y}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-4\pi^{2}p_{1}^{2}\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu^{6}\left(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon\right)\left[\frac{\partial h^{(2,1)}_{2}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{3}}{\partial y}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{3}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)\frac{\partial h^{(2,1)}_{2}}{\partial y}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(p_{1}^{2}\left(\mu^{8}\varepsilon d^{-11}|\xi-\omega|+\mu^{6}\varepsilon^{2}d^{-11}|\xi-\omega|+\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\left(\mu^{10}+\mu^{8}\left(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon\right)\right)d^{-12}\right)\right)

(A.17)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=O\left(p_{1}^{2}\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu^{6}(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)d^{-10}\right).

Moreover, similar to the estimate of (
A.11
), there holds

(A.18)

\displaystyle 2p_{1}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}PZ^{2,2}_{1,1}\cdot\left[(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}\right]

\displaystyle-2\varepsilon p_{1}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}g\cdot\left[(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge\left(PZ^{2,2}_{1,1}\right)_{y}+\left(PZ^{2,2}_{1,1}\right)_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}\right]+2\varepsilon^{2}p_{1}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}PZ^{2,2}_{1,1}\cdot\left[g_{x}\wedge g_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=2\pi^{2}p_{1}^{2}(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\mu^{4}\left[\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{1}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial y}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{1}}{\partial y}(\xi,\xi)\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle-2\pi^{2}p_{1}^{2}(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\varepsilon\mu^{2}\left[\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{1}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial y}(\xi,\omega)-\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{1}}{\partial y}(\xi,\omega)\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle+O\left(p_{1}^{2}(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\left(\left(\mu^{10}+\mu^{8}(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)\right)d^{-14}+\varepsilon\mu^{8}d^{-12}+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{6}d^{-10}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(p_{1}^{2}(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\left((\mu^{4}+\varepsilon\mu^{2})d^{-6}+(\mu^{8}(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{6})d^{-14}\right)\right).

Therefore, combining (
A.15
), (
A.16
), (
A.17
) and (
A.18
), we conclude that as
\displaystyle|\xi|\to 1
, with
\displaystyle\mu^{2}-\varepsilon=O\left(\varepsilon d_{\varepsilon}^{4}\right)
and
\displaystyle d_{\varepsilon}=O(\mu^{\frac{2}{3}-\alpha})
for

small, compared to (

), the terms in
\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(2)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}
that contain
\displaystyle p_{1}^{2}
are of higher order.

Follow the same path, using (
B.29
) and (
B.30
), we obtain

\displaystyle\displaystyle-2p_{2}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{1}\cdot\left[(PZ_{2,2})_{x}\wedge(PZ_{2,2})_{y}+\left(\delta_{x}\wedge(PZ^{2,2}_{2,2})_{y}+(PZ^{2,2}_{2,2})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\frac{\pi^{2}}{2}p_{2}^{2}\mu^{6}\left[\frac{\partial^{2}h_{3}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\partial^{2}h_{3}^{(1)}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)\right]+\frac{\pi^{2}}{32}p_{2}^{2}\mu^{6}\left[\frac{\partial^{4}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{\partial^{4}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial y^{4}}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\frac{3\pi^{2}}{16}p_{2}^{2}\mu^{6}\left[\frac{\partial^{4}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{3}{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\partial^{4}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x{\partial}y^{3}}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\pi}{120}p_{2}^{2}\mu^{8}\left[\frac{\partial^{6}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{6}}(\xi,\xi)+5\frac{\partial^{6}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{4}y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)-5\frac{\partial^{6}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}y^{4}}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{\partial^{6}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y^{6}}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\pi}{20}p_{2}^{2}\mu^{8}\left[\frac{\partial^{6}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{5}{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{10}{3}\frac{\partial^{6}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{3}{\partial}y^{3}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\partial^{6}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x{\partial}y^{5}}(\xi,\xi)\right]+O\left(p_{2}^{2}\mu^{10}d^{-10}+p_{1}^{2}\mu^{10}d^{-12}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-\frac{\pi}{15}p_{2}^{2}\mu^{8}\left[\frac{\partial^{6}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{6}}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{\partial^{6}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y^{6}}(\xi,\xi)\right]+O\left(p_{2}^{2}\mu^{10}d^{-10}+p_{2}^{2}\mu^{10}d^{-12}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=O\left(p_{2}^{2}\mu^{8}d^{-8}+p_{2}^{2}\mu^{10}d^{-12}\right),

then we deduce

(A.19)

\displaystyle-2p_{2}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[(PZ_{2,2})_{x}\wedge(PZ_{2,2})_{y}+\left(\delta_{x}\wedge(PZ^{2,2}_{2,2})_{y}+(PZ^{2,2}_{2,2})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(p_{2}^{2}(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\left(\mu^{6}d^{-8}+\mu^{6}(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)d^{-12}\right)\right).

And

\displaystyle\displaystyle-2p_{1}p_{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{1}\cdot\left[\left(PZ_{2,1}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(PZ_{2,2}\right)_{y}+\left(PZ_{2,2}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(PZ_{2,1}\right)_{y}+\delta_{x}\wedge(PZ^{2,2}_{1,2})_{y}+(PZ^{2,2}_{1,2})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\frac{\pi^{2}}{8}p_{1}p_{2}\mu^{6}\left[\frac{\partial^{4}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{3}{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\partial^{4}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x{\partial}y^{3}}(\xi,\xi)\right]+\frac{\pi^{2}}{16}p_{1}p_{2}\mu^{6}\left[\frac{\partial^{4}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{\partial^{4}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y^{4}}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\pi}{15}p_{1}p_{2}\mu^{8}\left[\frac{\partial^{6}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{5}{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{\partial^{6}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial xy^{5}}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\pi}{60}p_{1}p_{2}\mu^{8}\left[\frac{\partial^{6}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{6}}(\xi,\xi)+5\frac{\partial^{6}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{4}{\partial}y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)-5\frac{\partial^{6}h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{2}{\partial}y^{4}}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{\partial^{6}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y^{6}}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(p_{1}p_{2}\mu^{10}d^{-10}+p_{1}p_{2}\mu^{10}d^{-12}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-\frac{\pi}{15}p_{1}p_{2}\mu^{8}\left[\frac{\partial^{6}h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x^{6}}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{\partial^{6}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y^{6}}(\xi,\xi)\right]+O\left(p_{1}p_{2}\mu^{10}d^{-10}+p_{1}p_{2}\mu^{10}d^{-12}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=O\left(p_{1}p_{2}\mu^{8}d^{-8}+p_{1}p_{2}\mu^{10}d^{-12}\right),

then

(A.20)

\displaystyle-2p_{1}p_{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[\left(PZ_{2,1}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(PZ_{2,2}\right)_{y}+\left(PZ_{2,2}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(PZ_{2,1}\right)_{y}+\delta_{x}\wedge(PZ^{2,2}_{1,2})_{y}+(PZ^{2,2}_{1,2})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(p_{1}p_{2}(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\left(\mu^{6}d^{-8}+\mu^{6}(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)d^{-12}\right)\right).

And similar to (
A.18
), one has

(A.21)

\displaystyle 2p_{2}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}PZ^{2,2}_{2,2}\cdot\left[(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}\right]+2p_{1}p_{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}PZ^{2,2}_{1,2}\cdot\left[(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}\right]

\displaystyle-2\varepsilon p_{2}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}g\cdot\left[\left(PZ^{2,2}_{2,2}\right)_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}+(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge\left(PZ^{2,2}_{2,2}\right)_{y}\right]+2\varepsilon^{2}p_{2}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}PZ^{2,2}_{2,2}\cdot\left[g_{x}\wedge g_{y}\right]

\displaystyle-2\varepsilon p_{1}p_{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}g\cdot\left[\left(PZ^{2,2}_{1,2}\right)_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}+(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge\left(PZ^{2,2}_{1,2}\right)_{y}\right]+2\varepsilon^{2}p_{1}p_{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}PZ^{2,2}_{1,2}\cdot\left[g_{x}\wedge g_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\left(p_{2}^{2}+p_{1}p_{2}\right)(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\left((\mu^{4}+\varepsilon\mu^{2})d^{-6}+(\mu^{8}(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{6})d^{-14}\right)\right).

Therefore, compared to the results in Subsection
4.1
, the terms in
\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(2)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}
that contain
\displaystyle p_{2}^{2}
and
\displaystyle p_{1}p_{2}
are also of higher order.

\displaystyle\bullet
Cancellations of the terms containing
\displaystyle a_{k}
or
\displaystyle a_{k}a_{l}
.

We now turn to the terms in (
A.2
) that contain
\displaystyle a_{l}
,
\displaystyle l=1,2
. We begin with

\displaystyle-2\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge(PZ_{-1,l})_{y}+(PZ_{-1,l})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right].

Similar to (
4.27
), we have

\displaystyle 2\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge\left(\varphi_{-1,l}\right)_{y}+\left(\varphi_{-1,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left((a_{1}+a_{2})\left(\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu^{3}d^{-5}+\varepsilon\mu^{3}d^{-6}|\xi-\omega|\right)\right).

For
\displaystyle-2\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge\left(Z_{-1,l}\right)_{y}+\left(Z_{-1,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]
, we omit the detailed calculation, as it closely resembles the calculations in (
4.34
) and (
4.36
). The key difference lies in the definition of
\displaystyle\varphi_{1}
and
\displaystyle\varepsilon g

and the associated changes in the constants. Thus we conclude that

(A.22)

\displaystyle-2\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge(PZ_{-1,l})_{y}+(PZ_{-1,l})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=-32\pi a_{1}\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu\frac{\partial h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)-32\pi a_{2}\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu\frac{\partial h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)+O\left((a_{1}+a_{2})\left(\varepsilon\mu d^{-4}|\xi-\omega|+\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu^{3}d^{-5}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=O\left((a_{1}+a_{2})\left(\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu d^{-3}\right)\right).

And similar to (
A.11
), we can obtain

(A.23)

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{l}PZ_{-1,l}\right)\cdot[(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}]-2\varepsilon\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}g\cdot\left[\left(PZ_{-1,l}\right)_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}+(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge\left(PZ_{-1,l}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+2\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}PZ_{-1,l}\cdot\left(g_{x}\wedge g_{y}\right)

\displaystyle=O\left((a_{1}+a_{2})(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\left(\mu^{3}\left(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon\right)+\varepsilon^{2}\mu\right)d^{-9}\right).

For the terms that contain
\displaystyle a_{k}a_{l}
,
\displaystyle k,l=1,2
, using (
B.3
), computing analogously with (
4.35
), we have

(A.24)

\displaystyle-a_{1}a_{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}L_{\delta}\left[PZ_{-1,2}\right]\cdot\left(PZ_{-1,1}\right)

\displaystyle=-2a_{1}a_{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{-1,2}\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge\left(PZ_{-1,1}\right)_{y}+\left(PZ_{-1,1}\right)_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=-32\pi a_{1}a_{2}\mu^{2}\frac{\partial h^{(-1,2)}_{1}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)+O\left(a_{1}a_{2}\mu^{4}d^{-4}\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(a_{1}a_{2}\mu^{4}d^{-4}\right),

where we use the facts stated in Lemma
2.2
, i.e.
\displaystyle\frac{\partial h^{(-1,2)}_{1}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)=0.

Moreover, from (
B.25
), (
B.26
) and (
B.27
), it is easy to see that

\displaystyle\displaystyle-2\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{k}a_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{1}\cdot\left[(PZ_{-1,k})_{x}\wedge(PZ_{-1,l})_{y}+\left(\delta_{x}\wedge(PZ^{-1,-1}_{k,l})_{y}+(PZ^{-1,-1}_{k,l})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-4a_{1}^{2}\mu^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left[h_{1}^{(1)}(\xi,\xi)+O\left(\mu^{2}d^{-4}\right)\right]\frac{-192\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)f_{3}(x,y)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{5}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-4a_{2}^{2}\mu^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left[h_{1}^{(1)}(\xi,\xi)+O\left(\mu^{2}d^{-4}\right)\right]\frac{192\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)f_{6}(x,y)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{5}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-4a_{1}a_{2}\mu^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\left[h^{(1)}_{2}(\xi,\xi)+O\left(\mu^{2}d^{-4}\right)\right]\frac{-192\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)f_{9}(x,y)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{5}}+O\left(\left(a_{1}+a_{2}\right)^{2}\mu^{6}d^{-8}\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=O\left(\left(a_{1}+a_{2}\right)^{2}\mu^{4}d^{-4}+\mu^{6}d^{-8}\right),

thus

\displaystyle\displaystyle-2\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{k}a_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[(PZ_{-1,k})_{x}\wedge(PZ_{-1,l})_{y}+\left(\delta_{x}\wedge(PZ^{-1,-1}_{k,l})_{y}+(PZ^{-1,-1}_{k,l})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=O\left(\left(a_{1}+a_{2}\right)^{2}\left(\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\left(\mu^{2}d^{-4}+\mu^{2}(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)d^{-8}\right)+\mu^{2}\varepsilon d^{-5}|\xi-\omega|+\mu^{2}\varepsilon^{2}d^{-9}|\xi-\omega|\right)\right)

(A.25)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=O\left(\left(a_{1}+a_{2}\right)^{2}\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu^{2}(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)d^{-8}\right).

And similar to (
A.18
), there holds

(A.26)

\displaystyle 2\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{k}a_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}PZ^{-1,-1}_{k,l}\cdot\left[(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}\right]

\displaystyle-2\varepsilon\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{k}a_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}g\cdot\left[(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge\left(PZ^{-1,-1}_{k,l}\right)_{y}+\left(PZ^{-1,-1}_{k,l}\right)_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}\right]+2\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{k}a_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}PZ^{-1,-1}_{k,l}\cdot\left[g_{x}\wedge g_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\left(a_{1}+a_{2}\right)^{2}\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\left((\mu^{6}+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{2})d^{-8}+(\mu^{4}|\log\mu|+\varepsilon\mu^{2}|\log\mu|)d^{-6}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+O\left(\left(a_{1}+a_{2}\right)^{2}(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\left(\mu^{6}(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{4}\right)d^{-12}\right).

Thus, compared to the results in Subsection
4.1
, the terms in
\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(1)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}
and
\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(2)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}
that involve
\displaystyle a_{k}
and
\displaystyle a_{k}a_{l}
,
\displaystyle k,l=1,2
are of higher order.

\displaystyle\bullet
Cancellations of the terms containing
\displaystyle a_{k}p_{l}
.

Now, we deal with the mixed terms that contain
\displaystyle a_{k}p_{l}
for
\displaystyle k,l=1,2
in
\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(2)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}
.
Firstly, using (
B.31
), we obtain

\displaystyle-2a_{1}p_{1}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[\left(PZ_{-1,1}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(PZ_{2,1}\right)_{y}+\left(PZ_{2,1}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(PZ_{-1,1}\right)_{y}+\delta_{x}\wedge\left(PZ^{-1,2}_{1,1}\right)_{y}+\left(PZ^{-1,2}_{1,1}\right)_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=-2a_{1}p_{1}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[\left(Z_{-1,1}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(Z_{2,1}\right)_{y}+\left(Z_{2,1}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(Z_{-1,1}\right)_{y}+\delta_{x}\wedge\left(Z^{-1,2}_{1,1}\right)_{y}+\left(Z^{-1,2}_{1,1}\right)_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+O\left(a_{1}p_{1}\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\left(\mu^{3}d^{-5}+\mu^{5}(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)d^{-11}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(a_{1}p_{1}\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\left(\mu^{3}d^{-5}+\mu^{5}(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)d^{-11}\right)\right).

The remaining terms can be estimated in a similar manner, and we omit the details here for brevity. There holds

(A.27)

\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{l}PZ_{-1,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}PZ_{2,l}\right)_{y}+\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}PZ_{2,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{l}PZ_{-1,l}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{k}p_{l}PZ^{-1,2}_{k,l}\right)_{y}+\left(\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{k}p_{l}PZ^{-1,2}_{k,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left((a_{1}+a_{2})(p_{1}+p_{2})\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\left(\mu^{3}d^{-5}+\mu^{5}(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)d^{-11}\right)\right).

And

(A.28)

\displaystyle 2\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{k}p_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}PZ^{-1,2}_{k,l}\cdot\left[(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}\right]

\displaystyle-2\varepsilon\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{k}p_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}g\cdot\left[(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge\left(PZ^{-1,2}_{k,l}\right)_{y}+\left(PZ^{-1,2}_{k,l}\right)_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}\right]+2\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{k}p_{l}\int_{\mathcal{D}}PZ^{-1,2}_{k,l}\cdot\left[g_{x}\wedge g_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left((a_{1}+a_{2})(p_{1}+p_{2})\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\left((\mu^{6}+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{2})d^{-8}+(\mu^{4}+\varepsilon\mu^{2})d^{-6}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+O\left((a_{1}+a_{2})(p_{1}+p_{2})(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\left(\mu^{5}(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{3}\right)d^{-11}\right).

Recall that
\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}
denotes the higher order expansions of
\displaystyle\delta_{\mu,\xi,a,p}
, in (
A.15
)-(
A.21
) and (
A.25
)-(
A.28
), we have estimated the corresponding terms that of second order expansion. Similar to the reasoning above, the remaining terms in

\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{1}\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\left[(\varphi_{1})_{x}\wedge(\varphi_{1})_{y}\right]

\displaystyle+2\varepsilon\int_{\mathcal{D}}g\cdot\left[\left(P\delta\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\delta\right)_{y}\right]+2\varepsilon^{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot(g_{x}\wedge g_{y})

can be estimated as

(A.29)

\displaystyle O\left(\sum_{k,l,m=1}^{2}\left(a_{k}a_{l}a_{m}+a_{k}a_{l}p_{m}+a_{k}p_{l}p_{m}+p_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\right)(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\left(1+(\mu^{4}+\varepsilon\mu^{2})d^{-6}+\mu(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)d^{-7}\right)\right).

Therefore, we get the estimate of
\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(1)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}
and
\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(2)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}
. Indeed, combining the fact that
\displaystyle|\xi|\to 1
,
\displaystyle d=d_{\varepsilon}=O(\mu^{\frac{2}{3}-\alpha})
for
\displaystyle\alpha>0
small, we conclude that

(A.30)

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(1)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=(\ref{M1p1})+(\ref{Jan4-2})+(\ref{Jan6-2})+(\ref{A30-a5})+(\ref{Jan4-3})

\displaystyle=O\left(\left(p_{1}+p_{2}\right)(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\left(\mu^{4}d^{-6}+(\mu^{8}+\varepsilon\mu^{6}+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{4})d^{-12}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+O\left((a_{1}+a_{2})(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\left(\mu d^{-3}+\left(\mu^{3}\left(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon\right)+\varepsilon^{2}\mu\right)d^{-9}\right)\right).

And

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(2)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=(\ref{Au11-6})+(\ref{D30-1})+(\ref{D28-3})+(\ref{Jan2-7})+(\ref{D28-1})+(\ref{Jan2-8})+(\ref{Jan2-9})+(\ref{D28-4})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+(\ref{D24-2})+(\ref{A30-a2})+(\ref{Jan2-10})+(\ref{Jan1-1})+(\ref{D31-4})+(\ref{F24-1})

\displaystyle\displaystyle=O\left(p_{1}p_{2}\mu^{10}d^{-10}+\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}p_{k}p_{l}(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\left(\mu^{6}(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)d^{-12}+(\mu^{4}+\varepsilon\mu^{2})d^{-6}+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{6}d^{-14}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(a_{1}a_{2}\mu^{4}d^{-4}+\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{k}a_{l}\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\left(\mu^{2}(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)d^{-8}++\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{4}d^{-12}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{k}p_{l}(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\left(\mu^{3}d^{-5}+\left(\mu^{5}(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)+\varepsilon^{2}\mu^{3}\right)d^{-11}\right)\right)

(A.31)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\sum_{k,l,m=1}^{2}\left(a_{k}a_{l}a_{m}+a_{k}a_{l}p_{m}+a_{k}p_{l}p_{m}+p_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\right)(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\left(1+(\mu^{4}+\varepsilon\mu^{2})d^{-6}+\mu(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon)d^{-7}\right)\right).

\displaystyle\bullet
The estimates of
\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(3)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}
and
\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(4)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}
.

Now, we estimate
\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(3)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}
(
A.4
). Integrating by parts, we can obtain

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(3)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}+\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{2}a_{k}\varphi_{-1,k}+p_{k}\varphi_{2,k}\right)\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}+\delta_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{2}a_{k}PZ_{-1,k}\right)\cdot\left[\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}PZ_{2,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{m=1}^{2}p_{m}PZ_{2,m}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{2}p_{k}PZ_{2,k}\right)\cdot\left[\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{l}PZ_{-1,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{k=1}^{2}a_{k}PZ_{-1,k}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{2}a_{k}PZ_{-1,k}\right)\cdot\left[\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{l}PZ_{-1,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{m=1}^{2}a_{m}PZ_{-1,m}\right)_{y}\right]

(A.32)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{2}p_{k}PZ_{2,k}\right)\cdot\left[\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}PZ_{2,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{m=1}^{2}p_{m}PZ_{2,m}\right)_{y}\right].

We start with the terms that contain
\displaystyle a_{k}p_{l}p_{m}
for
\displaystyle k,l,m=1,2
.
For the first term in (
A.32
), one has

\displaystyle\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi\cdot\left[\left(\sum_{k=1}^{2}a_{k}PZ_{-1,k}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l,m=1}^{2}p_{l}p_{m}PZ^{2,2}_{l,m}\right)_{y}+\left(\sum_{l,m=1}^{2}p_{l}p_{m}PZ^{2,2}_{l,m}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{k=1}^{2}a_{k}PZ_{-1,k}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\frac{8\pi}{3}a_{1}p^{2}_{1}\mu^{3}\left[\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{1}}{\partial x}-\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial y}\right](\xi,\xi)-\frac{8\pi}{3}a_{2}p^{2}_{1}\mu^{3}\left[\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{1}}{\partial y}+\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial x}\right](\xi,\xi)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-\frac{8\pi}{3}a_{1}p^{2}_{2}\mu^{3}\left[\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{1}}{\partial x}-\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial y}\right](\xi,\xi)+\frac{8\pi}{3}a_{2}p^{2}_{2}\mu^{3}\left[\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{1}}{\partial y}+\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial x}\right](\xi,\xi)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\frac{16\pi}{3}a_{1}p_{1}p_{2}\mu^{3}\left[\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{1}}{\partial y}+\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial x}\right](\xi,\xi)+\frac{16\pi}{3}a_{2}p_{1}p_{2}\mu^{3}\left[\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{1}}{\partial x}-\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial y}\right](\xi,\xi)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\sum_{k,l,m=1}^{2}a_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\left(\mu^{5}d^{-5}+\mu^{9}d^{-13}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=O\left(\sum_{k,l,m=1}^{2}a_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\left(\mu^{5}d^{-5}+\mu^{9}d^{-13}\right)\right),

where we use the fact that
\displaystyle\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{1}}{\partial x}-\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial y}=0
and
\displaystyle\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{1}}{\partial y}+\frac{\partial h^{(1)}_{2}}{\partial x}=0
.
And

\displaystyle\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{1}\cdot\left[\left(\sum_{m=1}^{2}p_{m}PZ_{2,m}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{k}p_{l}PZ^{-1,2}_{k,l}\right)_{y}+\left(\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{k}p_{l}PZ^{-1,2}_{k,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{m=1}^{2}p_{m}PZ_{2,m}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\frac{-16\pi}{3}a_{1}p_{1}^{2}\mu^{3}\left[\frac{{\partial}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x}(\xi,\xi)+3\frac{{\partial}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)\right]+\frac{16\pi}{3}a_{2}p_{1}^{2}\mu^{3}\left[\frac{{\partial}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)-3\frac{{\partial}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-\frac{16\pi}{3}a_{1}p_{2}^{2}\mu^{3}\left[3\frac{{\partial}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{{\partial}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)\right]+\frac{16\pi}{3}a_{2}p_{2}^{2}\mu^{3}\left[3\frac{{\partial}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)-\frac{{\partial}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\sum_{k,l,m=1}^{2}a_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\left(\mu^{5}d^{-5}+\mu^{9}d^{-13}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=O\left(\sum_{k,l,m=1}^{2}a_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\left(\mu^{3}d^{-3}+\mu^{9}d^{-13}\right)\right).

Thus,

\displaystyle\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[\left(\sum_{k=1}^{2}a_{k}PZ_{-1,k}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l,m=1}^{2}p_{l}p_{m}PZ^{2,2}_{l,m}\right)_{y}+\left(\sum_{l,m=1}^{2}p_{l}p_{m}PZ^{2,2}_{l,m}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{k=1}^{2}a_{k}PZ_{-1,k}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[\left(\sum_{m=1}^{2}p_{m}PZ_{2,m}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{k}p_{l}PZ^{-1,2}_{k,l}\right)_{y}+\left(\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{k}p_{l}PZ^{-1,2}_{k,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{m=1}^{2}p_{m}PZ_{2,m}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=O\left(\sum_{k,l,m=1}^{2}a_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\left(\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu d^{-3}+\mu\varepsilon d^{-4}|\xi-\omega|\right)\right).

And by (
1.10
), (
2.4
), (
B.17
), (
B.18
) and (
B.24
), it is direct to see that

\displaystyle\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{2}a_{k}PZ_{-1,k}\right)\cdot\left[\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}PZ_{2,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{m=1}^{2}p_{m}PZ_{2,m}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{2}a_{k}\varphi_{-1,k}\right)\cdot\left[\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}Z_{2,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{m=1}^{2}p_{m}Z_{2,m}\right)_{y}\right]+O\left(\sum_{k,l,m=1}^{2}a_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\left(\mu^{5}d^{-5}+\mu^{11}d^{-13}\right)\right).

Combining the corresponding terms that contain
\displaystyle a_{k}p_{l}p_{m}
,
\displaystyle k,l,m=1,2
in

-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{2}a_{k}\varphi_{-1,k}+p_{k}\varphi_{2,k}\right)\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}+\delta_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}\right],

and reasoning as (
A.16
), (
A.19
) and (
A.20
), we deduce

\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{2}a_{k}\varphi_{-1,k}\right)\cdot\left[\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}Z_{2,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{m=1}^{2}p_{m}Z_{2,m}\right)_{y}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\delta_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l,m=1}^{2}p_{l}p_{m}PZ^{2,2}_{l,m}\right)_{y}+\left(\sum_{l,m=1}^{2}p_{l}p_{m}PZ^{2,2}_{l,m}\right)_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\sum_{k,l,m=1}^{2}a_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\mu^{7}d^{-7}\right).

And

\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{2}p_{k}\varphi_{2,k}\right)\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l,m=1}^{2}a_{l}p_{m}PZ^{-1,2}_{l,m}\right)_{y}+\left(\sum_{l,m=1}^{2}a_{l}p_{m}PZ^{-1,2}_{l,m}\right)_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\sum_{k,l,m=1}^{2}a_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\mu^{5}d^{-5}\right).

Thus, combining the above estimates along with the fact that
\displaystyle\varepsilon d^{-1}|\xi-\omega|=O\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)
, we conclude that the terms in

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(3)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}
that contain
\displaystyle a_{k}p_{l}p_{m}
can be estimated as

\displaystyle O\left(\sum_{k,l,m=1}^{2}a_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\left(\mu^{5}d^{-5}+\mu^{11}d^{-13}+\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu d^{-3}\right)\right).

For the terms that contain
\displaystyle p_{k}p_{l}p_{m}
,
\displaystyle k,l,m=1,2
, there holds

\displaystyle\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\varphi_{1}\cdot\left[\left(\sum_{k=1}^{2}p_{k}PZ_{2,k}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l,m=1}^{2}p_{l}p_{m}PZ^{2,2}_{l,m}\right)_{y}+\left(\sum_{l,m=1}^{2}p_{l}p_{m}PZ^{2,2}_{l,m}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{k=1}^{2}p_{k}PZ_{2,k}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\frac{-\pi^{2}}{8}p_{1}^{3}\mu^{4}\left[\frac{{\partial}^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{{\partial}^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)\right]-\frac{\pi^{2}}{8}p_{1}^{2}p_{2}\mu^{4}\left[\frac{{\partial}^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{{\partial}^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\pi^{2}}{8}p_{1}p_{2}^{2}\mu^{4}\left[\frac{{\partial}^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{{\partial}^{2}h_{1}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)\right]-\frac{\pi^{2}}{8}p_{2}^{3}\mu^{4}\left[\frac{{\partial}^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{{\partial}^{2}h_{2}^{(1)}}{{\partial}y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\sum_{k,l,m=1}^{2}p_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\left(\mu^{6}d^{-6}+\mu^{14}d^{-16}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=O\left(\sum_{k,l,m=1}^{2}p_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\left(\mu^{6}d^{-6}+\mu^{14}d^{-16}\right)\right),

thus

\displaystyle\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\varphi_{1}-\varepsilon g\right)\cdot\left[\left(\sum_{k=1}^{2}p_{k}PZ_{2,k}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l,m=1}^{2}p_{l}p_{m}PZ^{2,2}_{l,m}\right)_{y}+\left(\sum_{l,m=1}^{2}p_{l}p_{m}PZ^{2,2}_{l,m}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{k=1}^{2}p_{k}PZ_{2,k}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=O\left(\sum_{k,l,m=1}^{2}p_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\left(\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu^{4}d^{-6}+\varepsilon\mu^{4}d^{-7}|\xi-\omega|\right)\right).

Moreover,

\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{2}p_{k}\varphi_{2,k}\right)\cdot\left[\delta_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l,m=1}^{2}p_{l}p_{m}PZ^{2,2}_{l,m}\right)_{y}+\left(\sum_{l,m=1}^{2}p_{l}p_{m}PZ^{2,2}_{l,m}\right)_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}\right]=O\left(\sum_{k,l,m=1}^{2}p_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\mu^{6}d^{-6}\right),

and

\displaystyle\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}PZ_{2,l}\right)\cdot\left[\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}PZ_{2,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}PZ_{2,l}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{32\pi}{9}p_{1}^{3}\mu^{6}\frac{{\partial}^{2}h_{2}^{(2,1)}}{{\partial}x{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{32\pi}{9}p_{1}p_{2}^{2}\mu^{6}\frac{{\partial}^{2}h_{2}^{(2,1)}}{{\partial}x{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)

\displaystyle+\frac{32\pi}{9}p_{2}p_{1}^{2}\mu^{6}\frac{{\partial}^{2}h_{2}^{(2,2)}}{{\partial}x{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)+\frac{32\pi}{9}p_{2}^{3}\mu^{6}\frac{{\partial}^{2}h_{2}^{(2,2)}}{{\partial}x{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)+O\left((p_{1}+p_{2})^{3}\left(\mu^{8}d^{-8}+\mu^{12}d^{-14}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=O\left((p_{1}+p_{2})^{3}\left(\mu^{6}d^{-6}+\mu^{12}d^{-14}\right)\right).

Thus, we conclude that the terms in

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(3)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}
that contain
\displaystyle p_{k}p_{l}p_{m}
can be estimated as

\displaystyle O\left(\sum_{k,l,m=1}^{2}p_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\left(\mu^{6}d^{-6}+\mu^{12}d^{-14}+\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu^{4}d^{-6}\right)\right).

For the remaining terms in

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(3)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}
, they can be estimated in a similar way. Omitting extensive and cumbersome calculations, we present the results here. There holds

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(3)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}=

\displaystyle\displaystyle~O\left(\sum_{k,l,m=1}^{2}a_{k}a_{l}a_{m}\left(\mu^{3}d^{-3}+\mu^{5}d^{-7}+\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\left(\mu d^{-3}+\mu^{3}d^{-7}+\mu^{3}\left(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon\right)d^{-9}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+O\left(\sum_{k,l,m=1}^{2}a_{k}a_{l}p_{m}\left(\mu^{2}d^{-2}+\mu^{14}d^{-16}+\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\left(\mu^{2}d^{-4}+\mu^{4}\left(\mu^{2}+\varepsilon\right)d^{-10}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+O\left(\sum_{k,l,m=1}^{2}a_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\left(\mu^{5}d^{-5}+\mu^{11}d^{-13}+\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu d^{-3}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+O\left(\sum_{k,l,m=1}^{2}p_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\left(\mu^{6}d^{-6}+\mu^{12}d^{-14}+\left(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon\right)\mu^{4}d^{-6}\right)\right)

(A.33)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+O\left(\left(a_{1}+a_{2}+p_{1}+p_{2}\right)^{4}\left(\mu d^{-1}+(\mu^{2}-\varepsilon)\mu^{2}d^{-6}\right)\right).

Now, we analyze the terms in
\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(4)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}
from (
A.5
). We begin by estimating the following terms

(A.34)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\nabla\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}\right].

Omitting the extensive and cumbersome calculations, we present the estimates for the terms in (
A.34
). Specifically, we have

(A.35)

\displaystyle\sum_{j,k=1}^{2}a_{j}^{2}a_{k}^{2}\left[\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left|\nabla(PZ^{-1,-1}_{j,k})\right|^{2}+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta\cdot\left[(PZ^{-1,-1}_{j,k})_{x}\wedge(PZ^{-1,-1}_{j,k})_{y}\right]\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{256\pi}{5}a_{1}^{2}a_{2}^{2}+O\left(\sum_{j,k=1}^{2}a_{j}^{2}a_{k}^{2}\left(\mu^{4}d^{-4}+\mu^{4}d^{-6}+\mu^{8}d^{-10}+\mu^{12}d^{-14}\right)\right).

For the term involving
\displaystyle a_{j}^{2}p_{k}^{2}
,
\displaystyle j,k=1,2
, there holds

(A.36)

\displaystyle\sum_{j,k=1}^{2}a_{j}^{2}p_{k}^{2}\left[\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left|\nabla(PZ^{-1,2}_{j,k})\right|^{2}+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta\cdot\left[(PZ^{-1,2}_{j,k})_{x}\wedge(PZ^{-1,2}_{j,k})_{y}\right]\right]

\displaystyle=6\pi^{2}\left(a_{1}^{2}p_{1}^{2}+a_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}+a_{2}^{2}p_{1}^{2}+a_{2}^{2}p_{2}^{2}\right)+O\left(\sum_{j,k=1}^{2}a_{j}^{2}p_{k}^{2}\left(\mu^{4}d^{-4}+\mu^{6}d^{-8}+\mu^{10}d^{-12}\right)\right).

We also consider the term with
\displaystyle p_{j}^{2}p_{k}^{2}
,
\displaystyle j,k=1,2
, we have

(A.37)

\displaystyle\sum_{j,k=1}^{2}p_{j}^{2}p_{k}^{2}\left[\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left|\nabla(PZ^{2,2}_{j,k})\right|^{2}+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta\cdot\left[(PZ^{2,2}_{j,k})_{x}\wedge(PZ^{2,2}_{j,k})_{y}\right]\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{64\pi}{5}p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}+O\left(\sum_{j,k=1}^{2}p_{j}^{2}p_{k}^{2}\left(\mu^{4}d^{-4}+\mu^{12}d^{-14}+\mu^{16}d^{-18}\right)\right).

For
\displaystyle(j,k)\neq(l,m)
and
\displaystyle(j,k)\neq(m,l)
, one has

(A.38)

\displaystyle\sum_{j,k,l,m=1}^{2}a_{j}a_{k}a_{l}a_{m}\left[\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla(PZ^{-1,-1}_{j,k})\cdot\nabla(PZ^{-1,-1}_{l,m})\right.

\displaystyle\left.+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta\cdot\left[(PZ^{-1,-1}_{j,k})_{x}\wedge(PZ^{-1,-1}_{l,m})_{y}+(PZ^{-1,-1}_{l,m})_{x}\wedge(PZ^{-1,-1}_{j,k})_{y}\right]\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{-256\pi}{5}a_{1}^{2}a_{2}^{2}+O\left(\sum_{j,k,l,m=1}^{2}a_{j}a_{k}a_{l}a_{m}\left(\mu^{4}d^{-4}+\mu^{4}d^{-6}+\mu^{8}d^{-10}+\mu^{12}d^{-14}\right)\right),

(A.39)

\displaystyle\sum_{j,k,l,m=1}^{2}a_{j}a_{k}a_{l}p_{m}\left[\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla(PZ^{-1,-1}_{j,k})\cdot\nabla(PZ^{-1,2}_{l,m})\right.

\displaystyle\left.+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta\cdot\left[(PZ^{-1,-1}_{j,k})_{x}\wedge(PZ^{-1,2}_{l,m})_{y}+(PZ^{-1,2}_{l,m})_{x}\wedge(PZ^{-1,-1}_{j,k})_{y}\right]\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\sum_{j,k,l,m=1}^{2}a_{j}a_{k}a_{l}p_{m}\left(\mu^{3}d^{-3}+\mu^{7}d^{-9}+\mu^{11}d^{-13}\right)\right),

(A.40)

\displaystyle\sum_{j,k,m,l=1}^{2}a_{j}a_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\left[\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla(PZ^{-1,-1}_{j,k})\cdot\nabla(PZ^{2,2}_{l,m})\right.

\displaystyle\left.+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta\cdot\left[(PZ^{-1,-1}_{j,k})_{x}\wedge(PZ^{2,2}_{l,m})_{y}+(PZ^{2,2}_{l,m})_{x}\wedge(PZ^{-1,-1}_{j,k})_{y}\right]\right]

\displaystyle=-6\pi^{2}\left(a_{1}^{2}p_{1}^{2}+a_{2}^{2}p_{2}^{2}+a_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}+a_{2}^{2}p_{1}^{2}\right)+O\left(\sum_{j,k,m,l=1}^{2}a_{j}a_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\left(\mu^{4}d^{-4}+\mu^{10}d^{-12}+\mu^{12}d^{-14}+\mu^{14}d^{-16}\right)\right),

(A.41)

\displaystyle\sum_{j,k,l,m=1}^{2}a_{j}p_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\left[\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla(PZ^{-1,2}_{j,k})\cdot\nabla(PZ^{2,2}_{l,m})+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta\cdot\left[(PZ^{-1,2}_{j,k})_{x}\wedge(PZ^{2,2}_{l,m})_{y}+(PZ^{2,2}_{l,m})_{x}\wedge(PZ^{-1,2}_{j,k})_{y}\right]\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\sum_{j,k,l,m=1}^{2}a_{j}p_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\left(\mu^{3}d^{-3}+\mu^{9}d^{-11}+\mu^{13}d^{-15}\right)\right),

and

(A.42)

\displaystyle\sum_{j,k,l,m=1}^{2}p_{j}p_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\left[\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla(PZ^{2,2}_{j,k})\cdot\nabla(PZ^{2,2}_{l,m})+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta\cdot\left[(PZ^{2,2}_{j,k})_{x}\wedge(PZ^{2,2}_{l,m})_{y}+(PZ^{2,2}_{l,m})_{x}\wedge(PZ^{2,2}_{j,k})_{y}\right]\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{64\pi}{5}p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}+O\left(\sum_{j,k,l,m=1}^{2}p_{j}p_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\left(\mu^{4}d^{-4}+\mu^{12}d^{-14}+\mu^{16}d^{-18}\right)\right).

We observe that the leading-order terms cancel out in the sum of (
A.35
)-(
A.42
).
The remaining terms in (
A.34
) can be estimated in a similar manner. We conclude that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\nabla\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\nabla\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}P\delta\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\left(a_{1}+a_{2}+p_{1}+p_{2}\right)^{3}\left(a_{1}+a_{2}\right)\left(\mu^{3}d^{-3}+\mu^{4}d^{-6}\right)+\left(p_{1}+p_{2}\right)^{4}\left(\mu^{4}d^{-4}+\mu^{12}d^{-14}\right)\right).

Next, we estimate
\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}\right]
. We start with the terms that contain
\displaystyle a_{k}^{2}p_{l}^{2}
,
\displaystyle k,l=1,2
. Indeed, there holds

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{k}a_{l}PZ^{-1,-1}_{k,l}\right)\cdot\left[\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}PZ_{2,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}PZ_{2,l}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=6\pi^{2}\left(a_{1}^{2}p_{1}^{2}+a_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}+a_{2}^{2}p_{1}^{2}+a_{2}^{2}p_{2}^{2}\right)+O\left(\sum_{j,k=1}^{2}a_{j}^{2}p_{k}^{2}\left(\mu^{4}d^{-4}+\mu^{10}d^{-12}+\mu^{14}d^{-16}\right)\right),

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}p_{k}p_{l}PZ^{2,2}_{k,l}\right)\cdot\left[\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{l}PZ_{-1,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{l}PZ_{-1,l}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=6\pi^{2}\left(a_{1}^{2}p_{1}^{2}+a_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}+a_{2}^{2}p_{1}^{2}+a_{2}^{2}p_{2}^{2}\right)+O\left(\sum_{j,k=1}^{2}a_{j}^{2}p_{k}^{2}\left(\mu^{2}d^{-2}+\mu^{10}d^{-12}\right)\right),

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}a_{k}p_{l}PZ^{-1,2}_{k,l}\right)\cdot\left[\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{l}PZ_{-1,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}PZ_{2,l}\right)_{y}+\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}PZ_{2,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}a_{l}PZ_{-1,l}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=-12\pi^{2}\left(a_{1}^{2}p_{1}^{2}+a_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}+a_{2}^{2}p_{1}^{2}+a_{2}^{2}p_{2}^{2}\right)+O\left(\sum_{j,k=1}^{2}a_{j}^{2}p_{k}^{2}\left(\mu^{2}d^{-2}+\mu^{10}d^{-12}\right)\right).

As for the terms that contain
\displaystyle p_{j}p_{k}p_{l}p_{m}
,
\displaystyle j,k,l,m=1,2
, there holds

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(\sum_{k,l=1}^{2}p_{k}p_{l}PZ^{2,2}_{k,l}\right)\cdot\left[\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}PZ_{2,l}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(\sum_{l=1}^{2}p_{l}PZ_{2,l}\right)_{y}\right]=O\left(\sum_{j,k,l,m=1}^{2}p_{j}p_{k}p_{l}p_{m}\left(\mu^{5}d^{-5}+\mu^{16}d^{-18}\right)\right).

The remaining terms in
\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}\right]
are of higher order. We can conclude that

\displaystyle 2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\left(a_{1}+a_{2}+p_{1}+p_{2}\right)^{3}\left(a_{1}+a_{2}\right)\left(\mu^{2}d^{-2}+\mu^{4}d^{-6}\right)+\left(p_{1}+p_{2}\right)^{4}\left(\mu^{5}d^{-5}+\mu^{16}d^{-18}\right)\right).

Finally, it is easy to check that

\displaystyle\frac{2}{3}\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}\right]+2\int_{\mathcal{D}}\left(P\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)\cdot\left[\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{x}\wedge\left(P\mathcal{R}_{\mathcal{A}}\right)_{y}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\left(a_{1}+a_{2}+p_{1}+p_{2}\right)^{5}\left(1+\mu^{7}d^{-9}\right)\right).

Thus,
\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(4)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}
can be estimated as

(A.43)

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{(4)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=O\left(\left(a_{1}+a_{2}+p_{1}+p_{2}\right)^{3}\left(a_{1}+a_{2}\right)\left(\mu^{2}d^{-2}+\mu^{4}d^{-6}\right)+\left(p_{1}+p_{2}\right)^{4}\left(\mu^{4}d^{-4}+\mu^{12}d^{-14}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+O\left(\left(a_{1}+a_{2}+p_{1}+p_{2}\right)^{5}\right).

The details are omitted here for brevity.

Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathcal{R}^{(1)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}+\mathcal{R}^{(2)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}+\mathcal{R}^{(3)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}+\mathcal{R}^{(4)}_{\mu,\xi,a,p,\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=(\ref{Jan8-4})+(\ref{Jan2-13})+(\ref{Jan2-3})+(\ref{Au12-2})

(A.44)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tilde{e}^{(2)}(\varepsilon,\mu,a,p),

where
\displaystyle\tilde{e}^{(2)}(\varepsilon,\mu,a,p)
is given in (
4.2
).
