\displaystyle\left(\left(5x^{8}+6\right)y^{4}+x^{8}+\left(10x^{4}-9\right)y^{8}+x^{4}+5x^{2}y^{10}+2x^{2}\left(5x^{4}-7\right)y^{6}+x^{2}\left(x^{8}+6x^{4}-13\right)y^{2}+y^{12}\right),

\displaystyle f_{7}(x,y)=

\displaystyle\left(2\left(5x^{4}-8\right)y^{6}+5x^{2}y^{8}+\left(5x^{8}-24x^{4}+15\right)y^{2}+2x^{2}\left(5x^{4}-18\right)y^{4}+x^{2}\left(x^{4}-5\right)\left(x^{4}+1\right)+y^{10}\right),

\displaystyle f_{8}(x,y)=

\displaystyle\left(21x^{4}-55\right)y^{10}+7x^{2}y^{12}+\left(35x^{8}-358x^{4}+115\right)y^{6}+x^{2}\left(35x^{4}-227\right)y^{8}

\displaystyle+\left(7x^{12}-83x^{8}+105x^{4}-21\right)y^{2}+x^{2}\left(21x^{8}-262x^{4}+225\right)y^{4}+x^{2}\left(x^{4}+1\right)\left(x^{8}-8x^{4}+3\right)+y^{14}

(B.27)

\displaystyle(Z_{-1,1})_{x}\wedge(Z_{-1,2})_{y}+(Z_{-1,2})_{x}\wedge(Z_{-1,1})_{y}+\delta_{x}\wedge(Z^{-1,-1}_{1,2})_{y}+(Z^{-1,-1}_{1,2})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{768xy(x-y)(x+y)\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(3\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}-5\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{5}}\\
\frac{-192\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)f_{9}(x,y)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{5}}\\
\frac{768xy\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(\left(2\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}-5\right)\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{5}}\end{pmatrix},

where

\displaystyle f_{9}(x,y)=

\displaystyle x^{12}+6x^{10}y^{2}+x^{8}\left(15y^{4}-2\right)+4x^{6}y^{2}\left(5y^{4}-8\right)+3x^{4}\left(5y^{8}-20y^{4}-1\right)+2x^{2}y^{2}\left(3y^{8}-16y^{4}+17\right)

\displaystyle+y^{4}\left(y^{4}-3\right)\left(y^{4}+1\right)

(B.28)

\displaystyle(Z_{2,1})_{x}\wedge(Z_{2,1})_{y}+\delta_{x}\wedge(Z^{2,2}_{1,1})_{y}+(Z^{2,2}_{1,1})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}-\frac{96(x-y)(x+y)\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(5x^{4}-22x^{2}y^{2}+5y^{4}-3\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{5}}\\
-\frac{192xy\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(7x^{4}-18x^{2}y^{2}+7y^{4}-1\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{5}}\\
-\frac{32\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(5x^{8}-4x^{6}y^{2}-x^{4}\left(18y^{4}+17\right)+x^{2}\left(38y^{2}-4y^{6}\right)+5y^{8}-17y^{4}+2\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{5}}\end{pmatrix},

(B.29)

\displaystyle(Z_{2,2})_{x}\wedge(Z_{2,2})_{y}+\delta_{x}\wedge(Z^{2,2}_{2,2})_{y}+(Z^{2,2}_{2,2})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{96(x-y)(x+y)\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(x^{4}-30x^{2}y^{2}+y^{4}+1\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{5}}\\
\frac{192xy\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(3x^{4}-26x^{2}y^{2}+3y^{4}+3\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{5}}\\
\frac{32\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(x^{8}-\left(42x^{4}+1\right)y^{4}-x^{4}-20x^{2}y^{6}+10\left(7-2x^{4}\right)x^{2}y^{2}+y^{8}-2\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{5}}\end{pmatrix},

(B.30)

\displaystyle(Z_{2,1})_{x}\wedge(Z_{2,2})_{y}+(Z_{2,2})_{x}\wedge(Z_{2,1})_{y}+\delta_{x}\wedge(Z^{2,2}_{1,2})_{y}+(Z^{2,2}_{1,2})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}-\frac{384xy\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(7x^{4}-18x^{2}y^{2}+7y^{4}-1\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{5}}\\
\frac{192(x-y)(x+y)\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(x^{4}-30x^{2}y^{2}+y^{4}+1\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{5}}\\
-\frac{768xy(x-y)(x+y)\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}-3\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{5}}\end{pmatrix},

(B.31)

\displaystyle(Z_{-1,1})_{x}\wedge(Z_{2,1})_{y}+(Z_{2,1})_{x}\wedge(Z_{-1,1})_{y}+\delta_{x}\wedge(Z^{-1,2}_{1,2})_{y}+(Z^{-1,2}_{1,2})_{x}\wedge\delta_{y}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}-\frac{192x\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(5x^{8}+4x^{6}y^{2}-10x^{4}\left(y^{4}+1\right)+4x^{2}y^{2}\left(7-3y^{4}\right)-3y^{4}\left(y^{4}+6\right)+1\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{5}}\\
\frac{384y\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(-5x^{8}-10x^{6}y^{2}+x^{4}\left(11-4y^{4}\right)+2x^{2}y^{2}\left(y^{4}-8\right)+y^{8}+y^{4}\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{5}}\\
-\frac{256x\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)\left(x^{10}+4x^{8}y^{2}+x^{6}\left(6y^{4}-7\right)+4x^{4}y^{2}\left(y^{4}-2\right)+x^{2}\left(y^{8}+5y^{4}+4\right)+6y^{2}\left(y^{4}-1\right)\right)}{\left(\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}+1\right)^{5}}\end{pmatrix}.

Appendix C
Proof of Lemma
2.2

\displaystyle\bullet
Evaluation of the Robin functions
\displaystyle h^{(-1,1)}
and
\displaystyle h^{(-1,2)}
.

For simplicity, we denote
\displaystyle h^{(-1,1)}_{1}(z,\xi)
and
\displaystyle h^{(-1,1)}_{2}(z,\xi)
as
\displaystyle h_{1}(z,\xi)
and
\displaystyle h_{2}(z,\xi)
, respectively, and set
\displaystyle h(z,\xi)=h_{1}(z,\xi)+ih_{2}(z,\xi)
, where
\displaystyle z=re^{i\theta}=x+iy
. Then, using the Poisson integral formula, we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle h(z,\xi)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\frac{-1}{\pi}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{1-r^{2}}{|e^{it}-re^{i\theta}|^{2}}\frac{e^{it}-\xi}{|e^{it}-\xi|^{2}}dt

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\frac{-1}{\pi}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{1-r^{2}}{|e^{it}-re^{i\theta}|^{2}}\frac{e^{it}-e^{i\theta_{0}}\xi_{0}}{|e^{it}-e^{i\theta_{0}}\xi_{0}|^{2}}dt

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\frac{-1}{\pi}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{1-r^{2}}{|e^{it}-re^{i(\theta-\theta_{0})}|^{2}}\frac{e^{i\theta_{0}}\left(e^{it}-\xi_{0}\right)}{|e^{it}-\xi_{0}|^{2}}dt

\displaystyle\displaystyle=e^{i\theta_{0}}v(e^{-i\theta_{0}}z,e^{-i\theta_{0}}\xi).

Here
\displaystyle e^{i\theta_{0}}=\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}+i\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}
,
\displaystyle\xi_{0}=(\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}},0)
and the function
\displaystyle v(z,\xi_{0})
is defined as

v(z,\xi_{0}):=\frac{-1}{\pi}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{1-r^{2}}{|e^{it}-re^{i\theta}|^{2}}\frac{e^{it}-\xi_{0}}{|e^{it}-\xi_{0}|^{2}}dt=v_{1}(z,\xi_{0})+iv_{2}(z,\xi_{0}).

Then we have

\displaystyle h_{1}(x,y,\xi_{1},\xi_{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}v_{1}\left(\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}x+\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}y,\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}y-\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}x,\sqrt{x_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}},0\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}v_{2}\left(\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}x+\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}y,\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}y-\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}x,\sqrt{x_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}},0\right)

and

\displaystyle h_{2}(x,y,\xi_{1},\xi_{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}v_{2}\left(\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}x+\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}y,\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}y-\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}x,\sqrt{x_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}},0\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}v_{1}\left(\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}x+\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}y,\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}y-\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}x,\sqrt{x_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}},0\right).

Therefore, it holds that

(C.1)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial x}h_{1}(\xi,\xi)

\displaystyle=\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x}v_{1}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})-\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial y}v_{1}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})

\displaystyle-\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x}v_{2}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})+\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial y}v_{2}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0}),

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial y}h_{1}(\xi,\xi)

\displaystyle=\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x}v_{1}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})+\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial y}v_{1}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})

\displaystyle-\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x}v_{2}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})-\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial y}v_{2}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0}),

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial x}h_{2}(\xi,\xi)

\displaystyle=\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x}v_{2}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})-\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial y}v_{2}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})

\displaystyle+\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x}v_{1}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})-\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial y}v_{1}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0}),

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial y}h_{2}(\xi,\xi)

\displaystyle=\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x}v_{2}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})+\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial y}v_{2}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})

\displaystyle+\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x}v_{1}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})+\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial y}v_{1}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0}).

Now differentiating under the integral sign, we can compute
\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial x}v_{1}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})
,
\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial y}v_{1}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})
,
\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial x}v_{2}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})
,
\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial y}v_{2}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})
directly as follows,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial x}v_{1}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{-1}{\pi}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\left(\frac{1-x^{2}-y^{2}}{1+x^{2}+y^{2}-2x\cos t-2y\sin t}\right)\frac{\cos t-\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}{(\cos t-\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}})^{2}+(\sin t)^{2}}dt\Bigg|_{x=\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}},y=0}

\displaystyle=\frac{-2}{(1-|\xi|^{2})^{2}},

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial y}v_{1}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{-1}{\pi}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{\partial}{\partial y}\left(\frac{1-x^{2}-y^{2}}{1+x^{2}+y^{2}-2x\cos t-2y\sin t}\right)\frac{\cos t-\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}{(\cos t-\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}})^{2}+(\sin t)^{2}}dt\Bigg|_{x=\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}},y=0}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial x}v_{2}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{-1}{\pi}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\left(\frac{1-x^{2}-y^{2}}{1+x^{2}+y^{2}-2x\cos t-2y\sin t}\right)\frac{\sin t}{(\cos t-\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}})^{2}+(\sin t)^{2}}dt\Bigg|_{x=\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}},y=0}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial y}v_{2}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{-1}{\pi}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{\partial}{\partial y}\left(\frac{1-x^{2}-y^{2}}{1+x^{2}+y^{2}-2x\cos t-2y\sin t}\right)\frac{\sin t}{(\cos t-\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}})^{2}+(\sin t)^{2}}dt\Bigg|_{x=\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}},y=0}

\displaystyle=\frac{-2}{(1-|\xi|^{2})^{2}}.

Using (
C.1
), we obtain

\displaystyle h_{1}^{(-1,1)}(\xi,\xi)=\frac{-2\xi_{1}}{1-|\xi|^{2}},\quad h_{2}^{(-1,1)}(\xi,\xi)=\frac{-2\xi_{2}}{1-|\xi|^{2}},

\displaystyle\frac{\partial h_{1}^{(-1,1)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)=\frac{\partial h_{2}^{(-1,1)}}{\partial y}(\xi,\xi)=\frac{-2}{(1-|\xi|^{2})^{2}},\qquad\frac{\partial h_{1}^{(-1,1)}}{\partial y}(\xi,\xi)=-\frac{\partial h_{2}^{(-1,1)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)=0,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(-1,1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)=-\frac{\partial^{2}h_{1}^{(-1,1)}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)=-\frac{4\xi_{1}}{(1-|\xi|^{2})^{3}},\quad\ \frac{\partial^{2}h_{2}^{(-1,1)}}{\partial x^{2}}(\xi,\xi)=-\frac{\partial^{2}h_{2}^{(-1,1)}}{\partial y^{2}}(\xi,\xi)=\frac{4\xi_{2}}{(1-|\xi|^{2})^{3}},

Note that
\displaystyle h_{1}^{(-1,1)}(z,\xi)=h_{2}^{(-1,2)}(z,\xi)
and
\displaystyle h_{2}^{(-1,1)}(z,\xi)=-h_{1}^{(-1,2)}(z,\xi)
, the corresponding values of
\displaystyle h^{(-1,2)}
in Lemma
2.2
can be directly obtained.

\displaystyle\bullet
Evaluation of the Robin function
\displaystyle h^{(1)}
.

Similar to the computation of
\displaystyle h^{(-1,l)}
, we denote
\displaystyle h_{1}^{(1)}(z,\xi)
and
\displaystyle h_{2}^{(1)}(z,\xi)
as
\displaystyle h_{1}(z,\xi)
and
\displaystyle h_{2}(z,\xi)
, respectively, and set
\displaystyle h(z,\xi)=h_{1}(z,\xi)+ih_{2}(z,\xi)
. By the Poisson integral formula, we have

\displaystyle h(z,\xi)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{1-r^{2}}{|e^{it}-re^{i\theta}|^{2}}\frac{\left(e^{it}-\xi\right)^{2}}{|e^{it}-\xi|^{4}}dt

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{1-r^{2}}{|e^{it}-re^{i\theta}|^{2}}\frac{\left(e^{it}-e^{i\theta_{0}}\xi_{0}\right)^{2}}{|e^{it}-e^{i\theta_{0}}\xi_{0}|^{4}}dt

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{1-r^{2}}{|e^{it}-re^{i(\theta-\theta_{0})}|^{2}}\frac{e^{i2\theta_{0}}\left(e^{it}-\xi_{0}\right)^{2}}{|e^{it}-\xi_{0}|^{4}}dt

\displaystyle=e^{i2\theta_{0}}v(e^{-i\theta_{0}}z,e^{-i\theta_{0}}\xi).

Here
\displaystyle e^{i\theta_{0}}=\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}+i\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}
,

\displaystyle\xi_{0}=(\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}},0)
and the function
\displaystyle v(z,\xi_{0})
is defined as

v(z,\xi_{0}):=\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{1-r^{2}}{|e^{it}-re^{i\theta}|^{2}}\frac{\left(e^{it}-\xi_{0}\right)^{2}}{|e^{it}-\xi_{0}|^{4}}dt=v_{1}(z,\xi_{0})+iv_{2}(z,\xi_{0}).

Then we have

\displaystyle h_{1}(x,y,\xi_{1},\xi_{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{\xi_{1}^{2}-\xi_{2}^{2}}{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}v_{1}\left(\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}x+\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}y,\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}y-\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}x,\sqrt{x_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}},0\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2\xi_{1}\xi_{2}}{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}v_{2}\left(\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}x+\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}y,\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}y-\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}x,\sqrt{x_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}},0\right),

and

\displaystyle h_{2}(x,y,\xi_{1},\xi_{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{\xi_{1}^{2}-\xi_{2}^{2}}{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}v_{2}\left(\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}x+\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}y,\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}y-\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}x,\sqrt{x_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}},0\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\xi_{1}\xi_{2}}{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}v_{1}\left(\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}x+\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}y,\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}y-\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}x,\sqrt{x_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}},0\right).

Thus

(C.2)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial x}h_{1}(\xi,\xi)

\displaystyle=\frac{\xi_{1}^{2}-\xi_{2}^{2}}{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x}v_{1}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})-\frac{\xi_{1}^{2}-\xi_{2}^{2}}{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial y}v_{1}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})

\displaystyle-\frac{2\xi_{1}\xi_{2}}{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x}v_{2}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})+\frac{2\xi_{1}\xi_{2}}{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial y}v_{2}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0}),

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial y}h_{1}(\xi,\xi)

\displaystyle=\frac{\xi_{1}^{2}-\xi_{2}^{2}}{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x}v_{1}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})+\frac{\xi_{1}^{2}-\xi_{2}^{2}}{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial y}v_{1}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})

\displaystyle-\frac{2\xi_{1}\xi_{2}}{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x}v_{2}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})-\frac{2\xi_{1}\xi_{2}}{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial y}v_{2}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0}),

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial x}h_{2}(\xi,\xi)

\displaystyle=\frac{\xi_{1}^{2}-\xi_{2}^{2}}{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x}v_{2}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})-\frac{\xi_{1}^{2}-\xi_{2}^{2}}{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial y}v_{2}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})

\displaystyle+\frac{2\xi_{1}\xi_{2}}{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x}v_{1}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})-\frac{2\xi_{1}\xi_{2}}{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial y}v_{1}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0}),

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial y}h_{2}(\xi,\xi)

\displaystyle=\frac{\xi_{1}^{2}-\xi_{2}^{2}}{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x}v_{2}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})+\frac{\xi_{1}^{2}-\xi_{2}^{2}}{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial y}v_{2}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})

\displaystyle+\frac{2\xi_{1}\xi_{2}}{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial x}v_{1}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})+\frac{2\xi_{1}\xi_{2}}{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}\frac{\partial}{\partial y}v_{1}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0}).

By differentiating under the integral sign, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial x}v_{1}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\left(\frac{1-x^{2}-y^{2}}{1+x^{2}+y^{2}-2x\cos t-2y\sin t}\right)\frac{(\cos t-\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}})^{2}-(\sin t)^{2}}{((\cos t-\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}})^{2}+(\sin t)^{2})^{2}}dt\Bigg|_{x=\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}},y=0}

\displaystyle=\frac{2|\xi|}{(1-|\xi|^{2})^{3}},

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial y}v_{1}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{\partial}{\partial y}\left(\frac{1-x^{2}-y^{2}}{1+x^{2}+y^{2}-2x\cos t-2y\sin t}\right)\frac{(\cos t-\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}})^{2}-(\sin t)^{2}}{((\cos t-\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}})^{2}+(\sin t)^{2})^{2}}dt\Bigg|_{x=\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}},y=0}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial x}v_{2}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\left(\frac{1-x^{2}-y^{2}}{1+x^{2}+y^{2}-2x\cos t-2y\sin t}\right)\frac{2(\cos t-\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}})(\sin t)}{((\cos t-\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}})^{2}+(\sin t)^{2})^{2}}dt\Bigg|_{x=\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}},y=0}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial y}v_{2}(\xi_{0},\xi_{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{\partial}{\partial y}\left(\frac{1-x^{2}-y^{2}}{1+x^{2}+y^{2}-2x\cos t-2y\sin t}\right)\frac{2(\cos t-\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}})(\sin t)}{((\cos t-\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}})^{2}+(\sin t)^{2})^{2}}dt\Bigg|_{x=\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}},y=0}

\displaystyle=\frac{2|\xi|}{(1-|\xi|^{2})^{3}}.

Therefore, using (
C.2
), we obtain the following evaluations at the point
\displaystyle\xi
,

\frac{\partial h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)=\frac{2\xi_{1}}{(1-|\xi|^{2})^{3}},\quad\frac{\partial h_{1}^{(1)}}{\partial y}(\xi,\xi)=-\frac{2\xi_{2}}{(1-|\xi|^{2})^{3}},

\frac{\partial h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial x}(\xi,\xi)=\frac{2\xi_{2}}{(1-|\xi|^{2})^{3}},\quad\frac{\partial h_{2}^{(1)}}{\partial y}(\xi,\xi)=\frac{2\xi_{1}}{(1-|\xi|^{2})^{3}}.

The computations of the other terms in Lemma
2.2
are similar, we omit the details here.

\displaystyle\bullet
Evaluation of the Robin functions
\displaystyle h^{(2,l)}
.

Similarly, we denote
\displaystyle h_{1}^{(2,1)}(z,\xi)
and
\displaystyle h_{2}^{(2,1)}(z,\xi)
as
\displaystyle h_{1}(z,\xi)
and
\displaystyle h_{2}(z,\xi)
, respectively and set
\displaystyle h(z,\xi)=h_{1}(z,\xi)+ih_{2}(z,\xi)
. From the Poisson integral formula, we have

\displaystyle h(z,\xi)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{1-r^{2}}{|e^{it}-re^{i\theta}|^{2}}\frac{\left(e^{it}-\xi\right)^{4}}{|e^{it}-\xi|^{8}}dt

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{1-r^{2}}{|e^{it}-re^{i\theta}|^{2}}\frac{\left(e^{it}-e^{i\theta_{0}}\xi_{0}\right)^{4}}{|e^{it}-e^{i\theta_{0}}\xi_{0}|^{8}}dt

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{1-r^{2}}{|e^{it}-re^{i(\theta-\theta_{0})}|^{2}}\frac{e^{i4\theta_{0}}\left(e^{it}-\xi_{0}\right)^{4}}{|e^{it}-\xi_{0}|^{8}}dt

\displaystyle=-e^{i4\theta_{0}}v(e^{-i\theta_{0}}z,e^{-i\theta_{0}}\xi).

Here
\displaystyle e^{i\theta_{0}}=\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}+i\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}
,

\displaystyle\xi_{0}=(\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}},0)
and the function
\displaystyle v(z,\xi_{0})
is defined as

v(z,\xi_{0}):=\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\frac{1-r^{2}}{|e^{it}-re^{i\theta}|^{2}}\frac{\left(e^{it}-\xi_{0}\right)^{4}}{|e^{it}-\xi_{0}|^{8}}dt=v_{1}(z,\xi_{0})+iv_{2}(z,\xi_{0}).

Then we have

\displaystyle h_{1}(x,y,\xi_{1},\xi_{2})

\displaystyle=-\frac{\xi_{1}^{4}-6\xi_{1}^{2}\xi_{2}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{6}}{(\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2})^{2}}v_{1}\left(\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}x+\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}y,\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}y-\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}x,\sqrt{x_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}},0\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{4\xi_{1}\xi_{2}(\xi_{1}^{2}-\xi_{2}^{2})}{(\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2})^{2}}v_{2}\left(\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}x+\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}y,\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}y-\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}x,\sqrt{x_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}},0\right),

and

\displaystyle h_{2}(x,y,\xi_{1},\xi_{2})

\displaystyle=-\frac{\xi_{1}^{4}-6\xi_{1}^{2}\xi_{2}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{6}}{(\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2})^{2}}v_{2}\left(\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}x+\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}y,\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}y-\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}x,\sqrt{x_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}},0\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{4\xi_{1}\xi_{2}(\xi_{1}^{2}-\xi_{2}^{2})}{(\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2})^{2}}v_{1}\left(\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}x+\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}y,\frac{\xi_{1}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}y-\frac{\xi_{2}}{\sqrt{\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}}}x,\sqrt{x_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}},0\right).

Similar to (
C.1
) and (
C.2
), by direct computation, we get

\displaystyle\frac{{\partial}h_{1}^{(2,1)}}{{\partial}x}(\xi,\xi)=-\frac{4\xi_{1}\left(\xi_{1}^{2}-3\xi_{2}^{2}\right)}{\left(1-|\xi|^{2}\right)^{5}},\qquad\frac{{\partial}h_{1}^{(2,1)}}{{\partial}y}(\xi,\xi)=-\frac{4\xi_{2}\left(-3\xi_{1}^{2}+\xi_{2}^{2}\right)}{\left(1-|\xi|^{2}\right)^{5}}

\displaystyle\frac{{\partial}^{4}h_{1}^{(2,1)}}{{\partial}x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)=-\frac{24\left(35+4\left(|\xi|^{2}-1\right)^{3}+30\left(|\xi|^{2}-1\right)^{2}+60\left(|\xi|^{2}-1\right)\right)}{\left(1-|\xi|^{2}\right)^{8}}=\frac{{\partial}h_{1}^{(2,1)}}{{\partial}y^{4}}(\xi,\xi),

\displaystyle\frac{{\partial}^{4}h_{2}^{(2,1)}}{{\partial}x^{4}}(\xi,\xi)=0=\frac{{\partial}^{4}h_{2}^{(2,1)}}{{\partial}x^{4}}(\xi,\xi).

Since
\displaystyle h_{1}^{(2,1)}(z,\xi)=-h_{2}^{(2,2)}(z,\xi)
,
\displaystyle h_{2}^{(2,1)}(z,\xi)=h_{1}^{(2,2)}(z,\xi)
, we can obtain the corresponding values of
\displaystyle h_{1}^{(2,2)}
in Lemma
2.2
.
